moḻi mīṭpiṉ mīḷ varavu
taṉit  tamiḻ vittai ūṉṟiyavar kālṭuvelār. atai muḷaikkac ceytavar paritimāl kalaiñar; ceṭiyāka vaḷarttavar niṟaimalaiyām maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ; maramāka vaḷarttu varupavaṉ yāṉē eṉṟa vīṟuṭaiyār pāvāṇar.
kiṟittu perumāṉ camaya mīṭpar; kāralmārkkacu poruḷiyal mīṭpar; moḻimīṭpar yāṉē eṉṉum perumita mikkār pāvāṇar.
āriyattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻai mīṭpataṟkāka yāṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa muṟaiyōṭu kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ muppatu  eṉṟu eḻutiya perumitat tōṉṟal pāvāṇar.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē; avaṉ pēciya moḻiyē ulaka mutaṉmoḻi; āriyattiṟku mūlamum, tiraviṭattukkut tāyum tamiḻē eṉṉum mummaṇik koḷkaiḷai nilai nāṭṭiya malaiyaṉṉa māṇpar pāvāṇar.
avar colliyavai eḻutiyavai aṉaittum meymmaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉavē eṉa iṉṟu ulaka āyvup perumakkaḷāl ovvoṉṟāka meyppikkappaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭait tam āyvu matukaiyāl tēvanēya ūḻi ākkiya pukaḻum vēṇṭāp pukaḻ māmaṇi tēvanēyap pāvaṇar.
avar moḻiyāyvuc ceytikaḷ oru nūlil, ōr itaḻil, oru malaril, orukaṭṭuraiyil, oru kaṭitattil, oru poḻivil ōr uraiyāṭalil aṭaṅkiyavai alla. koṅku tēr vāḻkkai añciṟait tumpiyāka avaṟṟai yellām tokuttu akara niralil tokukkappaṭṭa ariya tokuppē tēvanēyam ākum.
neṭṭa neṭuṅkālamākat tēvanēyattil ūṉṟiya yāṉ ataṉai akara niral tokaiyākki veḷiyiṭal tamiḻulakukkup perumpayaṉām eṉṟu eṇṇiya kālaiyil, tamiḻ moḻiyaiyum tamiḻ maṇṇaiyum tamiḻ iṉattaiyum tāṅkip piṭittu ūkkum - vaḷarkkum - vaṇmaiyarāy - pāvāṇarkku aṇukkarāy - avarāl uraiyum pāṭṭum oruṅku koṇṭa peruntoṇṭarāyt  tikaḻnta ciṅka purivāḻ tamiḻttiru ve. kōvalaṅ kaṇṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamiḻ viḻā oṉṟaṟkākac ceṉṉai vanta pōtu yāṉum,  muṉaivar ku. tirumāṟaṉārum cantittu aḷavaḷāviya pōtu ikkaruttai yāṉ uraikka uṭaṉē pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai tōṟṟuvippatākavum ataṉ vaḻiyē tēvanēyam veḷik koṇaralām eṉavum kūṟi appoḻutēyē aṟakkaṭṭaḷai amaittār.
tēvanēyar paṭaippukaḷ aṉaittilum uḷḷa collāyvukaḷait tiraṭṭi akara niral paṭuttip patiṉmūṉṟu tokutikaḷ ākkiṉēṉ. patippuk kuḻuvum amaikkappaṭṭatu; acciṭum poṟuppu, pāvāṇar pala kālattup palavakaiyāl veḷiyiṭṭa nūlkaḷaiyum kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyum oruṅkē tokuttu orē nērattil veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷar nāṭu moḻi iṉap pōrāḷi kō. iḷavaḻakaṉāriṭam oppaṭaikkappaṭṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. attēvanēyam tamiḻ āyvar, tamiḻ mīṭpar aṉaivar kaikaḷilum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṉum vēṇavāvāl mīḷpatippākat tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai niṟuviya kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār pukaḻ uṭal eytiya nilaiyil, avar eṉṟum iṟavā vāḻviṉar eṉpatai nilaip paṭuttum vakaiyil avarkkup paṭaiyalākki ippatippu veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu.
moḻi iṉa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar aṉaivariṭamum irukka vēṇṭiya nūl, pal patippukaḷ kāṇa vēṇṭum. varuṅkāla iḷaiñarkku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷiviḷakkākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum. ataṟkut tūṇṭalum tulakkalumāka irukka vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṟṟumaiyarum toṇṭaru māvar.
veḷiyīṭṭāḷarkkum parappunarkkum perunaṉṟiyuṭaiyēṉ.
vāḻiya nalaṉē!								iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
vāḻiya nilaṉē!							    irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


patippurai
20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya  iṇaiyaṟṟat tamiḻp pēraṟiñar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar. ivar vaṭamoḻi ātikkattiliruntu tamiḻai mīṭṭeṭuppataṟkākat tam vāḻviṉ muḻup poḻutaiyum celaviṭṭavar.
tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻit tamiḻ, intiya moḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻit tamiḻ, ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi tamiḻ eṉpatait tam paṉmoḻip pulamaiyāl ulakukku aṟivittavar.
ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ aṉaittaiyum tokuttu oru cēra tamiḻmaṇ patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatait tamiḻ ulakam aṟiyum. 
mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñar. vāḻum tamiḻukku vaḷam pala cērppavar. pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷai yellām tēvanēyam eṉṉum talaippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉkoḷḷum vakaiyil tokuttut tantuḷḷār. ittēvanēyat tokuppukaḷ tamiḻar kaḷukkuk kiṭaitta vairaccuraṅkam. it tokuppukaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatil perumaip paṭukiṟōm.
aṟiñarulakamum, āyvulakamum ivvaruntamiḻk karuvūlattai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
- patippāḷar
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ

poruḷātāram
nāṭṭaiyāḷvataṟkum pōr ceytaṟkum kuṭikaṭku naṉmai ceytaṟkum aracaṉukkup poruḷvēṇṭum. apporuḷ iṭaiviṭātu vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, atu varum vaḻikaḷum nilaiyāṉavaiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
araciyal varuvāykaḷ: mūvēntarkku mirunta poruḷ varuvāykaḷ, vari, iyaṟkaic celvam. tiṟai, putaiyal, piṟaṅkaṭai (vāricu) illāc cottu, kaiyuṟai, naṉkoṭai eṉa eḻutiṟattaṉa.
iyaṟkaic celvam yāṉaiyum muttum poṉṉum maṇiyum pōlvaṉa. kaiyuṟai kuṉṟakkuṟavar cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉukkuk kaṇṇakiyaip paṟṟik kūṟiṉapōtu koṭutta malaip poruḷkaḷ pōlvaṉa. (cilap. 25 : 37-54). teyvattirumuṉ kaiyuṟaiyōṭu celvatu pōṉṟu aracattirumuṉṉum celvatu marapu. naṉkoṭai karikālvaḷavaṉukku.
avanti vēntaṉ uvantaṉaṉ koṭutta
 nivantōṅku marapiṉ tōraṇa vāyil  (cilap. 5 : 103-4).
pōlvatu. piṟa veḷippaṭai.
cēraṉukku yāṉaiyum poṉṉum maṇiyum, cōḻaṉukkup poṉṉum vayiramum, pāṇṭiyaṉukku yāṉaiyum, muttum, ciṟanta iyaṟkaic celvaṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa.
veṭcik kāṉattu vēṭṭuva rāṭṭak
 kaṭci kāṇāk kaṭamā nallēṟu
 kaṭaṟumaṇi kiḷarac citaṟupoṉ miḷirak
 kaṭiya kataḻu neṭuvaraip paṭappai
 ....................................................................
 kōṭipala vaṭukkiya poruṇumak kutaviya
 nīṭunilai yaraiyattu...................................................  (puṟam. 202)
eṉṟu kapilar pāṭiyiruppatāl, koṅkunāṭu poṉṉukkum maṇikkum ciṟantiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum. koṅkunāṭṭiṟ piṟantu neṭuntolaivu ataṉvaḻiyē cellum kāviriyāṟu poṉṉi yeṉappaṭṭatum, tamiḻ nāṭṭup poṉ koṅkup poṉ eṉak cuṭṭappeṟṟatum, kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. koṅkunāṭu muṟkālattiṟ cēraṉukkum piṟkālattiṟ cōḻaṉukkum uriyatāyiruntatu.
cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭavellaip puṟattiluḷḷa kiruṭṭiṇai yāṟṟaṅkarai yilum, ataṉ kiḷaiyāṉa tuṅkapattiraik karaiyi lum, vayirac curaṅkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ulaka oḷimaṇiyāṉa kōki ṉūr vayiram kiruṭṭiṇai yāṟṟaṅ karaiyil eṭukkap peṟṟatē.
pāṇṭināṭṭil vacciranāṭu eṉap peyarpeṟṟa oru kaṭaṟkarai nāṭu iruntatākat kaḷaviyaṟkārikai eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟaivakai : araciyal varuvāykaḷuḷ nilaiyāṉatum mutaṉmai yāṉatum kuṭikaḷ celuttum variyē. atu, nilavari, nīrvari, maṉai vari, toḻilvari, āḷvari (Poll tax) vilaṅkuvari, poruḷvari, uyppuvari, maṇavari, taṇṭavari, kōyilvari, koṭaivari, ūḻiyappēṟṟuvari, paṭaivari, kāṭcivari, karuvūlavari, kācaṭip puvari eṉap palatiṟattatu.
nīrvari, viḷainilaṅkaṭku nīrnilaikaḷiṉiṉṟu nīraip pāyccik koḷ vataṟkuc celuttumvari, atu ērikuḷap pāyccalukkuriya tāyiṉ nilainīrp pāṭṭam eṉṟum, āṟṟup pāyccaṟkuriyatāyiṉ oḻukunīrppāṭṭam eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. iṉi, naṉṉīr puṉṉīr eṉavum vēṟupāṭuṇṭu. toḻilvari, uḻavoḻinta piṟa toḻilkaṭku vāṅkappaṭuvatu. vilaṅkuvari, āṭu peṟṟam (pacu) erutu erumai mutaliya vilaṅkukaṭku vāṅkappaṭuvatu. uyppu vari, nilattilum nīrilum poruḷkaḷai uyttaṟkuc celuttuvatu. atu āyam ulku cuṅkam eṉṉum peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. maṇavari tirumaṇattiṟku vitikkappaṭuvatu, atu araikkāl paṇamākum. taṇṭavari, kuṟṟavāḷikaṭkut taṇṭamāka vitikkap paṭuvatu. koṭaivari, aracaṉ antaṇarkkuc ceyyum peruṅ koṭaikaṭkut taṇṭappaṭuvatu. ūḻiyappēṟṟuvari, ūravai yār pāṭi kāvalaṉ mutaliyavariṉ campaḷattiṟkākat taṇṭappaṭu vatu. pāṭi kāvalaṉ campaḷattai avaṉē taṇṭik koḷḷa uttaraviruntatu. avaṉ campaḷam, naṉcey viḷaivil māviṟku oru kalamum. puṉcey viḷaivil māviṟku oru paṇamum, kamukiṟku maram oṉṟukku vīcam paṇamum, karumpu marukkoḻuntu iñci eḷ vāḻai ākiya veḷḷāṇmai yil māviṟku aintu paṇamum, vīṭṭiṟku āṇṭukku iraṇṭu paṇamum, ākum.
variyāṉatu avvattoḻiṟkēṟpa āyam, iṟai, kaṭaṉ, kaṭamai, kaṟai, kāṇikkai, tīrvai, pakuti, pāṭṭam, pūṭci, pēṟu, makamai, makaṉmai eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟatu. avaṟṟukkellām vari eṉpatu potuppeyarākum. ūrttalaivaṉ allatu ūravaiyār vitikkum vari ūriṭuvarippāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
vāṅkappaṭṭa varikaḷ : akkālattuk kuṭikaḷiṭattiṟ palavakai yilum vāṅkappaṭṭa varikaḷāvaṉa : aṅkāṭip pāṭṭam (aṅ kāṭikkūli), accuvari, aṭṭuk kiṟai, aṭikācu, aṭimaikkācu, aṇiyiṭuvāṉ vari, atikaraṇattaṇṭam, atikārap pēṟu (atikārappoṉ), arikoḻi, ari naṭṭi, arivāṭpatakku, aruntōṭu, araikkālvāci, aḻakerutu, aḻakerutuk kāṭcikkācu (kāṭciyerutu kācu) aḻukaṟcarakku, aḷḷukkācu, aḷiyiṭuvāṉvari, aṉuppu, ācukavikaḷ kācu (ācīvakkācu), ācavakkaṭamai āṭṭaiccammātam (āṭṭaikkāṇikkai), āṇṭeḻuttuttēvai, āttiṟaip pāṭṭam, ātiraippiḷḷai yār nōṉpu, āḷnel, āṟṟukkulai, āṟṟuppāṭṭam, iṭaippāṭṭam (iṭaippūṭci, iṭaivari), irācākaraṅ kāṇikkai, irupatakkaṭṭi, ilāñciṉaippēṟu, iṟaikāval, iṟaiccōṟu, iṉavari (iṉakkācu), īḻappuṉ cey (īḻampūṭci), īḻaṟkaṭivari, ukappārpoṉ (ukavaippoṉ), uppāyam (uppukkācu, uppukōc ceykai), upayam (upayamārkkam), uralvari, ulliyakkūli, ulāvu kāṭci, uvaccavari, uḻutāṉkuṭi, uḷvari, uṟupātai, uṟuvukōl, nilaṉkācu, ūcivāci, ūttaip pāṭṭam, ūrkkaṇakkar cīvitam (ūrkkalaṉai, ūrk kaḻañcu), ūrccarikai, ūrāṭci, ūriṭuvarippāṭu, ūreṭṭu, eṭkaṭamai (ekkaṭamai), eṭuttukkoṭṭi, eṭaivari, erumaip poṉ, eḻuvai, ēṇikkāṇam, ērk kāṭi (ērppoṉ, ērk kāṇikkai). ēriyāyam, ēlvai, oṭṭitaṟ kaṭamai, ōṭṭaccu, ōṭakkūli, kaṭaiyaṭaikkāy, kaṭaiyiṟai, kaṇkāṇi, kaṇkāṇi kaṇakkar mutal, kaṇkūli, kaṇṇālakkāṇam (kaliyāṇak kāṇam), kaṇṇiṭṭukkāṇam (kaṇṇēṭṭuk kāṇam), kaṇakkappēṟu, (kaṇakkilakkai), katirkkāṇam, karuvūlavari, kāṭṭāḷ kācu, kāṇavaṭṭam, kāṇiveṭṭi, kāriyavārāycci, kālattēvai, kāval parappu (kāvaṟ pēṟu), kīḻvārap paccai, kucakkāṇam, kuṭanāḻi, kuṭimai (kuṭikkācu, kuṭikkāṇam, kuṭiyiṟai), kutirai iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa varikaḷellām, ellāk kālattum ellāviṭattum ellāriṭattum vāṅkappaṭṭavaiyalla. vēṟu vari kuṟippaṉapōl tōṉṟum pala peyarkaḷ orē vari kuṟippaṉavākum. ikkālattut toḻil variyeṉappaṭum oṉṟē, akkālattut toḻil toṟum karuvitoṟum vevvēṟu peyarpeṟṟu nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa varipōl tōṉṟiṟṟu. āyiṉum, caṅka kālattu varikaḷiṉum piṟkālattu varikaḷ mikap pala veṉṟum, ikkālattilillāta āḷvari maṇavari tulāpāra vari mutaliya cila vitappu varikaḷ akkālattiruntaṉa veṉṟum, aṟitalvēṇṭum. āḷvari cōḻa nāṭṭil muṉṉiyūr tavirap piṟavūrkaḷil kūṉar kuṟaḷariṭattum vāṅkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
varip pākupāṭu : varikaḷellām, kīḻiṟaippāṭṭam (ciṟuvari) mēliṟaip pāṭṭam (peruvari) eṉa irupālāyp pakukkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. C®¡fHŠR Fku¡ f¢rhz« t©zhu¥ ghiw j£lhu¥ gh£l« KjÈad ÑÊiw¥ gh£lK«, ntiy¡ fhR â§fŸ nkuhK K¤jhtz« j¿¥òlit KjÈad nkÈiw¥ gh£lK« MF«.*
cila varikaḷ kācākavum, cila varikaḷ kūla (tāṉiya)mākavum, cila varikaḷ avviraṇṭiloṉṟākavum, celuttappaṭṭaṉa. kācākac celuttappaṭṭavai kācāyam (kācu kaṭamai) eṉṟum antarāyap pāṭṭam eṉṟum, kūlamākac celuttappaṭṭavai mēlaṭiyeṉ ṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa. kācu paṇam poṉ kāṇam eṉa iṟum peyarkaḷellām muṉvakaiyaiyum, arici nel nāḻi kuṟuṇi patakku eṉa iṟum peyarkaḷellām piṉvakaiyaiyum kuṟikkum. perumpālum toḻilvarikaḷ paṇamākavum, nilavari iṉamāka vum, celuttappaṭṭaṉa. cekkiṟai taṟiyiṟai kaṭaivari mutaliyaṉa āṇṭukku āṟu paṇamākum.
cantai aṅkāṭi teru tiruviḻā mutaliyavaṟṟil palavakaip poruḷkaḷ viṟkum cillaṟaik kaṭaikaṭku, nāḷvari vāṅkap paṭṭatu.
nāṭu muḻumaikkum potuvāṉa celaviṟkuriya varikaḷ kuṭika ḷellāriṭattum, oru kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟku maṭṭum payaṉ paṭum allatu payaṉpaṭuvatākak karutappaṭum celaviṟkuriya varikaḷ akkuṟippiṭṭa iṭattilum, taṇṭappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, ūravaiyārāl vitikkap paṭṭavum, aṟamaṉṟattārāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, kōyilatikāri kaḷāl vitikkappaṭṭavum, ciṟṟūrt talaivaṉāl vitikkappaṭṭa vum, āka aivēṟu  tokutippaṭṭiruntaṉa akkālattu varikaḷ. aracaṉukkuc celutta vēṇṭiyavai araciṟaiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
mēṉāṭṭup poruḷiyal nūṟpaṭi, nērvari (Direct tax) nēralvari (Indirect tax) ākiya iruvakai varikaḷum akkālattu vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa. vari koṭuppavaṉaiyē tākkum vari nērvari eṉṟum, vari koṭuppavaṉait tākkātu vēṟoruvaṉait tākkum vari nēral vari eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭum. āḷvari maṇavari mutaliyaṉa nēr variyum, viṟpaṉai vari ōṭak kūli mutaliyaṉa nēral variyum, ākum.
nilavari : varikaḷuṭ ciṟantatu nilavari. ataṉāl atu ciṟappāka iṟai allatu araciṟai eṉappaṭum. ataṟkuc ceykkaṭaṉ kāṇikkaṭaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa.
varivitittaṟku, viḷainilam viḷaiyānilam eṉa irupālāka ūrnilaṅkaḷ pakuttukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, viḷaiyā nilam, ūrppotunilamum paṭṭappāḻ eṉṉum taricumāka irupakutippaṭṭatu. ivviru pakutiyum iṟaiyili nilamāyirun taṉa. ūrppotu nilaṅkaḷ; palvēṟu cērikaḷ uḷḷiṭṭa ūrk kuṭiyiruppu, ūrkkaḷam, aḻintupōṉa vūrākiya nattappāḻ, kaṭaitteru, kōyil, kuḷaṅkuṭṭai, maṭam, cattiram, nantavaṉam, mantai, kaṉṟumēypāḻ, peruvaḻi, ōṭai, ūrnilattūṭaṟuttup pōṉa vāykkāl, pāṟai, āṟṟuppaṭukai, uṭaippu, iṭukāṭu, cuṭukāṭu, iṭukāṭṭiṟkum cuṭukāṭṭiṟkum cellumvaḻi, mutaliyaṉavākum.
viḷainilam, nīrnilam, kollai, kāṭu eṉa mūvakaiyāka vakukkap paṭṭiruntatu. ivai, muṟaiyē, naṉceyyum iṟaivaip puṉceyum vāṉāvārik kāṭumākum. naṉceykaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ viḷai viṟkut takkapaṭi palataramākap pākupāṭu ceyyap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
nilavari, tīrvai eṉṟum vāram eṉṟum iru vakaiyāyiruntatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu oru kuṟippiṭṭa aḷavum, piṉṉatu kaṇṭu mutalil oru pakutiyumākum. tīrvaikkuriya nilam tīrvaippaṟṟu eṉṟum, vārattiṟkuriya nilam vārappaṟṟu eṉṟum, vārattaip paṇamākac celuttum nilam kaṭamaippaṟṟu eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum, aḷantu kaṇṭumutal kāṇakkūṭiya kūla veḷḷāṇmaikku vāramum, aṅṅaṉañ ceyya iyalāta piṟavaṟṟiṟkut tīrvaiyum, vitikkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. vāram potuvāy āṟiloru paṅkāyiruntatu. itu toṉṟutoṭṭu vantamuṟaiyeṉpatu,
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
kaimpulattā ṟōmpal talai
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. vāram āṟiloru pakutiyā yirunta tiṉālēyē atu pakutiyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
vari vāṅkuvataṟkup potuvāka nalviḷaivē kavaṉikkappaṭṭatu. arai viḷaiccalum, naṭṭuppāḻ aḻukic cētam cāvi pūcci veṭṭu nōy aḻivu ākiya aṟuvakai naṭṭikaḷum, varivitikkap peṟṟila. aḻivāvatu, naṉṟāy viḷaintum kaḷvarālum pakaiva rālum vilaṅkālum aḻiyuṇṭanilai.
nalviḷaivuk kālattil, vari kaṇṭippāy vāṅkappaṭṭatu. īrāṇṭāka vari celuttātār nilamuḻutum paṟimutal ceyyap paṭṭatu. tañcaimāvaṭṭattaic cērnta ōrūril, pañcakkoṭu maiyāl kuṭipōṉa makkaḷiṉ nilattiṟkāka, ūravaiyār ūrppotunilattait kōyiṟku viṟṟu vanta paṇattaik koṇṭu, vari celuttiṉar eṉṉuṅ kalveṭṭuc ceytiyi ṉiṉṟu, cila aracar kālattil pañcattiṉāṟ payiriṭappaṭā nilattiṟkum vari vāṅkappaṭṭamai aṟiyalām. picirāntaiyār pāṭiya kāynel laṟuttu eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭāl (184). nallaracar kālattilum kaṭuntaṇṭalāḷar iruntu, kuṭikaḷai varuttiyamai aṟiyappaṭum.
varippaḷuvaik kuṭikaḷ tāṅkamuṭiyātapaṭi kaṭuvari vitikkappaṭuṅ kālattil, kuṭikaḷ muṟaiyiṭuvatum aracaṉ varivītattai māṟṟi yamaippatum uṇṭu. varippaḷuviṉālō pañcattiṉālō ōr ūr muḻutum kuṭipōyiṉ, avvūrukkuriya variyai avvūraik koṇṭa nāṭu ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. avvūr, aṇmaiyiluḷḷa teyvattiṟku nāṭu ceḻikkak kāṇikkai celuttumāṟu, ōriru celvariṭattil oppaṭaikkappaṭum. ittakaiya nikaḻcci, māṟavarmaṉ kulacēkaraṉ āṭciyil, tiruccimāvaṭṭattaic cērnta marutūril nikaḻntatu.
aracaṉ valiyaṟṟa kālattil, aracaṉukkaṭaṅkāta nāṭṭatikāri kaḷ, tam kaṇkāṇippiṟkuṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭu variyait tām payaṉpaṭuttikkoḷvatuṭaṉ varaiyiṟanta kaṭuvari vitippatu muṇṭu. aṉṟu kuṭikaḷ uḷḷaturiyatellām viṟṟukkoṭukkanērum.
varitaṇṭum iṭattiṟku nilaikkaḷam eṉṟupeyar. talainakaral lāta vūrkaḷilum nakarkaḷilum, taṇṭalāḷarāl taṇṭappaṭṭa kācuṅkūlamumākiya iruvakaipporuḷkaḷum, mutalāvatu, nāṭṭuppaṇṭāram eṉṉum ūrp paṇṭārattiṟ cērkkappaṭum. nāṭṭilāyiṉum, talai nakarilāyiṉum, paṇṭārattai mēṟpārppavaṉ paṇṭārak kaṇkāṇi eṉappaṭuvaṉ. poruḷkaḷai aḷappataṟkum niṟuppataṟkum, paṇṭāra nāḻi eṉṟum paṇṭārakkal eṉṟum aracamuttiraiyiṭṭa paṭiyum niṟaikallum aṅkirukkum. kaḷañciyam eṉṟum karuvūlam eṉṟum paṇṭāram irupakutiyatākum. muṉṉatil kūlamum piṉṉatil kācum cērkkappaṭum. kaḷañciyattiṟkuk koṭṭāram (koṭṭa kāram) eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. āṇṭutōṟum, ūrppaṇṭārat til cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷavaṟṟil celavupōka eñciyavellām, talainakariluḷḷa mūlapaṇṭārattiṟkaṉuppap peṟum.
nāṭṭuppuṟattūrkaḷilum talainakarilum taṇṭappaṭum varikaṭ kellām, talainakariluḷḷa variyatikārikaḷāl vacatiyākak kaṇakku vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu.  nilaṅkaḷiṉ taraṅkaḷ  kuṟikkap paṭṭirukkum puttakam taravucāttu eṉṟum, nilavarik kaṇakkaik kuṟikkum puttakam varip pottakam eṉṟum, piṟavarikkaṇakkaik kuṟikkum puttakam puravuvari eṉṟum, ellā varikaḷaiyum kuṟikkum puttakam aṭaṅkal eṉṟum, celuttap paṭṭa varittokaiyaik kuṟikkum puttakam variyilīṭu eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. varip pottakam variyilīṭu eṉṉum puttakap peyarkaḷ. avaṟṟiṟkup poṟuppāṉa atikārikaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
araciyal varumāṉamum kuṭikaḷ varumāṉamum cērnta nāṭṭu varumāṉattai (National Income)¥ perukkutaṟkuriya poruḷā tāram, akkālattil iyaṟṟal (Organisation), īṭṭal (Collection), kāttal  (Protection),  vakuttal  (Distribution)  eṉṉum nāṉku talaippiṟ kavaṉikkap peṟṟatu.
iyaṟṟalāvatu, mēṉmēlum poruḷkaḷ varumvaḻikaḷai amaittal  (Organization of resources). poruḷkaḷāvaṉa nel, maruntu, parutti, poṉ, maṇi, kōḻi, āṭu, māṭu, kutirai, yāṉai mutaliyaṉa. avaivarum vaḻikaḷāvaṉa. pakaivaraik koḷḷai yaṭittalum piṟanāṭukaḷaik kaippaṟṟit tiṟaikōṭalum, taṉ nāṭṭaiyum kuṭikaḷaiyum kāttalum mutalāyiṉa.
īṭṭalāvatu, amaikkappaṭṭa varuvāykaḷiṉiṉṟu varum poruḷkaḷai mūlapaṇṭārattiṟ cērttal.
kāttalāvatu, aṅṅaṉañ cērkkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai, pakai var kaḷvar cuṟṟattār araciyalviṉaiñar ākiyavar kavarāmaṟ kāttal.
vakuttalāvatu, aṅṅaṉaṅ kākkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai aṟam poruḷiṉ paṅkaḷiṉ poruṭṭuc celaviṭal. kaṭavuḷ, antaṇar, aṭiyār, vaṟiyār, uṟuppaṟaiyar. vaḻippōkkar mutaliyōrkkuc celaviṭuvatu aṟapporuṭṭum; palvakaip pulavarkkum, kalaiñarkkum, araciyal viṉaiñarkkum, paṭaikkum, araṇukkum, aracavuṟaviṟkum, pōrukkum celaviṭuvatu poruṭporuṭṭum; tēviyarkkum, ciṟanta vūṇuṭaikkum, aṇikalattiṟkum, icai kūttiṟkum, māḷikai ceykuṉṟu ilavantikaic cōlai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuñ celaviṭuvatu iṉpap poruṭṭum; ākum.
karikālvaḷavaṉ, cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ, mutalām irācarācaṉ, mutalām irācēntiraṉ, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ mutaliya pēraracar piṟanāṭukaḷai veṉṟu koṇṭa koṇṭiyun tiṟaiyuṅ koñcanañcamalla.
araṇmaṉaic celavum araciyaṟ celavum pōrc celavum pōka, eñciya poṟkācukaḷum maṇikaḷum karuvūlattiṟ pōṟṟappeṟum, maturaiyil kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ karuvūlam, 1200 kōṭit tiṉāram eṉṉum poṟkācukaḷaiyum, collāṟ kuṟikkoṇāp paṉmaṇik kuviyalkaḷaiyuṅ koṇṭiruntatu.  (pa.ta.ā.)
poḻil
māntaṉ tōṉṟu muṉṉum makkaḷ peruku muṉṉum ñālattiluḷḷa nilamellām maram ceṭi koṭipaṭarntu orē cōlaiyāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. perunilap pakutikaḷum ciṟunilap pakutikaḷum taṉittaṉi peruñcōlaiyum ciṟu cōlaiyumāka iruntaṉa. itaṉāl maṇṇulakamum ataṉ kaṇṭaṅkaḷum nāṭukaḷum poḻil eṉṉum potup peyar peṟṟaṉa. poḻil eṉpatu cōlai. poḻital tiraḷutal;  poḻittal tiraṭṭutal. maraṅkaḷiṉ poḻippu allatu tokuppu poḻil. oru pāṭṭiṉ coṟporuḷait tokuttuk kūṟum uraiyaip poḻippurai eṉṟal kāṇka.
(tōppu, tōṭṭam eṉṉum otta poruṭ coṟkaḷum ikkāraṇam paṟṟiyavaiyē. tokuppu - tōppu, tōṭu- tōṭṭam, tōṭu - tiraṭci.)  (col 17)
poḻutu
poḻutu eṉpatu, perum poḻutu ciṟupoḻutu eṉa aṭaiyaṭuttukiṉṟu muṟaiyē, irumāta aḷavāṉa paruva kālattaiyum pattu nāḻikaiyaḷavāṉa nāṭpakutiyaiyum kuṟikkum.
iḷavēṉil mutuvēṉil kār kuḷir muṉpaṉi piṉpaṉi eṉpaṉa, aṟu perum poḻutukaḷ; kālai naṇpakal eṟpāṭu, mālai yāmam vaikaṟai eṉpaṉa, aṟuciṟu poḻutukaḷ. (ciṟu poḻutukaḷ āṟum, muṟaiyē kālai 6 maṇi mutal nannāṉku maṇiyaḷavukaḷ)
vēḷai eṉpatu, pakalvēḷai irāvēḷai kālai vēḷai mālai vēḷai eṉa nāṭpakutiyaik kuṟikkum.
nēram eṉpatu, mikak kuṟukiya kālap peyarāy, avaṉ vanta nēram eḻutik koṇṭiruntēṉ, eṉa viṉai nikaḻ ciṟu kālattaiyum ivviṉai ceyya orumaṇi nēram cellum, eṉa viṉai nikaḻ kāla aḷavaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
camaiyam eṉpatu, nīṇṭatum kuṟukiyatumāṉa kālap peyarāy, oru poruḷ oṉṟaṟkuc camainta allatu pakkuvamāṉa nilaiyai maṭṭum uṇarttum, e.ṭu: camaiyam pārttu vantāṉ. amaiyam eṉpatu cantarppam.
cevvi eṉpatu, oruvaṉiṉ maṉam cevvaiyāṉa allatu icaivāṉa nilai.
aṟṟam eṉpatu oruvaṉatu viḻippaṟṟa nilai.
muṟai eṉpatu, oru varicaiyil oruvaṉukku oḻuṅkuppaṭi oruviṉai nikaḻum taṭavai (turn) e.ṭu : iru muṟai (two turns) (taṭavai eṉpatai vāṭṭi eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku). 
taram allatu taṭavai eṉpatu, varicai yoḻuṅkillāmal nikaḻum viṉai nikaḻccit tokai. e.ṭu : iru taṭavai (twice, two times)  (col: 51)



poṟkālam (Gold Age)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000 - 30,000)
tamiḻar varalāṟṟuk kālap pakutikaḷuḷ, paḻaṅ kaṟkālam putuk kaṟkālam eṉṉum iraṇṭum avvak kālattoṭu muṭintu pōṉavaiyākum. paḻaṅkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ putukkaṟ kālattilum, putukkaṟkālak karuvikaḷ perumpāṉmaiyākap poṟ kālattilum, vaḻaṅkiyirā. āyiṉ, poṟkālam mutaliya piṟkālak karuvikaḷ ulakuḷḷa kālamellām vaḻaṅkum.
poṟkālam cempukkālam uṟaikkālam irumpukkālam eṉap piritta tellām, perumpālum avaṟṟiṉ payaṉpāṭṭut toṭakkam paṟṟiyē yaṉṟi muṭivu paṟṟi yaṉṟu. āyiṉum, muṭivu kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatu, avvakkālattu māḻai perum pāṉmaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kālattiṉ muṭivaik kuṟittaṟkē eṉa aṟika,
poṉ aṇikalattiṟkum, cempum uṟaiyum (veṇkalamum) kuṭavamum (pittaḷaiyum) nīrkkalattiṟkum, irumpu ellāk karuvikaṭkum, eṉṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, poṉ mikac ciṟṟaḷavākavum irumpu mikap pēraḷavākavum irukkum.
māḻaikaṭkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kālaṅkaḷ, mēlai yaṟiñar kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟoṭu ovvā. avar tamiḻ nākarikattiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum muṉmaiyaiyum aṟiyārātaliṉ, māḻaik kālaṅkaḷaip piṟpaṭakkūṟuvatil viyappoṉṟu millai. āriyarālum avaraṭiyārālum maṟaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vuṇmai veḷippaṭṭapiṉ, mēlaiyarum eṉ kūṟṟai oppuk koḷvatu tiṇṇam.
poṟkālam eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻakam tavira vēṟen nāṭṭiṟkum iruntatillai. piṟa nāṭukaḷilellām, poṟkālam eṉpatu, aṇiyiyaṟ poruḷil, oḻukkattilum celvattilum kalviyi lum talaiciṟanta kālattaik (Golden Age) kuṟikkumēyaṉṟi, ceñcoṟporuḷil poṟkaruvikaḷē vaḻaṅkiya kālamoṉ ṟaik kuṟikkātu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuḷḷa palvakait taṉic ciṟappuk kaḷuḷ poṟkālamum oṉṟākum. polivuṟṟatu poṉṉeṉap paṭṭatu. pol - poli - polivu. pol- poṟpu. pollutal = aḻakātal, polital. poṟṟa = aḻakiya, ciṟanta, poṉṉālāṉa. pol - poṉ. pol - polam - polaṉ.
kumari nāṭṭiṟ poṟkālamiruntamaikkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:
(1) poṉṉiṉ peyar māḻaip potup peyarāṉamai.
tamiḻarāl mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa māḻai (metal) poṉṉātalāl, ataṉ peyarē piṟkālattiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkap paṭṭa piṟavaṟṟiṟkum potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
e-ṭu :	cempoṉ = cempu. veṇpoṉ = veḷḷi.
		karumpoṉ = irumpu.
tūṇṭiṟpoṉ mīṉviḻuṅki yaṟṟu, eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷaṭiyil (931), poṉ eṉṉuñ col aṭaiyiṉṟiyum irumpaik kuṟittatu. nāka (īya) maṇal poṉvittu eṉappaṭum.
(2) kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilum poṉ mikutiyāka vaḻaṅkiṉamai.
akkālattuk kācellām poṉṉāyiruntamaiyāl, kācu poṉ eṉṟum, paṇamuṭippu poṟkiḻi eṉṟum, paṇattaṇ ṭam poṉtaṇṭam eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa. aracar tammaip pāṭiya pulavarkku ilakkakkaṇakkiṟ poṟkācai vāri vaḻaṅkiṉar. kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār karikāl vaḷa vaṉaip pāṭip patiṉāṟilakkam poṉpeṟṟār. kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēralaippāṭi, nāṟpatilakkam poṉṉum avaṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamum peṟṟār. kākkaipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷaiyār āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉaippāṭi, aṇikalaṉukkeṉṟu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum peṟṟār. kapilar celvak kaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉaippāṭi ōrilakkam poṟkācum oru nāṭum peṟṟār. aricilkiḻār takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēralirum poṟai yaippāṭi, toṇṇilakkam (oṉpatu nūṟāyiram) poṟkācum, ariyaṇaik kīṭāka amaiccurimaiyum peṟṟār. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār kuṭakkō iḷañcēral irumpoṟaiyaip pāṭi, muppattīrāyiram poṟkācum palvakaip paricilum peṟṟār.
mātavipōl taṉ āṭalpāṭalai araṅkēṟṟiya nāṭakak kaṇikaiya rellārum, āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉ peṟṟaṉar. aracarai yaṭuttup pāṭiya pāṇarellārum poṟṟāmaraiyum, pāṭiṉiyarellārum poṉṉari mālaiyum, paricākap peṟṟaṉar.
tillaic ciṟṟampalam poṟpalakaiyāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. maturaiyampalam veḷḷiyampālam eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntata ṉāl, tillaiyampalam aṉṟu poṉṉampalamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
mūvēntarum koṭaimaṭampaṭṭup pirāmaṇap pūcakarkkut tulainiṟaip poṉ tāṉañceytu vantaṉar. kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattil ittuṇaip poṉvaḷam iruntateṉiṉ, talaik kaḻakattiṉum muṟpaṭṭu makkaḷ tokai mikak kuṟaivāyirunta kālattil, ciṟcila karuvikaḷaiyēṉuñ ceytu koḷḷap pōtiya poṉṉiruntateṉpatu, nampattakātataṉṟu.
(3) teṉṉāppirikkāvilum āttirēliyāvilum iṉṟum poṉ kiṭaikkiṉṟamai.
ulakiṉ iṉṟu mikutiyākap poṉ kiṭaikkum iṭaṅkaḷ, iraciyā, teṉṉāppirikkā, kāṉaṭā, amerikka oṉṟiya nāṭukaḷ (U.S.A.) eṉṉum nāṉkām.
poṉ kiṭaikkum vakaikaḷ, tūḷ (dust), maṇal (grain), takaṇai (nugget),  narampu (lode or vein), kalappu (mixture) eṉa aintu.
1850 - il āttirēliyap poṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ, poṟpittar peruntiraḷākap pōy aṅkuccarintaṉar. palaviṭat tiṟ peruntakaṇaikaḷ nila maṭṭattiṟku oru nila viralaṅkaṭkuk (inches) kīḻēyē kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. arampa malai maṇal takaṇai (‘Sierra Sanda’ nugget) 1, 117 viraṉaiyum, āttam perumāṭṭi (Lady Hotham) 1177 viraṉaiyum nalvaravu ayalār takaṇai (Welcome Stranger Nugget), 2000 viraṉaikku mēlum, ālatarmaṉ takaṇai (Holtermann Nugget) 200 tulattiṟku  (lb.) mēlum eṭai niṉṟaṉa.
kumarik kaṇṭam āttirēliyāvōṭum teṉṉāppirikkā vōṭum iṇaintiruntataṉāl, annaṭuviṭattilum poṟ ṟakaṇaikaḷ eḷitāyk kiṭaittiruttal vēṇṭum.
muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭattaic cērnta tamiḻ nilam muḻutum paḻam pāṇṭināṭākum. cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakuti toṇṭai maṇṭalam eṉṟu pirintatu pōṉṟē, cēranāṭṭiṉ vaṭapakutiyum koṅku maṇṭalam eṉap pirintatu. akkoṅkum piṉṉark kuṭakoṅku kuṇakoṅku eṉa irupāṟpaṭṭatu. koṅkunāṭṭilum cōḻa nāṭṭilum ōṭum pērāṟu kāvēri. akkāvēri poṉ kalanta maṇalaik koḻittataṉāṟ poṉṉi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cōḻa nāṭṭil mikutiyākap poṉ kiṭaittataṉāl, mutaṟ parāntakaṇ, iraṇṭāṅ kulōttuṅkaḷ mutaliya cōḻavēntar palar tillaic ciṟṟampalam poṉ vēyntaṉar. iraṇṭām irācarācaṉ, ciṟṟampalam maṭṭumaṉṟip pērampalam māṭa māḷikai kūṭakōpuram mutaliya palavaṟṟaiyum, poṉṉā laṇivittut tillaiyaip poṉvaṇṇamākkiṉāṉ.
ciṟṟam palamun tirupperumpē rampalamum
maṟṟum palapala maṇṭapamum - cuṟṟiya
māḷikaiyum pīṭikaiyum māṭamuṅ kōpuramum
cūḷikaiyu matteruvun tōraṇamum - āḷuṭaiyāṉ
kōyil tirukkāmak kōṭṭamu makkōyil
vāyil tiruccuṟṟu māḷikaiyum - tūyacem
poṉṉiṟ kuyiṟṟip puṟampiṟ kuṟampaṟuttu
muṉṉiṟ kaṭalkaḷuḷ mūḻkuvitta ceṉṉi  (irācarāca tēvarulā)
araṇmaṉaikaḷil aracaṉ taṅkum taṉi māḷikaiyum poṉṉāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. cuntarac cōḻaṉaip poṉmāḷikait tuñciya tēvar eṉṟu kalveṭṭuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
koṅku nāṭṭiṟ kiṭaitta poṉ koṅkup poṉ eṉac ciṟap pittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum curaṅkattiṟ poṉ ṉeṭukkum kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) paṇṭaik koṅku nāṭṭaic cērntatē.
koḻuṅkoṭi yaṟukaiyuṅ kuvaḷaiyuṅ kalantu
viḷaṅkukatirt toṭutta viriyal cūṭṭip
pāruṭaip paṉarpōṟ paḻicciṉar kaitoḻa
ēroṭu niṉṟōr ērmaṅ kalamum
eṉṉum cilappatikāra nāṭu kāṇ kātai yaṭikaṭku (132-5) cenneṟ katirōṭē aṟukaiyum kuvaḷaiyaiyum kalantu toṭuttu mālaiyai mēḻiyilē cūṭṭip pārai iraṇṭākap piḷappāraip pōlap pōṟṟuvār toḻap poṉṉēr pūṭṭi niṉṟōr ēraip pāṭum ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭumeṉka. eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār urai varaintuḷḷār.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akaramutali, itaip piṉpaṟṟip poṉṉēr eṉpataṟku, paruva kālattil nalla nāḷil mutaṉ muṟaiyāka uḻuṅ kalappai eṉṟu viḷakkaṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu.
paṇṭai makatanāṭṭūrkaḷil āṇṭu tōṟum mutaluḻavu uḻum pōtu, ūrttalaivaṉ poṉṉāṟ ceyta ēraip pūṭṭi uḻavar varicaiyil mutalil niṉṟu, piṟar piṉvara, oru paṭaic  cālōṭṭit toṭaṅkivaippāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatāl, kumari nāṭṭil ataṟku mūlamāṉa vaḻakkam iruntirukkalā meṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
uruvap paḵṟēriḷañcēṭa ceṉṉi poṟṟērai yuṭai yavaṉāyintāṉ.
polantērmicaip polivutōṉṟi
mākkaṭal nivanteḻutaruñ
ceññāyiṟṟuk kaviṉaimātō  (puṟam. 4)
eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭutal kāṇka. paṇṭait tamiḻaracariṉ tēr kutirai yāṉaikaḷ poṟpaṭaikaḷāl aṇiceyyap peṟṟiruntaṉa.
makiḻā tītta iḻaiyaṇi neṭuntēr  (puṟam. 123 : 4)
pāṭip peṟṟa poṉṉaṇi yāṉai  ( 177:3)
valampaṭu tāṉai vēntar
polampaṭaik kalimā eṇṇu vōrē.  (puṟam. 116)
aracaruṭaiya pōr muracum atupōṉṟa piṟa ciṉṉaṅkaḷum poṉṉāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
polaṅkuḻai yuḻiñaiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik
kuruti vēṭkai yurukeḻu muracam  (puṟam.50)
ki.mu.11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum 10-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum iṭaiyil yūtēyā nāṭṭai yāṇṭa cālōmōṉ aracaṉ  (1015 - 975), oppīr (Ophir) eṉṉum intiyat tuṟaimukattiṉiṉṟu varuvitta poruḷkaḷuḷ poṉṉum oṉṟu. attuṟainakar, vēmpāy eṉappaṭum pampāy mānakarkku vaṭakkē 37 kal tolaivilirunta, upparā allatu upparakā eṉṟu ārāycci yāḷarāṟ karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kālṭuvelār atu orukāl pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkaraiyiluḷḷa uvariyā yirukkalā meṉṟu karutiṉar. etuvāyiṉum, oppīr ōr intiyattuṟainakar eṉpatu uṟuti. cālōmōṉ kālattiṟ cēranāṭu kuccaram (kucarāttu) varai paraviyiruntatu.
toṉṟu toṭṭup poṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟu mēṉāṭukaṭku ērāḷamāka ēṟṟumatiyāki vantataṉāl, cēkkacuppiyar (Shakes-peare) tam paṉṉiraṇṭām iravu (Twelfth Night) eṉṉum nāṭakat til, poṉṉai intiya māḻai (metal of India) eṉṟār (2:5)
(4) kumarināṭṭil poṉ kiṭaittamai paṟṟiya neṭṭimaiyār kūṟṟu.

cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē.  (puṟam.9).
itaṉ paḻaiyavurai:  - tammuṭaiya kōvākiya civanta nīr maiyaiyuṭaiya pōkkaṟṟa paciya poṉṉaik kūttarkku vaḻaṅkiya munnīrk kaṭaṟṟeyvattiṟ keṭutta viḻāviṉaiyuṭaiya neṭiyōṉā luḷa tākkappaṭṭa nalla nīraiyuṭaiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṉ maṇaliṉum palakālam
kumari nāṭṭu māntar mutaṟkālattil iyaṟkai nīrnilaikaḷaiyē cārn tiruntataṉālum, āṟillāta viṭamellām aṭarn tiruṇṭa kāṭāyirunta taṉālum, makkaḷ tokai mikamikap perumpālum āṟukaḷaiyaṭuttē paravi yiruttal vēṇṭum. ātalāl, perumalai nāṭāṉa kuṟiñciyaiyum kuṟumalai nāṭāṉa mullaiyaiyuṅ kaṭantapiṉ, karaṭum kallum murampum pāṟaiyum aruki nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamum mikka maruta nilattiṟkē iyaṟkaiyākap paraviyirun tiruppar.
aṅku nellum vāḻaiyum pōṉṟa nilaittiṇai (tāvara) vakaikaḷaiyum, avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa meṉṉilattaiyuṅ kaṇṭu, avaṟṟaic ceyaṟkaiyāka viḷai vikka muṉaintu uḻaittaṉar. eṉṟum nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷaik karical nilattilum, iṭaiyiṭṭu nīr vēṇṭum payirkaḷaic cival nilattilum payiriṭṭaṉar. ceṭikoṭi puṟkaḷaital, kalleṭuttal, uramiṭutal, paṉmuṟai yuḻutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (palliyāṭutal), puḻutiyāk kutal, kāyaviṭutal eṉṉum palvakaiyil tiruttappaṭṭa nilam cey eṉappaṭṭatu. ceytal tiruttutal, pēraḷavākat tiruttap paṭṭatu naṉcey eṉṟum, ciṟṟaḷavākat tiruttappaṭṭatu. puṉcey eṉṟum, collap paṭṭaṉa. naṉcey uram mikutiyāka vaikkappaṭuvatāl vayal eṉṟum, (vai - (vay) - vayal), cēṟu paṭutalāl ceṟu eṉṟum, paḷḷamāyiruntalāl paṇṇai yeṉṟum, pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷañ cērntiruppiṉ kaḻaṉi yeṉṟum, nīṇṭa kālam paṇpaṭuttap paṭṭup paḻamaiyāṉapiṉ paḻaṉam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu. nelvayalil uḻumpōtum aṟuvaṭai ceyyum pōtum kiṇaippaṟai (uṟumi) koṭṭappaṭṭatu.
nellum vāḻaiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟai naṉceyilum, cōḷamum kēḻvarakum pōṉṟavaṟṟaip puṉceyilum, varakum cāmaiyum pōṉṟavaṟṟai vāṉāvārik kollaiyilum, payiriṭṭaṉar. nīrppācaṉa ēntu (vacati) irukkumiṭamellām puṉceyai naṉceyākkiṉar. patiṉeṇ kūlamum eḷḷum iñciyum mañcaḷum paruttiyum piṟavum viḷaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
nīrppācaṉattiṟku mutalil āṟum poykaiyum ākiya iyaṟkai nīrnilaikaḷaiyē cārntiruntaṉar. piṉṉar avaiyillāta viṭaṅkaḷil, kaṇṇāṟuṅ kālvāyum ēriyuṅ kuḷamum veṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. avai ellārkkum potuvāyirunta maiyālum, kōṭaikkālattil vaṟṟip pōṉamaiyālum, āṇṭu muḻutum payaṉpaṭumāṟum contavuṭamaiyāyirukku māṟum, nīrcurakkumiṭamellām kiṇaṟukaḷaiyum veṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. akkālattil makkaḷ tokai mikak kuṟaivā yiruntamaiyālum, nilapparappil mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēlum mara maṭarnta aṭaviyā yiruntamaiyālum, kōṭai maḻai, kāla maḻai, aṭai maḻai, paṭai maḻai ākiya nālvakai maḻaiyum kālantappātu peytu vantaṉa. a(l)kāla vampa maḻaiyum orōvoru camaiyam peytatu.
kālvāy nīriṟaikka iṟaipeṭṭi (iṟaikūṭai), uḻaṇi (ōṇi) mutaliya karuvikaḷum, kiṇaṟṟu nīriṟaikka ēṟṟam, kammālai eṉṉum poṟikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. am = nīr. am-kam=nīr. ālutal = āṭutal, cuṟṟutal, āl-ālai = cuṟṟivarum poṟi. cekkālai, karumpālai mutaliyavaṟṟai nōkkuka. iṉṟum, tirukkōvalūrkkum villipurattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa ūrkaḷiṟ cuṟṟukkavalaiyāṭutal kāṇka. kammālai - kamalai- kavalai, ēṟṟam kaiyāl iyakkappaṭuvatu. atu kaittulā, āḷēṟuntulā eṉa iruvakai, kammālai erutu pūṭṭi iyakkap paṭuvatu.
neṟcōṟu marutanilattil mutaṉmutalākap poṅkappaṭṭatu. col=nel. col-coṉṟi- cōṟu. ciṟṟuṇṭiyākap payaṉpaṭumāṟu, aricippori porikkavum avaliṭikkavum kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. payaṟu vakaikaḷai avittum cuṇṭiyum tiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟiṉ paruppāṟ kummāyamuṅ kūṭṭum cāṟuṅ kuḻampum ākkic cōṟṟuṭaṉ kalantuṇṭaṉar. vāḻai, vaḻutuṇai (kattari), veṇṭai, curai, pīrkku, pūcaṇi mutaliya kāykaḷait taṉittum paruppuṭaṉ cērttum, kaṟivakaikaḷākac camaittaṉar.
ūṉukku, vayalilum vāykkālilum piṭitta mīṉaiyum āmaiyaiyum, vīṭṭil vaḷartta āṭu kōḻikaḷaiyum, payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. iṭaiyiṭai mullaikkuṅ kuṟiñcikkuñ ceṉṟu, tamakku vēṇṭiya vilaṅku paṟavaikaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum vantaṉar.
kārattiṟku miḷakaiyum, iḷaṅkārattiṟkum cuvaikkum veṅkāyattaiyum, niṟattiṟkum maṇattiṟkum caḷimuṟippiṟkum mañcaḷaiyum, cappeṉṟiruttalai nīkkic cuvaiyūṭṭataṟku umaṇ eṉṉum uvarnilattu uppaiyum camaiyaliṟ cērttuk koṇṭaṉar.
kuṟiñciyilum mullaiyilum perumpālum pālum cūṭṭi ṟaicciyumē uṇṭataṉāl, avvuṇaviṟku uvarppup poruḷ tēvaiyillātiruntatu. mullai nilattil, tiṉai varaku cāmai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ ariciyaip pāliluñ camaittirukkalām. maruta nilattiṟku vantapiṉpē ciṟanta muṟaiyiṟ camaiyal toṭaṅkiya tāl, poṅkal puḻuṅkal aviyal cuṇṭal tuvaṭṭal puraṭṭal kāyccal poriyal mutaliya viṉaikaḷai nīriṟ ceytaṟku, uvarppuc carakkuc cērkka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu, uppu viḷaikkum uvarnīrk kaṭalaic cārnta neytal nilattai aṭukkumuṉ, umaṇaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal vēṇṭum. umaṇ eṉpatu uvarmaṇ, atai mutalil viṟṟavar, piṉpu neytal nilattu uppaḷattil uppu viḷaittapōtum, umaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.
kaḷarnilat tuppiṟanta vuppiṉaic cāṉṟōr
viḷainilattu nelliṉ viḻumitāk koḷvar  (nālaṭi.133)
poriyal mutalil āviṉ neyyilēyē ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉālēyē, piṉṉar eḷḷilirunteṭukkappaṭṭa neyp poruḷ eṇṇey (eḷney) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. eḷḷiṉ neyyē mutaṟkālattiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkiṉatiṉāl, piṟkālattil, āviṉ ney tavira maṟṟellā neyvakaikaṭkum eṇṇey eṉpatu potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
camaiyaṟku vēṇṭiya neruppu, nāḷtoṟum ñelikōlāl viraintu kaṭaiyappaṭṭatu. atu vīṭutoṟum iṟavāṇattiṟ cerukappaṭṭiruntatu. kuṟiñcivāṇarum mullaivāṇarum vēṭṭaikkārarum, atai eṉṟuṅ kaiyil vaittiruntaṉar.
illiṟaic cerīiya ñelikōl pōla  (puṟam. 315 : 4)
kāṉavar pottiya ñelitī viḷakkattu  (puṟam. 247 : 2)
pulleṉ mālaic ciṟutī ñeliyuṅ
kallā viṭaiyaṉ pōla  (puṟam. 331 : 4)
celvat tōṉṟalōr valvil vēṭṭuvaṉ
toḻutaṉa ṉeḻuvēṟ kaikavit tirīi
iḻuti ṉaṉṉa vāṉiṇak koḻuṅkuṟai
kāṉatar mayaṅkiya iḷaiyar vallē
tāmvan teytā vaḷavai yoyyeṉat
tāṉñeli tīyiṉ viraivaṉaṉ cuṭṭuniṉ
irumpē rokkaloṭu tiṉmeṉat tarutaliṉ  (puṟam. 150 : 7-13)
ivai piṟkālattuc ceyyuḷāyiṉum, muṟkālattu nilaimaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
tīppaṭṭa viṭattiṟ kaḷimaṇ iṟukik kaṟpōṟ keṭṭi yāṉataik kaṇṭu, kaḷimaṇṇāṟ pāṉai caṭṭikaḷai vaṉaintu cuṭṭuc camaiyaṟ kalaṅkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
paruttip pañcāl nūliḻaittup palvakai āṭaikaḷai neytu koṇṭaṉar. nūl nūṟṟal peṇṭirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpatai, nuṇṇiya palavāya pañcunuṉikaḷāṟ kaivaṉ makaṭūut taṉatu ceykai māṇpiṉāl ōriḻaip paṭuttalām, ulakattu nūṉūṟṟaleṉpatu eṉṉum iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷuraik kūṟṟālum (nūṟpā. 1),
pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva liḻaiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻaiyā - eñcāta
kaiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
maiyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu.
eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyira nūṟpāvālum (23), aṟiyalām. vīṭṭiliruntu ceyyum toḻilukku ēṟṟavar peṇṭirē. parutti nūl nūṟṟa peṇṭir paruttip peṇṭir eṉṉap paṭṭaṉar.
paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuvalaṉṉa  (puṟam. 125)
kaṇavaṉoṭu kūṭiyapeṇ vīṭṭuvēlaiyuñ ceyyalām;  naṉcey puṉceyk kāṭṭuvēlaiyuñ ceyyalām. kaṇavaṉai yiḻanta kaimpeṇ, taṉ kaṟpaik kāttuk koṇṭu piḻaikka vīṭṭuvēlaiyē ceytal kūṭum. attakaiya vēlai akkālattu nūl nūṟṟalē. ātalāṟ kaimpeṇṭir ataṉai mēṟ koṇṭaṉar.
āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta
nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval pōla  (naṟ. 353)
āṭainecavu irupālārāluñ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu.
aḻakiṟkum maṇattiṟkum, palvakai naṟumalarkaḷaik kaṇṇiyākak kaṭṭiyum mālaiyākat toṭuttum, āṭavar kaḻuttilum peṇṭir koṇṭai yilum nāḷtoṟum putitu putitāka aṇintu koṇṭaṉar.
marutanilamaṇ keṭṭiyāyiruntataṉāl, uyarnta maṇcuvar eḻuppip peruvīṭu kaṭṭik kūrai vēyntu koṇṭaṉar. ūrt talaivarāyiruntavar, mullainilattuk karaṭukaḷiluṅ kuṉṟukaḷilu miruntu kaṟkoṇarvittu, kārai (maṇal kalantu cuṇṇāmpuc cāntu) pūcik kalvīṭu kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. cuṭumaṭkalan tōṉṟiyapiṉ cuṭumaṭ kalluñ cuṭumaṇ ōṭun tōṉṟiyataṉāl, atikāramuñ celvamuñ ciṟantavar kāraipūcic ceṅkal maṉaikaḷ kaṭṭi ōṭuvēyntu koṇṭaṉar. ōṭu vēyātu maṭṭamāka mukaṭamaitta kāraivīṭu maccuvīṭeṉappaṭṭatu. cuṭumaṇ eṉpatu, mutalil, cuṭṭakalam, cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭṭa ōṭu ākiya mūṉṟuṟaiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
cuṭumaṇ = 1. maṭkalam, (cilap. 14 : 146. arum). 2. ceṅkal. cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunakar varaippil (perumpāṇ. 405). 3. ōṭu. cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ (cilap. 14 : 146)
cuṭṭa ceṅkal cuṭumaṭ palakai yeṉṟuñ collappaṭṭatu. payiluñ cuṭumaṭ palakaipala koṇarvittu (periya pu. ēyarkōṉ. 49).
iṟutiyil ceṅkal eṉṉum peyarē nilaittatu. pārvaikku naṉṟā yirukkavum vaṇṇa ōviyam varaiyavum, ceṅkaṟ cuvarellām maṇal kalavāta veṇcāntiṉāl tīṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
veṇcāntu pūciya kāraivīṭu, kūraivēynta maṇ vīṭṭoṭu oppunōkkiya pōtu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nakar eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu. nakutal viḷaṅkutal. naku - nakar. nakarmikka vūrum ciṉaiyāku peyarāka nakar eṉappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭaik kuṟikkum kuṭi eṉṉuñ collum, kuṭimikka vūraik kuṟittal kāṇka. 
(e-ṭu):  maṉṉārkuṭi, kāraikkuṭi). im mayakkan tavirttu ūraiyē kuṟikka nakari eṉṉuñ col eḻuntatu. nakarkaḷai (kārai vīṭukaḷai) uṭaiyatu nakari. periya nakar (ūr) nakaram eṉappaṭṭatu. ikkālattil mānakar eṉpar. aṅṅaṉañ collat tēvaiyillai. am eṉpatu perumaip poruḷ kuṟikkum piṉṉoṭṭu, e-ṭu: nilai - nilaiyam viḷakku- viḷakkam, mati - matiyam = muḻunilā.
nakarkaḷilēyē nākarikan tōṉṟiyataṉāl, nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakar eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟē tirinta maintatu. nakar - nakarakam - nakarikam - nākarikam.
aracaṉ allatu avaṉuṭaiya tuṇaiyatikārikaḷ vatital, aṟiñarum pulavarum pērāciriyarum vāḻtal, kaṇakkāy paḷḷikaḷum taṉippaṭṭa perumpulavariṉ uyarnilaik kalvi nilaiyaṅkaḷum amaintiruttal, perumpālār eḻutappaṭikkat terintavarāyiruntu tiruttamākap pēcutal, ellārun tiruntiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷai mēṟkoḷḷutal, aḻakiya āṭaiyaṇi kaḷ uṭal maṟaiya aṇiyappaṭutal, tuppuravāṉa kāraivīṭukaḷum māṭamāḷikai kaḷum kūṭakōpuraṅkaḷum nērāṉa perun terukkaḷum putaicālakaṅkaḷum iruttal, ēntāṉa aḻakiya vūrtikaḷ iyaṅkutal, uyarnta aṟucuvai yuṇṭikaḷ uṇṇap paṭutal, palavakai yuyarnta piṟanāṭṭu aimpula nukarcciyarum poruṭ kaṭaikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttiruttal, kaḷavuṅ koḷḷaiyum nikaḻāvāṟu ūrkāvalar allum pakaluṅ kāttal, kolaiyāḷikaḷaiyum piṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷaiyun taṇṭikkum aṟamaṉṟamum, tirukkōvilkaḷum tuṟaviyar maṭamum coṟpoḻivukkūṭamum paṭṭimaṇṭapamum uṇmai, mutaliya ētukkaḷāl, nakaraṅkaḷum nakarkaḷumē nākarikap piṟappiṭamāyiṉa. anākarikaṉai nāṭṭuppuṟattā ṉeṉṟum, paṭṭikkāṭṭā ṉeṉṟum colvatē, itai valiyuṟuttum. nakara mēmpāṭukaḷ nakarkaḷiṟ kuṉṟiyirukku mēṉum nākarikattiṟ kētuvāṉavaiyē. nākarikam nakariṟ piṟantateṉiṉ, nakarattiṟ ciṟanta teṉalām. pāṇṭiyaṉ maturai oru nakaram (mānakar) akkālattu nellai (tirunelvēli) orunakar, vaikai maturai pōṉṟatē paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟut teṉmaturai.
nākarikattiṉ muḷai kuṟiñci nilattilēyē tōṉṟiyatēṉum, atu muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭaintatu marutanilattu nakarppāṅkē yeṉṟum, kuṟiñci nākarikamum mullai nākarikamum marutanākarikattiṉ kīḻnilaikaḷē yeṉṟum, neytal nilai vaṇikavaḷarccikkum celvap perukkiṟkumē ētuveṉṟum, pālai nilai nākarika vaḷarcciyiṉṟip paṇpāṭṭiḻipaiyē kāṭṭumeṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum.
maruta nilak kumukāya vāḻvil, orumaṉai maṇamum iṟappuvarai piriyā ilvāḻvum perumpāṉmaiyāyiṉa. oruvaṉāl maṇañ ceyyappaṭṭamaikku aṟikuṟiyākap peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttiṟ poṟṟāli kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. paṉmaṉai maṇamum tīrvai muṟaiyum toṭarntaṉavēṉum, tollai mikutiyum paṇpāṭṭiḻipum paṟṟi avai tāḻvākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ūrttalaivaṉum avaṉukku mēṟpaṭṭa aracarum, iṉpac ciṟappu nōkkip paṉmaṉai maṇattaiyē kaṭaip piṭittārēṉum, ataṉ iḻivai avaratu teyvat taṉmaiyāṉa atikāram muṟṟilum maṟaittuviṭṭatu. maruta nilattu nilaiyāṉa kuṭiyiruppum payirttoḻiliṟ kuṭumpak kūṭṭuḻaippum, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar kātalai vaḷarttu maṇavāḻkkaiyai nīṭikkac ceytaṉa. uyirukkiṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ērāḷamākak kiṭaittataṉāl, vayiṟāravuṇṭu iṉpuṟavum, uḻaikka iyalātavarkkutavi aṟam vaḷarkkavum, ētuvāyiṟṟu.
poṟkāla marutanila āṭcittuṟaiyil, taṉiyūrāṭcipōy, oru pērūrum ataṉaic cūḻnta uṭkiṭai pōṉṟa ciṟṟūrkaḷuñ cērnta kūṭṭūrāṭci tōṉṟi, piṉṉar ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuṟunila maṉṉarāṭci ēṟpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
taṉiyūrāṭci eṉpatu, ellaiyaḷavil ikkālattu nāṭṭāṇmaikkāraṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, paṭṭakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, mūppaṉ, talaivaṉ, kavuṇṭaṉ, kuṭumpaṉ mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum kuṭittalaivaṉ āṭci pōṉṟatu. āyiṉ, atikāra aḷavil piṟkālattu vēntaṉ āṭci pōṉṟē kōṉmai (Sovereignty) koṇṭatu. kūṭṭūrāṭci eṉpatu, kirēkka nāṭṭu nakara nāṭu (City State) pōṉṟatu;  ciṉṉūṟūrkaḷum paṉṉūṟūrkaḷuṅ koṇṭatu. ammaṉṉariṉ aracu vīṟṟirukkaikaḷellām nakarkaḷ. amaiccarum paṭai maṟavarum avarukkut tuṇaiyāyiruntaṉar.
maruta nilakkuṭiyiruppup pōṉṟē, mullai kuṟiñci nilak kuṭiyiruppu kaḷum āṭciyil māṟutaṭaintaṉa. atāvatu, taṉiyūrāṭci pōyk kūṭṭūrāṭci tōṉṟiṟṟu. paṭaittuṇai yiṉmaiyāṟ kuṟunila vāṭci tōṉṟavillai. ivvāṭci māṟṟam paṇṭamāṟṟu, viḻāk kāṇṭal, vēṭṭaiyāṭal mutaliya toṭarpukaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, marutampōl nākarika vaḷarcciyaṭaiya villai. itaṟku nila amaippē aṭippaṭaik karaṇiyam. ataṉāl, nakaraṅkaḷun tōṉṟavillai;  ūrppeyarkaḷum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. marutanilattu ūrē ūreṉap paṭṭatu. ur=uṟu. uṟutal poruntutal. ur-ūr, uḻavut toḻilē nilattoṭu porunti nilaiyāka vāḻa ētuvāyiṟṟu, ataṉāl, ciṟṟūr pērūr mūtūrum āyiṉa. nakaraṅkaḷ perumai paṟṟip pērūr eṉṟum, mutumai paṟṟi mūtūr eṉṟum, collappaṭṭaṉa.
vāṇaṉ pērūr  (maṇi. 3 : 123)
atirāc ciṟappiṉ maturai mūtūr  (cilap. pati. 39)
mullainilattiṟ kālnaṭaikaṭkup pulveḷi tēṭi aṭikkaṭi iṭam peyara vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāṟ kaṭṭai maṇmēṟ kūraivēyntu, uyaramiṉṟit tāḻvāṉa vīṭukaḷaik kaṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl annilattūr pāṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭuttal= tāḻtal, tāḻvāyiruttal. paṭu-pāṭu-pāṭi. iṉi, paṭu-paṭi-pāṭi eṉṟumām. vīṭukaḷ ceṟintiruntataṉāl, avvūr cēri eṉavum paṭṭatu.
kuṟiñci nilak kuṭiyiruppu nilampaṟṟik kuṟicci yeṉappaṭṭatu. kuṟiñci - kuṟicci. ciṟuciṟu kūṭṭamāka vāḻntataṉāl ciṟukuṭi yeṉavum paṭṭatu. kuṭiyeṉṟatu kuṭit tokutiyai, kuṭi= vaṭṭamāṉa ciṟu vīṭu.
poṟkāla moḻi, pakucoṉṉilai (Inflexional Stage) kaṭantu tokunilai (Synthetic Stage) paltokunilai (Polysynthetic Stage) eṉṟa nilaikaḷum aṭaintiruttal vēṇṭum.

aritu - arutu = kūṭātu. ceyyarutu = ceyyak kūṭātu.
putuk kaṟkālattil, peyarviṉai yeṉṉum col vēṟupāṭum, taṉmai muṉṉilai paṭarkkai yeṉṉum iṭavēṟupāṭum, orumai paṉmai yeṉṉum eṇ vēṟupāṭumē iruntiruntu, poṟkālattil irutiṇaiyum aimpālum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. marutanilac coṟkaḷāl colvaḷam perukiṟṟu.
mataviyal vāḻkkaiyil, poṟkāla marutanilat tamiḻar, paḻaiya kuṟiñci mullait teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṟanta aracaṉaiyum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
akkālattil, perumpaṇṇaikaḷi lellām, tāmāka vantu oṭṭik koṇṭavarum vilaikku vāṅkappaṭṭavarumāka, irucār aṭimaiyar iruntaṉar. avar tappi ōṭippōṉālum aṭimaiyareṉṟu aṟiyappaṭuvataṟku, avar kātil tuḷaiyiṭap paṭṭatu.
teyvappaṟṟu mikunta piṟkālattil, āṇṭi mutal aracaṉ varai ellā vakuppārum tammai iṟaiyaṭiyār eṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟkut tam kātu kuttit tuḷaiyiṭṭuk koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. attuḷai tūrntu pōkāvāṟē, mutalil ōlaiccuruḷum piṉṉarp poṉṉōlaiyum piṟavaṇikaḷum peṇṭir aṇintu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. pāmpaṭam (nākapaṭam), taṇ ṭoṭṭi, aricittaḻuppu, pūccikkūṭu, mēlīṭu eṉṉum aivakai nakaikaḷai yaṇitaṟkut tam iḷamaiyilēyē kuṇukku eṉṉum kutampaiyāl tam kātut tuḷaiyaip peritāka vaḷarttuk koṇṭaṉar. kumarināṭṭuṭaṉ iṇaintirunta pāṇṭi nāṭṭil, ivvaḻakkam iṉṟum iruntu varukiṉṟatu. atu kātu vaḷarttal eṉappaṭum. āṭavar tamcōṇait taṇṭai attuṇai nīṭṭāviṭiṉum, kuḻaiyum kuṇṭalamum kuṇṭuk kaṭukkaṉum aṇiyumaḷavu tuḷaiyai akalittuk koṇṭaṉar.
uḻavut toḻil varavara vaḷarntu muḻunēra vuḻaippaiyum vēṇṭiyamaiyālum, ataṟkup pala putukkaruvikaḷ vēṇṭi yiruntamaiyālum, vāḻkkaiyilum nākarikam vaḷaravaḷarap putituputitākap pala tēvaikaḷ tōṉṟiyamaiyālum, ellārkkum ellā vēlaiyuñ ceyya nēramillai yeṉṟum;  nēramiruppiṉum, viruppaṅkaḷum tiṟamaikaḷum iyaṟkaiyākavē makkaṭku vēṟupaṭṭiruppatāl, ellārum ellā vēlaiyuñ ceyya viyalāteṉṟum;  kaṇṭapōtu, iyaṟkaiyākavē paṟpala toḻiṟpātīṭukaḷ vevvēṟu kālattil ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
marutanila vāḻkkaiyil, aḷavāṉa ūrtoṟum, mutaṉ mutaṟ kōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya oru pūcakaṉum, kāval ceytu vaḻakkut tīrkka oru talaivaṉum, paṇṭamāṟṟa oru kaṭaikkāraṉun tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ataṉpiṉ, uḻavarkku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷaic ceytutavap pakkattuṇaiyākap pala toḻilāḷar paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. avaraip patiṉeṇkuṭi makkaḷ eṉpatu marapu. (ta.va.)
poṟu (1)
poṟuttal = 1, cumattal, civikai poṟuttāṉōṭūrntāṉiṭai (kuṟaḷ. 37), icaiyilā yākkai poṟutta nilam (239).  2. tāṅkutal. ālattu... neṭuñciṉai vīḻ poṟuttāṅku (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital. ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟaintāṉ (upatēcakā. uruttirāk.44). 4. nōvu tāṅkutal. 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. 6. maṉantāḷutal. poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ (tiruvāca. 23: 6).  7. oru viṉaiyaic caṟṟu niṟutti vaittal. poṟu, appuṟam uṇlām (u.va.). 
8. kālantāḻttal. oru maṇinēram poṟuttu vantāṉ. 9. nīṇṭa kālan tuṉpappaṭṭu amaitiyāyiruttal. 10. oṉṟiṟkup poṟuppātal. atu uṉ poṟuppu. 11. maṉṉittal. 12. iḷakkāraṅkoṭuttal. 12. marakkalam nilantaṭṭutal. tōṇi poṟuttup pōyviṭṭatu. 14. celavu tākkutal. vaṇṭi uṭaintu viṭṭāl āyiram urupā uṉṉaip poṟuttuviṭum. 15. uvamaiyākat takutal. ulampoṟukkalāta tōḷāy  (cīvaka. 402)
poṟukka = nirampa, mūkku muṭṭa, aḷaviṟku miñci. poṟukkap piṭittuviṭṭāṉ. (koccai vaḻakku)
poṟuti = piṟar kuṟṟam poṟuttal.
poṟuppu = oru kaṭamai niṟaivēṟṟattai allatu viṉait tavaṟṟai ēṟṟuk koḷḷutal.
poṟumai = nīṇṭa kālam oṉṟiṟku amaitiyākak kāttiruttal.
poṟai = nīṇṭakālan tuṉpam poṟuttal.
poṟāmai = piṟarākkaṅ kaṇṭu maṉam puḻuṅkutal. poṟuttal eṉṉuñ colliṟku mēṟkūṟiya poruḷkaḷellāvaṟṟiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉatu cumattal eṉpatē atu uṭampāṟ cumattalum uḷattāṟ cumattalum eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ muntiyatē muṉṉatu.
poṟu eṉṉum tūya teṉcol, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭakam, kōttam, tuṭavam mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟi, āriya moḻikaḷ aṉaiyavaṟṟilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅku vatu, kīḻvarum eṭuttukkāṭṭu viḷakkattāl aṟiyappaṭum.

kālṭuvelār collarāycci
Glossarial Affinities - West Indo - European Family. “Poru, to sustain to bear, to suffer pationtly, poruppu, responsibility, porumei patience, Comp. Sans. bhri (bhar), to bear; Gothic bairan, bar, beram, to bear; Old High German beram, peran; English bear, bore; Old English bearn, - child; Greek phero; Latin fer-o. Tamil distinguishes between this word and pira; to be born.... Latin in like manner distinguishes between par-io and fer-o. whilst the Teutonic tongues make no difference between bear, to sustain, and bear, to bring forth. They constitute one word, from which is formed the past participle to be born or borne and also the noun birth.”
piṟantai eṉṉum tamiḻc collai ‘birth’ eṉṉum āṅkilac col ottiruttalāl, piṟappu viṉaiyaik kuṟikka vēṟoru collum tiyūttāṉiya moḻikaḷil mutalil vaḻaṅkip piṉpu maṟaintirukkalām.
kālṭuvelār, poṟu eṉṉum teṉcoṟku iṉamāṉa āriyac coṟkaḷai, tāmē kaṇṭiruppatu iṅkup perituṅ kavaṉikkattakkatu.
mākkacumullar ārāycciyuṅ kūṟṟum
“No one can help seeing that even amongst the most ordinary words in English there are some which are very much alike in sound. If these words have also some similarity in meaning we are justified in supposing that they may have a common origin.
“Take for instance, such words as, to bear, burden, bier, and barrow. They all have the same constituent element, namely, br; they all have a meaning connected with bearing or carrying. Burden is what is carried; bier, what a person is carried on; barrow, in wheel-barrow, an implement for carrying things.
“No doubt, this is only prima facie evidence. We must not forget that we are dealing with a modern language which has passed through many vicissitudes. In order to institute truly scientific comparisons, we should have in each case to trace these words to their Anglo-Saxon, or even to their corresponding Gothic forms.
“But though it is necessary, before we institue comparisons, always to go back to the oldest forms of words which are within our reach, still for practical purposes it suffices if we kown that such words as bear, burden, bier and barrow have all beeen proved to come from one common source.
“And more than this. As to bear is used in many languages in the sense of bearing children, we may safely trace to the same source such English words as brith, and bairn, a child.
“Nay, as the same expression is also used of the earth-bearing fruit, we can hardly be wrong in explaining, for instance, bar-ley, as what the earth bears or brings forth. In German Getrcide, M.H.G.  Getregede, literally, what is born, has become the name of every kind of corn. If we go back to Anglo-Saxon, we find boer-lic for barley, in which lic is derivative, while bere by itself meant barley. In Scotland more particularly bear continued to be used for barley and a coarse kind of barley is still called bear-barley. Barn also receives the explanation from the same quarter. For barn is contracted from bere-oern, which means barley-house, or, as also called, bere-flor.
“But now we shall be asked, What are those mrysterious syllables? What is, for instance, that bar, which we discovered as the kernel of ever so many words?
“These syllables have been called roots, That is, of course, nothing but a metaphorical expression........ In burden, den is formative; in birth, th is formative; in bairn, n is formative. In barn, too, n is formative, but it is different from the n in bairn, because it is really a contraction of oern. Bere-oern meant a place for bar-ley, just as horsern meant a place for horses, a stable, sleepern, a sleeping place.
“There remain therefore bar with a variable vowel, and this we call a root, or an ultimate element, of speech, because it cannot be analysed any further.
“This root bar, however, is not an English root. It existed long before English existed, and we find it again in Latin, Greek, Celtic, Slavonic, Zend, and Sanskrit, that is, in all the languages which form what is called the Aryan family of speech. As this root bar exists in Latin as fer, in Greek as pher, in Celtic as ber, in Slavonic as ber, in Zend as bar, and in Sanskrit as bhar, it is clear that it must have existed before these languages separated, and that, as you may imagine, must have been a very, very long time ago”.  (Three Lectures on the Science of Language, pp. 20 - 23.)

ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (Gerundial form)
poṟu-ttal, (ce. kuṉṟā. vi.). 1. cumattal, to bear, sustain. civikai poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntā ṉiṭai (kuṟaḷ. 37). icaiyilā yākkai poṟutta nilam (kuṟaḷ. 239). 2. tāṅkutal. to prop, to support, ālattu... neṭuñciṉai vīḻpoṟuttāṅku (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital, to put on. ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟaintāṉ (upatēcakā. uruttirāk. 44), 4. uṭaṉ koṇṭiruttal, to continue, to possess, as one’s own body. poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ (tiruvāca. 23 : 6). 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. to endure, suffer. 6. kuṟṟam poṟuttal, to bear with, to put up with. 7. iḷakkāraṅ koṭuttal, to indulge to allow. 8. maṉṉittal, to excuse, forgive, pardon. tammai yikaḻvārp poṟuttal talai (kuṟaḷ. 151). 9. piṟa karuttu vēṟupāṭṭai allatu mata vēṟupāṭṭaip poṟuttuk koḷḷutal, to tolerate. 10. poṟuppu (uttaravātam) ēṟṟal, to take resposibility, to be accountable for. 11. kālan tāḻttal, to delay, to postpone. 12. uvamaiyākap peṟutal, to be similar. ulakam poṟukkalāta tōḷāy (cīvaka. 402). 13. aḻuttutal, to press heavily, as a load.
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. poṟumaiyāyiruttal, to be patient, exercise forbearance. 2. viṉaiyiṭai niṉṟu koḷḷutal. to stop, wait halt in doing anything. 3. vilai koḷḷutal, to cost, as an article; to be spent or expended on. 4. tōṇi taṭṭippōtal, to run aground, as a vessel, to strand. 5. māṭṭik koḷḷutal, to become fixed, jammed, wedged in (W.). 6. kaṭamaiyākak cumarutal, to devolve upon, as a duty responsibility expense. 7. iṇaṅkiyiruttal, to consent yield, comply with. 8. mikuntiruttal, to be excessive. 9. kaṉattal, to be heavy, weighty.
