toḻilāku peyarum ataṉ tiripukaḷum : (Metorymical verbal noun and its derivatives)
poṟai = pe. 1. cumai (pāram). (tivā.) kuḻaiyu miḻaiyum poṟaiyā (kalit. 90); burden, load. 2. kaṉam, poṟai tantaṉa kācoḷir pūṇ (kamparā. uyut. atikā. 40); weight, heaviness.  3. cūl (karuppam) Pregnancy (cūṭā.). 4. ciṟu kuṉṟu, aṟaiyum poṟaiyum maṇanta talaiya (puṟanā. 118); hillock. 5. malai. neṭum poṟai micaiya kuṟuṅkāṟ koṉṟai (aiṅkuṟu.430); mountain. 6. ñālam (pūmi. va.) (piṅ.). poṟaitarat tiraṇṭa tāru (iraku. tacarataṉ cāpa. 50), earth. 7. tuṉpantāṅkum taṉmai. vaṉmaiyuḷ vaṉmai maṭavārp poṟai (kuṟaḷ. 153), forbearance. 9. aṭakkam. poṟaiyu nāṇu nīṅkiṉār (kamparā. mīṭci. 38); modesty meekness. 10. valimai. pōtakātipaṉ mutalaivāyiṭaip poṟai taḷarntu (pārata. āti. vēttirakīya. 1); strength, fortitude.
poṟai - poṟṟai = pe. 1. kaṟpāṟai (tiv. tiruccan. 52. vyā). rock. 2. ciṟumalai (piṅ.). poṟṟaimāl varaikaḷōveṉ puyaneṭum poruppu mammā (kamparā. ilaṅkai. kāṇ. 22); hillock, mound. 3. malai. (piṅ.). poṟṟaiyuṟ ṟeṭuttāṉ (tēvā. 1218.10); mountain.
tūṇkaḷ maṇṭapattait tāṅkuvatupōl malaikaḷ vāṉa mukaṭṭait tāṅkiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum paṇṭaik karuttuppaṟṟi malai poṟaiyeṉappaṭṭatu.
“Atlas .... (Atlas-antos (1) Greek god of the older family. who held up pillars of universe; (2) the mountain in Libya regarded as supporting the heavens)”
“Atlantic... 1. Pertaining to mount Atlas in Libya; hence applied to sea near western shore of Africa, & later to whole ocean between Europe and Africa on east & America on west.... (f. L. f. Gk. Atlantikos. f. Atlas)” eṉṟu erutantuṟaic ciṟṟakaramutali (the concise oxford dictionary) kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.
poṟaiyaṉ = pe. 1. cumappavaṉ. puṉṉilaip pavattuk kellān tāṉoru poṟaiyaṉāki (upatēcakā. civapuṇ. 344); bearer, sustainer. 2. malaiyaracaṉāṉa cēraṉ. yāṉaik kaṭumāṉ poṟaiya (puṟanū. 53); Cera king, as lord of the mountainous region in the Tamil  country. 3. poṟumaikkum poṟaikkuñ ciṟanta tarumaputtiraṉ; Dharmaputra, the eldest of the the Pandavas, as embodiment of patience.
kuṟumpoṟai = pe. ciṟumalai (piṅ.), varaiyaka naṇṇik kuṟum poṟai nāṭi (patiṟṟup. 74 : 7).
kuṟumpoṟai nāṭaṉ = mullainilat talaivaṉ (iṟai. 1 : 18) chief of the sylvan or pastral tract.
irumpoṟai = pe. cērar paṭṭap peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu (patiṟṟup. 89 : 9). e-ṭu : cēramāṉ kaṇaikkālirumpoṟai; a title of the Cera kings.
poṟaiyāḷaṉ = pe. 1. poṟumaicāli 2. tarumaputtiraṉ (piṅ.).
poṟaiyāṭṭi = 1. poṟumaiyuḷḷavaṉ. curuṅkum maruṅkuṟ perum poṟai yāṭṭiyai (tirukkōvai. 353); patient woman.  2. kāvu (pali) koṭukkum pūcārip peṇ. kāṉappali nērkaṭavuṭ poṟaiyāṭṭi vantāḷ. (periyapu. kaṇṇap. 65); priestess who offers animals to god in sacrifice.
poṟaiyilāṉ = pe. 1. poṟumaiyillātaṉ, impatient person. 2. vēṭaṉ, savage hunter.
poṟainilai = pe. maṉattai oru vaḻippaṭuttutal (tāraṇai. t.); concentrated attention in yogic meditation.
viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar : (participal noun or appelativetive verb).
poṟuttār puviyāḷvar poṟuttār nāṭāḷvar, poṅkiṉār kāṭāḷvar (paḻamoḻikaḷ). paci poṟukkātavaṉ (e.ma.), one who can not bear hunger.
kūṭṭuccol : (compound words)
e-ṭu :irumpoṟai, kuṟumpoṟai, neṭumpoṟai, poṟaiyilāṉ.
iṇaimoḻi : (words in pairs)
e-ṭu :aṟaiyum poṟaiyum, pe.
toṭar moḻi : (phrase)
e-ṭu :poṟuppaṟṟataṉam, pe.
marapu vaḻakku : (idioms)
poṟuttiru-ttal (ce. kuṉṟiya viṉai.) To wait patiently.
poṟuttukkoḷ-ḷutal. (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) To bear with.
poṟuttup pō-tal : (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. tōṇitaṭṭutal to ran aground. 2. māṭṭik koḷḷutal. to be stuck or jammed in. ce. kuṉṟiya vi. toṭarntu poṟuttal, to continue. to tolerate.
poṟuppuk kaṭṭu-tal : (ce. kuṉṟāvi.). 1. poṟuppēṟṟutal (uttaravātap paṭuttutal), to put responsibility on 2. īṭukāṭṭutal, to tender as security. 3. muṟṟuvittal to accomplish (W.).
poṟaiyiruntāṟṟu-tal : (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) tuṉpam poṟuttalaik kaṭaippaṭittal, to bear with patience poṟaiyirun tāṟṟuyeṉṉuyirum pōṟṟi ṉēṉ (kamparā. cuntara. urukkāṭṭu. 11).
poṟaiyuyir-ttal (ce.kuṉṟā vi). 1. marakkalam carakkiṟakkutal, to be unloaded, as a vessel. 2. iḷaippāṟumāṟu cumaiyiṟakkutal, to disburden and rest tuṟaituṟai tōṟum poṟaiyuyirt toḻuki (poruna. 239). 3. makap peṟutal. peruntēvi poṟaiyuyirtta kaṭṭil (naṉ. 269, urai); to be delivered of a child.
piṟaviṉai (causal verb)
1. poṟukkavaikkiṟēṉ, poṟukkavaittēṉ, poṟukka vaippēṉ.
poṟukkavai-ttal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.). 1. cāravaittal to place, lean against. 2. mikucumaiyēṟṟutal, to overload 3. poṟup pēṟṟutal, to impose a duty or expense upon 4. poṟuttuk koḷḷac ceytal, 5. tōṇi taṭṭac ceytal to run aground, as a vessel.
2. poṟuppikkiṟēṉ, poṟuppittēṉ, poṟuppippēṉ.
poṟuppi-ttal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. cumattutal, to cause to rest on. 2. muṭṭukkoṭuttal, to prop; to sustain (W). 3. poṟukkumāṟu ceytal. tiruvaṭiyaip poṟuppikkumavaḷ taṉcolvaḻi varumavaṉaip poṟuppikkac colla vēṇṭāviṟē (aṣṭātaca mumuṭcup) to cause to bear. 4. poṟuppuk kaṭṭutal, to put responsibility on.
(5) mūlamum tiripum (Original and derivation)
pul - pol, poru - poṟu
pullutal = poruntutal. pollutal = poruntutal. porutal = poruntutal. poṟuttal = poṟuntutal, poruntit tāṅkutal, cumattal.
	o. nō :	muṭṭutal = poruntutal, poruntit tāṅkutal. muṭṭuk koṭuttal eṉpatu tāṅkutal allatu tāṅkavaittal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
(6) iṉaccoṟkaḷ (congnates and allied words)
tiraviṭam
malaiyāḷam - poṟu(kka), kaṉṉaṭam - poṟu. kuṭakam - pori, kōttavam - por, tuṭavam - pir, tuḷuvam - pute = (cumai.) load, burden. kōṇṭi - pukuttāṉā = kaṉattal to high heavily.
OE., OS., OHG ber (an), E. bear, ON ber (a), Goth bair (an) L. fer (re) 
Gk. pher (ein). Skt bhar. LL port (are), to carry. port eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirintavaiyē porter, portage, portable, portative mutaliya taṉiccoṟkaḷum, import, export, comfort, deport, purport, rapport, report, support mutaliya muṉṉoṭṭup peṟṟa coṟkaḷum fortfire, fortfolio, fortmanteau mutaliya kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum.
bar allatu bhar eṉṉum āriya vērccol paṟṟi For instance. the root bar or bhar particularly if we minde the words derived from Latin ferre and adopted English such as, for instance, fertile, far (barley), farina, barley-flower, reference, deference, conference, difference, inference, preference, transference and all the rust, would more than a hundred English words. We should not want therefore more than a hundred such roots to accounts for 10,000 words in English.” (p.26).
“Thus birth was originally bhar, to bear plus a domonstrative element in English th which localises of bearing here and there”.
“The sanskrit bi-bhar-mi shows us the same root reduplicated, so as to express continous action and followed by as a personal demonstrative bearing comes to mean, I bear.
The English bear-able is a compound of bear with the Roman suffixable, the Latin abilis, which expresses fitness.” (p. 27)
eṉṟu mākkacu mullar taṉ moḻinūl paṟṟiya muc coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Three Leatures on the Science of Language) eṉṉum pottakattil varaintiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
orucila mēlai moḻi nūlārāycciyāḷar piratar (brother) eṉṉum āṅkila muṟaippeyaraiyum bar (bear) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirittu, kuṭumpattait tāṅkupavar eṉṟu poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. aḵtuṇmaiyāyiṉ, poṟuttār eṉṉum tamiḻccoṟku aḵtu iṉamāyirukkum.
iṉi, ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cīvakacintāmaṇiyil
karuṅkaṭal vaḷantarak karaiyum paṇṭiyum  (cīvaka. 62)
eṉṟu uyttal allatu ēṟṟiccellutal eṉṉum poruḷil āḷap peṟṟirukkum karaital eṉṉum tūya tamiḻccol, kēri (carry) eṉṉum āṅkilac collai oliyilum poruḷilum kuṟittiruppatu āḻntu kavaṉikkattakkatu. karai eṉpatu paḻaṅkaṉṉaṭattilum kara eṉṉum vaṭiviluḷḷatu. āṅkila akaramutalikaḷ carry eṉṉum collai vaṇṭiyaik kuṟikkum kār (car) eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. āṅkilar varukaikku āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa karai eṉṉuñcol āṅkilattiṉiṉṟu vantirukka muṭiyātu.
ME. & ONF carre f.L. carrus. cum four wheeled vehicle, f. celt karrom OS, Olr. O Welsh karr, E. car.
iṅṅaṉam nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭai āriyaccoṟkaḷ tamiḻc coṟkaḷōṭu ottiruppatuṭaṉ tamiḻilēyē vērkaḷaik koṇṭiruppatāl avvoppumai taṟceyalāka nērttirukka muṭiyātu.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
āriyattil vaḻaṅkum tamiḻccoṟkaḷuḷ poṟu eṉpatum oṉṟu. mēlaiyāriyattilum kīḻaiyāriyattilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭivukaḷai nōkkiṉ, tiyūttāṉiyak kuṭumpattaic cērnta āṅkilaccol tamiḻccoṟku mika nerukkamākavum, ilattīṉac collum kirēkkac collum caṟṟut tirintum camaṟkirutac col mikat tirintum iruppataik kāṇalām.
okaram ekaramākat tirivatu iyalpē.
e-ṭu : coruku - ceruku. immuṟaippaṭi poṟu eṉpatu āṅkilattilum ilattīṉilum kirēkkattilum ber, fer, pher eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. camaṟkirutattil bhar eṉṟu okaram akaramākat tirintuḷḷatum iyalpēyāyiṉum aḵtu eṭuppoliyum mūccoliyum kūṭi mikat tirintuḷḷamai kāṇka. attiripē kalavait tamiḻil pari eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Port eṉṉum ilattīṉac col poṟuttal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyariṉiṉṟu tirintirukkalām.
tiruttam
ceṉṉai yakaramutaliyil bhar eṉṉum camaṟkirutac colloṭu oppunōkkumāṟu ēvi, attiripaiyē mūlampōṟ kuṟippākak kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum.
pōṭṭi vakai
ikal : pakaimaiyāl iṭumpōṭṭi;
ical : viḷaiyāṭṭāka iṭum pōṭṭi;
vīmpu : vampiṟku iṭum pōṭṭi;  (col: 59)
pōlit tamiḻppaṟṟu
ulakiṟ pala poruḷkaḷ pōliyākavuḷḷaṉa. potuvāka, veḷip pārvaiyil pōlip poruḷkaḷē uṇmaip poruḷkaḷi ṉum polintu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ip potuviyalpu pōlit tamiḻp paṟṟāḷarkku vilakkaṉṟu.
	(1) ayaṉmoḻip peyar
āriyar varumuṉ tamiḻar peyarellām tūya tamiḻc coṟka ḷākavē yiruntaṉa eṉpataic colla vēṇṭuvatillai. avar vantu āyiram āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭṭa kaṭaikkaḻakap pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar peyaruḷḷum toṇṭē (oṉpatē) muḻumaiyākavō pakutiyākavō vaṭacollā yiruntaṉa. ak kālattil moḻiyārāycci iṉṟuḷḷa vakaiyilum aḷavilum illai. āriyamum pirākirukatamuṅ kalanta vētamoḻiyum, ataṉoṭu tamiḻkalanta camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻiyum, tēvamoḻiyeṉa muṟṟilum nampappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉamiruntum āriyaroṭu paḻakiya tamiḻp pulavaruḷ aintiloru pakutiyiṉarē vaṭacoṟpeyar tāṅkiṉar. āriyar toṭarpu ciṟitumaṟṟa nāṭṭuppuṟat tamiḻar peyar perumpālum tūya tamiḻc collākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉiṉum, aṉṟiliruntu 1916ām āṇṭu varai, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻp pēccilum ilakki yattilum tamiḻiṉ toṇṭai neriyumaḷavu ciṟitu ciṟitāy mēṉmēlum toṭarntu kalantu vantatiṉāl. tamiḻar peyarum an nilaimai yaṭaintaṉa.
tamiḻarkku naṟkālamāka, 1916ām āṇṭu tavattiru maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ vaṭacoṟkaḷai aṟavē kaḷaintu, tamiḻppayir mīṇṭum taḻaittōṅkumāṟu ceytaruḷiṉārkaḷ. aṉṟiliruntu taṉit tamiḻ  kallāp potumakkaḷiṭaiyum kaṭukip paravi varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, innilaimaikku muṟṟum māṟāka, talaimait tamiḻp pērāciriyarum tamiḻkkāvalarum tamiḻa amaiccarum vaṭacoṟ peyar tāṅkivaruvatu viyakkattakka vēṭikkaic ceytiyē. avar vaṭacoṟ peyar tām tāṅkuvatu maṭṭumaṉṟit tam makkaṭkum pērappiḷḷaikaṭkum iṭṭu āriyamarapai aḻiyātu kāttu varukiṉṟaṉar.
pērā. paritimāṟ kalaiñaṉ pirāmaṇarāyiruntum taṉittamiḻukku vittūṉṟit tam peyarait tūy tamiḻc collāka māṟṟik koṇṭār. tamiḻp peyar tāṅkātavar uṇmait tamiḻp paṟṟuṟṟuḷḷavarāka irukkavē muṭiyātu.
im mūvakaiyārum. ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭu amaiccar, tāmum tam kāṉmuḷaiyarum tamiḻap peyar tāṅkāk kuṟṟattai maṟaikka oruvaḻi kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṉṟaṉar. aḵtu araciyal aluvalakaṅkaṭkum tām taṅkiyirukkum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaṭkum tamiḻp peyariṭuvatē. aiyaṟivuḷḷa arimāvum varimāvum karimāvum narimāvum mutaliya vilaṅkukaṭkum tamiḻp peyar cūṭṭiṉ avai immiyum etirkkātu ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. aṅṅaṉamirukka, ōraṟivuyirumaṟṟa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amaitiyāy ēṟkātu eṉ ceyyum? iṉi. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaṭkup peyariṭuvatil ōr ēntum (vacatiyum) uḷḷatu. vilaṅkāyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭit toṅkaviṭṭa peyarp palakaikaḷaic citaittuviṭalām allatu eṅkēṉum vīḻttiṭalām. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷō perumaḻai peytāloḻiyap peyar aḻiyap peṟā; nilanaṭukkam uṇṭāṉāloḻiyap palakai citaiyappeṟā.
iṉi, nakarap pēriyaṅkikaḷil tirukkuṟaḷ eḻutivaippatu allatu tirukkuṟaṭpalakai āṇiyaṟaintu vaippatum, tirukkuṟaḷ aṟivaiyō, tamiḻp paṟṟaiyō, avaṟṟil ēṟic celvārkku uṇṭākkiviṭātu. muṉṉum piṉṉum niṟkum allatu irukkum āḷ teriyātavāṟu ciṟitu nēram nerukki niṉṟu. iṟaṅkumiṭam ceṉṟu cērvataik kavaṉippatilēyē kaṇṇuṅ karuttumāyiruntu ēntākac cīṭṭu vāṅkavum iyalāta vaḻip pōkkar, tirukkuṟaṭ palakaiyirukkumiṭaṅ kaṇṭu vācittu ataṉ poruḷuṇarntu ataṉpaṭi naṭappar eṉpatu, veṇṇey taṭavik kokkup piṭippatai nampuvatiṉuṅ kēṭāṉatē.
oṟuttārk korunāḷai yiṉpam poṟuttārkkup
poṉṟun tuṇaiyum pukaḻ  (kuṟaḷ 156)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷaip paṭittup poruḷuṇarntiruntāl, ceṉṟa āṇṭu (1968) māṇavarkkum ceṉṉai mānakarap pēriyaṅkit toḻilāḷarkkum, iṭai nikaḻnta caccaravu nērntēyirātu. kuṟaḷ neṟiyaik kaik koḷḷāmaṟ kuṟaḷaip pārttāl maṭṭum pōtumeṉiṉ, ovvoru kaṭṭaṭattilum cuvarukkoru kuṟaḷō kuṟaḷatikāramō eḻuti. ciṟukaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷil ōrīriyalaiyum peruṅkaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil ōrirupālaiyum māperuṅ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil muppālaiyum muṭittuviṭalām. mēlum, taṉippaṭṭavar pēriyaṅkikaḷilum māṭṭuvaṇṭi kutirai vaṇṭikaḷilum ovvoru kuṟaḷ aṭṭai toṅka viṭalām.
tiruvaḷḷuvar kaḷneṟiyaik kaṇṭippatu pōṉṟē kavaṟāṭṭai yuṅ kaṇṭikkiṟār. kavaṟāṭṭut taṉmaiyuḷḷa cīṭṭāṭ ṭellām kavaṟāṭṭē.
2. kalavai naṭaippēccu
iṟṟait tamiḻiṟ kalantuḷḷa ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷellām ovvoru collākat tamiḻiṟ pukuntavai allatu pukuttap paṭṭavaiyē. avai pukuttappaṭumuṉ avaṟṟāl iṉṟu vaḻakku vīḻttappaṭṭuḷḷa tūya tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām ellārkkum eḷitāyp poruḷ viḷakkiṉavē. avaṟṟiṉ iṭattil iṉṟu eḷituṇar poruṭcoṟkaḷ pōl vaḻaṅkum ayaṟcoṟ kaḷum toṭakkattil arituṇar poruṭcoṟkaḷāyiruntaṉavē. ātalāl, eḷiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷai nīkkiviṭṭu ariya vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷaip pukuttutal kūṭāteṉṟu kūṟuvār, aṟivārāycciyillātavarum tamiḻp pakaivarumē yāvar. orunāḷukku ōr ayaṟ collai vilakkiṉālum ōrīrāṇṭiṟkuḷ oruvar taṉittamiḻ pēcap payiṉṟu koḷḷalām. ovvoruvarum nūṟṟiṟku nūṟu tūymaiyākap pēcāviṭiṉum iyaṉṟavarai pēciṉāṟ pōtum!
(3) vaḻunaṭaip pēccu
iṉi. tūya tamiḻaiyum ilakkaṇap piḻaiyiṉṟit tiruntiya vaṭiviṟ pēcuvatē kaṟṟōrk kaḻakām.
kalvik kaḻaku kacaṭaṟa moḻital. eṉṟār ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyar. paṇṭaik kālattiṟ kumarināṭṭiṟ potu makkaḷum tiruttamāyp pēci vantaṉar. iṉṟu pulamakkaḷum piḻaiyiṉṟip pēcuvatillai. koccait tamiḻai virumpum kāmil cuvalepil eṉṉum cekkōcilāvakkiyat tamiḻaṟiñarum tam tamiḻ varalāṟṟilakkaṇac coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Lectures on Historical Grammar of Tamil) eṉṉum āṅkilac cuvaṭiyil, eḻuttiṟkum muṟaippaṭṭa pēcciṟkum payaṉpaṭuttappeṟum ilakkiya naṭait tamiḻai, tamiḻnāṭṭut talaiciṟanta kalviyāḷarum vaḻakkamāṉa uraiyāṭṭil orupōtum kaiyāḷvatillai. palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻp pērāciriyar oruvar tam maṉaiviyāroṭu allatu makkaḷoṭu pēcum pōtu colkiṟēṉ eṉṟu colvatillai, colrēṉ eṉṟē colkiṟār. tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar oruvar tam naṇparoṭu pēcumpōtu, illā viṭṭāl eṉṟa vaṭivattaiyaṉṟu, illāṭṭa eṉṟa vaṭivattaiyē payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟār. (pak. 5-6) eṉṟu iṟṟait tamiḻp pērāciriyaraiyum eḻuttāḷaraiyum paḻittirukkiṉṟār. vaḻunaṭaiyaip pōṉṟē ilakkaṇa naṭaiyum vaḻakkamāyiṉ eḷitāvatē. cila coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻuvaṭivam uṇmaiyil tiruntiya vaṭivattiṉum palukkal (uccarippu) aritāyirukkum. āyiṉum, vaḻakkaṅ karaṇiyamāka eḷitāka vāykku varum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tañcai vaṭṭārattil eṇpatu eṉṉum colliṉ empaḷatu eṉṉum tiruntā vaṭivam eḷitāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
	(4) ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutalit tiruttam
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali, vēṇṭu meṉṟē tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṉ kiḷai eṉṟu ayalār karutumāṟu, tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itil, akkai, accaṉ, accu, appam, āppam, am (ām, ampu) ampalam, arakku, araṅku (araṅkam), aracu (aracaṉ), arattam, avai (avaiyam) āciriyaṉ (āciriyam) āṇi, āyiram, ilakkaṇam, ilakkiyam, uvaṇam (cuvaṇam), uvamai, uru (uruvu, urupu, uruvam). ulaku (ulakam), aiyaṉ mutaliya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭait tamiḻc coṟkaḷ āriyac coṟkaḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pērā. parō, pērā. emaṉō ākiya mēṉāṭṭup pērāciriyar itai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatiṉālēyē, tam tiraviṭa oppiyal akaramutaliyaiyum kuṉṟak kūṟalākavum vaḻuppaṭavun tokuttuḷḷaṉar. inti tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pukuvataṟkum kaṭanta īrulakat tamiḻ mānāṭukaḷum tamiḻp paḻippu mānāṭukaḷāka muṭintamaikkum, ceṉṉaip pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutaliyē karaṇiyam. itait tiruttumāṟu araciṟku ettuṇaiyō tamiḻp pērāciriyarum tamiḻ maṉṟaṅkaḷum ettuṇaiyō muṟai vēṇṭiyum. aḵtu immiyum poruṭpaṭuttavillai.
(5) kaṭanta 2ām ulakat tamiḻmānāṭu tamiḻnāṭṭut talainakaril, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ vāḻnta iṭattil naṭantum kōlālampūr mānāṭṭaiviṭak kēṭāka muṭintatu. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ vaḻiyiṉar vilakkappaṭṭaṉar. vaṅkanāṭṭup pirāmaṇar moḻittuṟait talaivarāyiṉar. tilliyil naṭuvaṇāṭṭut tuṟaiyaic cērnta pirāmaṇaroruvar, tamiḻeḻuttum tolkāppiyamum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭiṟkup piṉ ki.mu. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiṉaveṉa acciṭṭa kaṭṭurai paṭittār. pireñcukkāra roruvar, paṇṭaikkālattil camaṟkirutamē intiyap potumoḻiyāyiruntateṉṟum, ataṉvaḻi vantatē tamiḻeṉṟum, kaṭṭurai paṭittār. tiru. kāmil cuvelapilār maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷaiyum nāvalar cōmacuntara pāratiyaiyum puraṭcippāvalar pāratitācaṉaiyum moḻittuṟaip paṇpāṭaṟṟavarāka vaṉmaiyākak kaṇṭitta kaṭṭuraiyoṉṟai acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉār. tamiḻnāṭṭu mup palkalaikkaḻakat talaimait tamiḻp pērāciriyarum itait taṭukkavillai. tamiḻaracum kaṇṭikkavillai. mānāṭu perumpālum āṅkilattilēyē naṭaipeṟṟatu.
(6) tamiḻnāṭṭu araciṉar pēriyaṅkip pōkkuvarattuttuṟai vaṇṭikaḷilum aluvalakaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻk kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷum toṭarkaḷum puṇarccip piḻaiyāka eḻutap paṭṭuḷa.
(7) kallūrikaḷiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭum aṟiviyal tuṟaip pāṭaṅkaṭkuriya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ muṟṟum taṉittamiḻil moḻi peyarkkappaṭa villai.
(8) vaḻakkaṟṟu varum tamiḻccoṟkaḷait tokukkavum, icai nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya tuṟaikkuṟiyīṭukaḷai āriyar varumuṉ ṉiruntatupōl tūya tamiḻākkavum, kōyil vaḻipāṭṭait tamiḻil naṭaipeṟuvikkavum, iṉṉum oru muyaṟciyum ceyyappeṟavillai. āyiṉum, tamiḻ ariyaṇai yēṟiviṭṭatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(9) taṉittamiḻ ārāycci nūlkaṭkum tamiḻ varalāṟṟu nūṟkum taṉittamiḻ ēṭukaṭṭum araciyalutavi oruciṟitumillai.
(10) maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār vaḻiyiṉar tamiḻttoṇṭu ceyya āvalākak kāttiruntum avar puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭu, avar irukka vēṇṭiya iṭattiṟku araikuṟaiyāṉa takuti yuṭaiyavarē amarttappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
(11) tamiḻiṉ peyarāl araciyaṟkaṭci vaḷarkkappaṭukiṉṟatē yoḻiya, tamiḻ vaḷarccikkāṉa takunta vaḻi ētum kaiyāḷap peṟavillai.
ivaṟṟaiyellāṅ kavaṉiyātu intiyetirppuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭattuvatum tiruvaḷḷuvar īrāyira āṇṭuviḻāp paṟṟi aṟikkai viṭuvatum, vaṇika vaḷarcciyum peyar viḷamparamum nōkkiyaṉavē yaṉṟi vēṟalla. ayaṉmoḻip peyarai māṟṟātu tamiḻppaṟṟuk kāṭṭuvatellām. peṟṟōrkku ōraḷavu utaviṉum avaraip piṟariṭattu maṟṟōrākak kāṭṭi maṟaippatu pōṉṟatē.  (teṉmoḻi kumpam 1969.)
pōṉmai (Likeness) vakai
	ōviyam	:	varaiyappaṭum cittiram.
	kaṭṭaḷai	:	uruvam vārkkum accu.
	cilai		:	niṉaivu kūrtaṟku oruvaraip pōl ceytu vaitta
uruvam.
	paṭimai	:	teyvac cilai.
	patumai	:	taṉippaṭṭavaraik kuṟiyātu potuvāyc ceytu
vaikkappaṭṭa āṇ peṇ uruvam.
	pommai	:	ciṟu patumai.
	pāvai	:	kaṉavaḷavillāta tōṟpāvai allatu kaṇpāvai.
(col. 41).

makkaḷ
makkaḷ taṅkaḷaip payirukku allatu marattiṟku oppākak karutik koḷvatu vaḻakkam kaṭavuṭ kāppaik karuti, ‘marattai naṭṭavaṉ taṇṇīrviṭa māṭṭāṉā?  eṉṟu kūṟuvatum, maṇa makkaḷai āyiraṅkālattup payir eṉṟaḻaippatum ulaka vaḻakku col.
makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku matam muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyā?
mata veṟiyum ataṉā lēṟpaṭum pōrumē, makkaḷ makiḻcciyāy vāḻvataṟkum amaitiyāy muṉṉēṟuvataṟkum taṭaiyāy niṟkiṉṟaṉa. matam taṉṉaḷavil makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku muṭṭuk kaṭṭai yaṉṟu. tamiḻar ellāt tuṟaiyilum talai ciṟantu cīruñ ciṟappumāka vāḻntatu, kumarināṭṭu mata vāḻkkaik kālamē. iṉṟum aṟiviyal kammiyat tuṟaikaḷil talai ciṟantu tiṅkaḷai yaṭainta amerikkar, kaṭavuḷ nampikkai yuḷḷavarē.
oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṉum uṇmaiyai yuṇarntu, mānta rellārum kaṭavuḷiṉ makkaḷāṉa uṭaṉ piṟap peṉṟu karuti, yātum ūrē yāvaruṅ kēḷir eṉṟu aṉpoṭu kūṭi vāḻvataṟkē matam ēṟpaṭṭatu. āyiṉ, māntar tam maṉam pōṉavāṟu matattait tirittu, tammoṭu māṟupaṭṭa karuttiṉaraip pakaittu, avari ṉiṉṟu pirintu pōvatum avaroṭu poruvatum, veṟiyāṉa matattiṉ viḷaivē yaṉṟi neṟiyāṉa matattiṉ viḷaivaṉṟu. paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu kūṭi vāḻnta intiyarum pākkittāṉiyarum, pakaivarāyp pirintu pōṉamaikku, mataveṟiyē karaṇiyam.
makkaḷ akak karaṇa vaḷarccik kēṟṟavāṟu, matam munnilaip paṭṭuḷḷatu. avaṟṟuḷ uyarnta nilaiyāṉa kaṭavuṭ camayattaik kaṭaip piṭippiṉ, kōvil kuḷamō, uruva vaḻipāṭō tēvaiyillai. ataṉāl, pāl ney mutaliyaṉa pāḻāvatum araciṟaiyāṉa potup paṇac celavum ēṟpaṭā.
uruva vaḻipāṭṭai virumpiyavarum, taṉippaṭṭavarākavō palar kūṭiyō, vīṇ celavu ceyyātu vaḻipaṭṭu, viṭumuṟai nāḷilum vēlaiyillā nāḷilum tam contap paṇattaic celaviṭṭu viḻāk koṇṭāṭi, piṟaraip pakaikkā tiruppiṉ, ataṉāluṅ kēṭillai. ākavē, iv viru vakaiyālum, matattāl makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkut taṭaiyillai yeṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭaip poṟutta varaiyil, makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku muṭṭuk kaṭṭaiyā yiruppatu, piṟavik kulap piriviṉaiyē yaṉṟi vēṟaṉṟu. itu intiyāviṟkē ciṟap pāṉa kumukāyak koṭu nōy. pirāmaṇaṉ taṉṉai nilat tēva ṉeṉṟum taṉ ilakkiya moḻiyait tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum colli ēmāṟṟi, matat tuṟaiyuṭ pukuntu, kōvil vaḻipāṭṭai yum, iru vakaic caṭaṅku naṭāttattaiyum taṉ kulat toḻilākak koṇṭataṉālēyē, makkaḷuṭaṉ matamuṅ keṭṭa taṉṟik kaṭavuḷ nampikkaiyā laṉṟu. in nilaimai eṉṟum nilaittaṟ poruṭṭē, tamiḻaruḷ tuppuravil uyarntavaṉ vīṭṭilum tāṉ taṇṇīruṅ kuṭippatillai yeṉṟu taṉṉai ellārkkum mēlāka vuyarttiyum, tamiḻarkku oṟṟumaiyum uramum orumitta muṉṉēṟṟamum ēṟpaṭāvāṟu, avarai nūṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa ēṟṟat tāḻvuḷḷa piṟavik kulak kūṇṭukaḷuḷ aṭaittum, vait tirukkiṉṟāṉ pirāmaṇaṉ. ākavē, tamiḻarai muṉṉēṟṟu taṟku, pirāmaṇiyattai matatti ṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum pirippatē aṟiñar mēṟ koḷḷat takka ceytiyām. matat tuṟaiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟaṅ kuṟaikaḷai nīkkātu matattaiyē oḻikka vēṇṭumeṉ patu, cōṟṟi luḷḷa kaṟkaḷaip poṟukkātu cōṟṟaiyē koṭṭi viṭuvatu pōṉṟatē. cōṟu muḻuvataiyum kalleṉṟō maṇa leṉṟō oruvaṉ karutuvā ṉāyiṉ, atu avaṉ puṟak kaṇṇiṉ allatu akak kaṇṇiṉ kōḷāṟē yākum.
matattiṟ kuṟṟaṅ kaḷaivatu eḷitaṉṟu; ellārkkum iyalvatu maṉṟu. paranta kalviyum āḻnta ārāycciyum paṇpāṭum poṟu maiyum uḷḷavarē, matattaic cīrtirutta muṭiyum. mūvāyiram āṇṭāka īṇṭi yuḷḷa aḻukkuk kuṉṟai orē talai muṟaiyil akaṟṟi viṭa muṭiyātu.
iṉṟum tamiḻaruṭ perum pālār kaṭavuḷ nampikkai yuḷḷa varātalāl, kaṭavu ḷillai yeṉṟu collac colla, atu āriya mēmpāṭṭiṟkum ēmāṟṟiṟkum araṇ ceytatēyākiṉ ṟatu. māntaṉ maṉattai iṟukap piṇikkak kūṭiya paṟṟukkaḷuḷ, mikunta valimai yuṭaiyatu matap paṟṟu allatu kaṭavuṭ paṟṟē. tamiḻaṉ arumpāṭu paṭṭuk kaṇṭa matattai āriyaṉatu eṉiṉ, āriyaṉukku uyarvum tamiḻaṉukkut tāḻvum ēṟpaṭuvatōṭu, uṟaitta matap paṟṟuḷḷa tamiḻaṉuk kirukkum āriya aṭimait taṉam iṭumput taṉamāka iṟukavē ceykiṉṟatu. ataṉāl āriyaṉ cuṟaṇṭalum atikarikkiṉṟatu. itu kaṇṇārak kāṇum ceyti.
māntaṉ puṟa nākarittiṉum ciṟantatu aka nākarikam. ataṉ viḷaivē palvēṟu paṇpāṭṭaṟiviyalkaḷ. avaṟṟuḷ talaimaiyāṉatu mataviyal allatu meyp poruḷiyal eṉṟē, mēlaiyarālum karutap peṟukiṉṟatu.
aṉpilā rellām tamakkuriyar aṉpuṭaiyār
eṉpum uriyar piṟarkku  (71)
eṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷ maṟai.
aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamāy amarntiruntārē.  (257)
eṉpatu tirumantira maṟai.
iṅṅaṉam uyarnta tēvika aṟattai valiyuṟuttum mataviyal, pakuttaṟiviṟku māṟāṉa teṉṟu entap pakuttaṟivāḷaṉ colla muṭiyum?
kattarikkā yaṟukka vāṅkiya katti kaḻut taṟukkuṅ karuviyā yiruppiṉ, atu kaḻut taṟuttāṉ kuṟṟamā? kattiyiṉ kuṟṟamā? māntarai yellām kaṭavuḷiṉ makkaḷākki uṭaṉ piṟap paṉpāl oṉṟupaṭ ṭiṉpuṟṟu vāḻac ceyyum uyarnta mataviyalai, oru taṉṉalak koḷḷaik kūṭṭam tām vāḻavum, piṟar tāḻavum payaṉpaṭuttiṉ, ak kuṟṟam eṅṅaṉ mataviyalaic cārum?
iṉṟu kaṭavu ḷillai yeṉṟu colvārum koḷvārum, tamakkuc camamāṉavarum tammuṭaṉ uṇṇum takuti yuṭaiya varumāṉa vakuppāruṭaṉum maṇavuṟavu kalavātu, āriyaṉ vakutta vaḻiyilēyē niṉṟu, eṉṟum tam piṟavik kulattuḷḷēyē koḷvaṉai koṭuppaṉai ceytu koḷvatu, eṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟivuc ceyalākum? itaṉālum, matam makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟat tiṟku muṭṭukkaṭṭai yaṉṟeṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟa taṉṟō? paḻiyōriṭam paḷakōriṭam.
āriyam eṉṟa pēru millāta kumarināṭṭilum, kaṭavuḷ nampikkai yillātavar cila riruntaṉar. pirāmaṇa vakuppillāta piṟanāṭukaḷilum, kaṭavuḷ nampikkaiyillāta pala rirukkiṉṟaṉar. itu piṟavik kuṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
kaṭavuḷum maṟumaiyum illai yeṉṟu kūṟum ulakiyam (lōkāyata) eṉṉum matam, kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattil tamiḻakat tiliruntaimai, pāṅkuṟum ulōkā yatamē pauttam eṉṉum maṇimēkalai yaṭiyāl (27:28) aṟiyappaṭum. im mataviyalai nūlāka virit turaittavaṉ cārvākaṉ (Ca#rva#ka).
accamē kīḻkaḷa tācāram eccam
avāvuṇṭēl uṇṭām ciṟitu  (kuṟaḷ. 1075)
eṉpatu uṇmai yātalāl, ōraḷavu nalloḻukkattiṟkut tūṇṭukōlāka irukkum vakaiyilēṉum, matattāl naṉmai yuṇṭeṉṟu karuti, matattiṉ peyarāl uṇmaiyil tīṅku ceyyum piṟavik kulap piriviṉaiyaiyē, aṟavē oḻittal vēṇṭum.
makkaḷ vāḻkkaimuṟai
nāṭṭuvāḻkkainilai : ikkālattiṟpōlac carittira nūlkaḷ muṟ kālattil eḻutappaṭāviṭiṉum, mutaṟṟamiḻariṉ vāḻkkai muṟai akapporuṭ ceyyuḷkaḷil, ciṟappākak kōvaiyil, mikak kāvalākap pōṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. makkaḷ evvaḷavu nākarikamaṭaiyiṉum, ataṉāl avaratu naṭaiyuṭai koḷkai evvaḷavu māṟiṉum, paṇṭaimuṟaipaṟṟiyē eṉṟum pāṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kōvaikku oru marapuḷḷatu. atu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum. atāvatu, uḷḷatum illatuṅ kalantu iṉiyatākavē yirukkum nāṭaka muṟaiyum, iṉiyatum iṉṉā tatuṅ kalantu uḷḷatākavē irukkum ulakiyal muṟaiyum oruṅkē taḻuviya nūṉeṟi vaḻakkākum.
kuṟiñci nāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷukku uṭaiyum aṇiyum taḻaiyākavē kōvaiyiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu, mikap paḻaṅkālattu iyalpaik kuṟippatākum. ikkālattiṟ karāccip paṭṭaṇiyum oru peṇṇaikkuṟittuk kōvai pāṭiṉum, paṇṭait taḻaiyē talaivaṉ kaiyuṟaiyākak kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟik karāccippaṭṭu kūṟappaṭātu. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
paṇṭaittamiḻar kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉa aintu vakaiyāṉa nilattiṟ kuṭiyiruntaṉar. ivai aintiṇai yeṉappaṭum. ivai nilaittiṇaiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa (iṭava ṉāku peyar). ivaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷuḷ, pālai marutam eṉṉum iraṇṭum marappeyarkaḷ; ēṉaiya pūppeyarkaḷ.
pālai eṉpatu piṟanāṭṭi luḷḷatupōṉṟa vaṟaṇṭa maṇal nilamaṉṟu. kuṟiñcikkum mullaikkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa nilam pālaimarac ciṟappāl pālaiyeṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil vaṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kālattil ceḻittumirukkum.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
ṟāṉalan tirukat taṉmaiyiṟ kuṉṟi
mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyu muṟaimaiyiṟ ṟirintu
nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
pālai yeṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum  (cilap. 11:12-16)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutal kāṇka. pālaiyiṉ mutu vēṉiṟkāla nilaiyē, piriviṟkuriyatāka akapporuṭ ceyyuḷ kaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
makkaḷ aintiṇai nilattiṟkup pirintupōṉatu, kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu pōvatum, ayalnāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vantu kuṭi pukuntu pōvatumāka iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu makkaṭperukkāl paṭippaṭiyāy nikaḻvatu; piṉṉatu terintukōṭalāl orē camaiyattum nikaḻak kūṭiyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatē tamiḻnāṭṭil nikaḻnta teṉka.
kuṟiñciyil maṭṭum makkaḷirunta kāla muṇṭu. atu māntaṉ tōṟṟattiṟku aṭutta nilaiyākum. tamiḻnūlkaḷ tōṉṟiyatu marutattil nakaram tōṉṟiyapiṉpātaliṉ, kuṟiñciyilmaṭṭum makkaḷirunta toṉṉilai appōtu maṟaintupōyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭavillai. āyiṉum ataik karuttaḷavai yāṉ aṟintukoḷḷalām.
māntaṉ tōṟṟam āṇum peṇṇumāyttāṉiruntirukka vēṇṭum. avaraiyē ātam ēvaiyeṉṟu kiṟittumatamum ilām matamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
irumutu peruṅkuravariṉiṉṟum pala makkaḷ tōṉṟiya piṉ, kuṟiñciyil iṭampōtāmal, cilar mullaikkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
mutaṟkālattil uṇavu tēṭuvatē māntar toḻilāyiruntatu. kuṟiñciyil kāykaṉikaḷaip paṟittum vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum uṇṭu vanta makkaḷ, iyaṟkaiyāy viḷaiyum maravuṇavu pōtāmai yālum, vēṇṭiyapōtellām ūṉuṇavu kiṭaiyāmai yālum, ceykaiyāyp payirpaccaikaḷaiyum vilaṅkukaḷaiyum vaḷarkkattoṭaṅkiṉar. itaṟku maramaṭarnta kuṟiñci vacati yāyirāmaiyāl mullaikkuc ceṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl koṭiya vilaṅkukaṭkum ōraḷavu tappiṉar.
māntaṉ mutaṉmutalāy vaḷartta vilaṅku āvē. ā eṉpatu mā eṉpataṉ mey nīkkam. mā eṉṟu kattuvatu māveṉappaṭṭatu. mā-māṉ-māṭu. mā eṉpatu ṉakara meyyīṟu peṟṟu, āviṟkiṉa māṉa māṉai uṇarttiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu māṭu eṉpatupōla mutalāvatu potuppeyarā yiruntu. piṉpu peṇpālukku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatu. mutalāvatu vaḷarkkap paṭṭa vilaṅku mā(ā) vātaliṉ ataṉ peyar vilaṅkiṉattiṟ kellām potuppeyarāyiṟṟu. ā eṉpatu ṉakara vīṟupeṟṟu āṉ eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
maravuṇavum ūṉuṇavum makkaṭku vēṇṭiyavāyuḷḷamaiyiṉ, uḻavum āvōmpalum oruṅkē tōṉṟiṉaveṉṉalām. āvāṉatu pāl tantatuṭaṉ uḻaviṟku vēṇṭiya kaṉṟukaḷaiyum īṉṟatu. pulveḷikaḷil māṭukaḷai mēyttuk koṇṭu, ciṟitu puṉceyp payirkaḷaiyum viḷaittuk koṇṭaṉar mullai nilattār.
māṭu paṟaiyarālmaṭṭum tiṉṉappaṭu kiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭukaḷil atai ellārum uṇkiṉṟaṉar.
mullainilattiṟ pōtumāṉa nīrvaḷamum nilavaḷamumillā maiyāl, makkaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl, mīṉaip peritum virumpi ṉavar neytalnilattiṟkum kūlattaip peritum viruppiṉavar marutanilattiṟkumākap pirintupōyiṉar.
pālainilavāṇarkku mutuvēṉilil uṇavu kiṭaiyāmaiyāl, aṉṟu avar āṟalaikkavum cūṟai koḷḷavum nērntatu.
aintu nilattilum māntar paraviyapiṉ, kuṟiñcinilattār kuṟi colvatāl kuṟavar eṉṟum, pālainilattār pōrt toḻil ceytu maṟam (vīram) ciṟantirun tamaiyiṉ maṟavar eṉṟum, mullainilattār kuṟiñcikkum marutattiṟkum iṭaiyilirunta maiyāl iṭaiyar eṉṟum āvaik kāttalāl āyar eṉṟum, marutanilattār uḻavaic ciṟappāyc ceytatāl uḻavar eṉṟum, neytal nilattār paṭavut toḻil ceytamaiyiṉ paṭavar allatu paravar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭār.
paṭaku paṭavu. ka-va, pōli. paṭavar-paravar-paratavar. ṭa-ra, pōli. paṭavar cenniṟamā yiruppatāl cempaṭavar eṉappaṭṭār. paratavar virittal tiripu.
nakaravāḻkkai nilai
māntar marutanilattiṟku vantapiṉ nilaiyāyk kuṭiyirukkat toṭaṅkiṉar; ataṉāl kuṭiyāṉavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nilai yāyk kuṭiyiruntatiṉāl, maruta nilattūrkaḷ mūtūrum pērūrum nakarum mānakaru māyiṉa. marutanilattūrkaḷ piṟa nilattūrkaḷiṉum palavakaiyiṟ ciṟantiruntamaiyāl, ūr eṉṟē yaḻaikkap paṭṭaṉa.
ūr nakar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, mutalil taṉi vīṭṭaiyum, piṉpu, vīṭṭut tokutiyāṉa ūraiyum kuṟittaṉa. nakar eṉpatu piṟkālattil pērūrukku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭatu.
nilaiyāṉa vāḻkkaiyālum, māntar perukkālum, mutaṉ mutal nakarattilēyē nākarikam ciṟappāyt tōṉṟiṟṟu. nākarikam eṉṉum collum nakaram eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantatē.
araciyal
māntar nilaiyāyuḷḷa iṭattil āṭporuṭ pātukāp piruppatum. nilaiyillāta iṭattil avaiyillātiruppatum iyalpu. marutanilattil māntar nilaiyāyiruntamaiyiṉ, āṭ poruṭ pātukāppiṟku mutalāvatu kāvalum, piṉpu ūrāṇmai nāṭṭāṇmaikaḷum, ataṉ piṉ araciyalum tōṉṟiṉa.
kuṭikaḷai oru mantaipōṉṟum aracaṉai ataṉ mēyppaṉ pōṉṟuṅ karutiṉar. ataṉāl, aracaṉ kōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kaiyil, araciṟku aṭaiyāḷamāka oru mēyppaṉ kōluṅ koṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu nērāyiruntamai paṟṟic ceṅ kōl eṉappaṭṭatu. ceṅkōl cemmaiyāṉa aracāṭcikku aṭaiyāḷam. 
kō = pacu. kō + aṉ = kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kō(ṉ). kōṉ eṉṉum col āveṉṉum poruḷil āriya moḻikaḷiṟ peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
Swed. - Dan. ko, Du. koe, Skt. go, Ger. kuh, Ice. kyr. Irish - Gael. bo, L. bos, Gk. bous.
kō eṉṉuñ col, āveṉṉum poruḷil tamiḻil vaḻaṅkā maiyālum, āriyattil vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālum atai āriyaccol eṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. pacuvaik kuṟikka, ā, peṟṟam eṉa ēṉaiyiru coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatiṉālēyē, kō eṉṉum col ap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟateṉka.
kōvaiyuṭaiyavaṉ kōṉ. aṉ īṟṟil akaram tokkatu. o.nō: yāvar - yār.
kōvaṉ, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟporuḷil iṭai yaṉaiyum, uruvakap poruḷil aracaṉaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
kōvaṉirai mīṭṭaṉaṉ (cīvaka. 455) eṉpatil iṭaiyaṉaiyum, kōvaṉum makkaḷum (cīvaka. 1843) eṉpatil aracaṉaiyum kōvaṉ eṉṉuñ col kuṟittatu.
kōṉ kōṉār (uyarvuppaṉmai) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iṭaiyarkkuk kuṭippeyarāy vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kōṉ eṉṉum col, ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷil aracaṉaik kuṟittatu.
aracaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil, kōṉ eṉṉum peyarē īṟu keṭṭuk kō eṉṟākum. talaikkaḻakak kālat taracar peyar ceṅkōṉ kaṭuṅkōṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉataiyum, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattaracar peyar (pālaipāṭiya peruṅ) kaṭuṅkō, iḷaṅkō (aṭikaḷ) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉataiyum, kō eṉṉum peyar pacupporuḷuk kēṟpataiyum kōṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉuk kum iṭaiyaṉukku maṉṟip pacuvuk kēlāmaiyaiyum nōkkuka.
kō eṉpatai, ā mā eṉpavaṟṟōṭu cērttu,
āmā kōṉav vaṇaiyavum peṟumē  (naṉ. 248)
eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār kūṟiyatu tavaṟākum.
kō eṉṉum peyar peṟṟattiṟku vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉamaiyiṉ, kōṉ eṉpatiṉ īṟukeṭṭa vaṭivam, aracaṉaik kuṟikkum pōtu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamillai.
tā eṉṉum col āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉum, tamiḻukku eṅṅaṉam uriyatō, aṅṅaṉamē kō eṉṉuñ collum uriya teṉka.
toḻiṟperukkamum kulap pirivum
marutanilattil mutalāvatu uḻavar eṉṉum orē vakuppār iruntaṉar. piṉpu, muṟaiyē vaṇikam araciyal tuṟavu eṉpaṉa paṟṟi, av vakuppiṉiṉṟum pirintu pōṉavar, vāṇikar aracar antaṇar eṉappaṭṭār. uḻavar mutaliya nāṟpālum piṟkālattil ēṟṟattalaimai muṟaiyil talaimāṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. uḻavar kaṭaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamaiyiṉ kaṭaiyar eṉappaṭṭār.
kol, necavu mutaliya kaittoḻilpaṟṟip piṉpu uḻavar kulattiṉiṉṟē palar pirintaṉar.
uḻavar piṟarainōkka, vēḷāṇmaiyil (upacārattil) ciṟantiruntamai yiṉ vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭār. avaruḷ vaṟiya rāyiṉār uḻutuṇpārum celvarāyiṉār uḻuvittuṇpāru māyiṉar. ivarē niṟampaṟṟi muṟaiyē karuṅkaḷamar allatu kārāḷar eṉṟum, veṇkaḷamar allatu veḷḷāḷar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭu pavar. kaḷattil vēlai ceypavar kaḷamar.
karuṅkaḷamarum oḻukkam, ūṇ, iṭam, paḻakkavaḻakkam mutaliyaṉapaṟṟip piṟkālattiṟ paṟpala kulamāyp pirintu pōyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamē piṟakulattārum.
nakarattil araciyal tōṉṟiṉapiṉ tiṇaimayakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu. mullai nilattilirunta iṭaiyarum pālainilattilirunta kaḷḷar maṟavarum, neytala nilattilirunta cempaṭavarum, kuṟiñcinilatti lirunta kuṟavarum nakarattiṟku vantu tattam toḻilaic cey vārāyiṉar. avaruṭ kaḷḷarum maṟavarum muṟaiyē cōḻaṉukkum pāṇṭiyaṉukkum paṭaiñarāyiṉar. paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ veṟṟic ciṟappiṟku ivviru kulamum peruṅkāraṇam.
kaḷḷar taṉit tamiḻarāyiruppavum, avaraip pallavareṉṟu ōr ayal vakuppārākak kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar cilar. pallavar eṉpār. ki.pi. 4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyum, cōḻanāṭṭai allatu toṇṭai maṇṭalattai yāṇṭa ōr aracak kuṭumpattārēyaṉṟit taṉik kulattiṉarallar. avaruṭaiya kuṭikaḷum paṭaiñarum tamiḻarē. avarukkut taṉimoḻiyum taṉimatamumillai. tamiḻnāṭṭu moḻikaḷum mataṅkaḷumē avarkkiruntaṉavum. celvamum maṟamum paṭaitta evaṉum, oru paṭaiyait tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu oru nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟa eṉṟum iṭamuṇṭu. 
pallavarkkirunta paṭṭap peyarkaḷum, toṇṭaiyaṉ eṉpatum oṉṟu. pallavarkkum paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpē, cōḻanāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattiṟkut toṇṭaimaṇṭalam eṉṟum, ataṉ aracaṉukkut toṇṭaimāṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṉamai perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyā laṟiyappaṭum. avvāṟṟuppaṭait talaivaṉaip pāṭiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār, karikāl vaḷavaṉaiyum pāṭiyuḷḷār. karikāl vaḷavaṉ kālam ki.pi. 1 ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ākaiyāl, putukkōṭṭai aracarait toṇṭaimāṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam paṟṟip pallava marapiṉarākak kūṟutal poruntāteṉka. mēlum toṇṭaimāṉ eṉpatu taṉittamiḻc collātaluṅ kāṇka.
vaḻipāṭum matamum
vaḻipāṭāvatu oru ciṟuteyvattaiyēṉum muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai yēṉum vaṇaṅkum vaṇakkam. matamāvatu vīṭupēṟu karuti muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷai yaṭaiyum peruneṟi.
mati + am = matam. matittal - karututal. kaṭavuḷaippaṟṟiya matippu matamākum. matattiṟkuc camayam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. camai + am = (camaiyam) - camayam. camaital - pakkuvamātal. peṇṭir pūppaṭaitalaiyum arici cōṟātalaiyum camaital eṉṟu colluvatum, pakkuvamātal eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē. matam āṉmākkaḷai vīṭu pēṟṟiṟkup pakkuvappaṭuttalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camaiyam = pakkuvamāṉa vēḷai, vēḷai, camayam pakkuvamākkum neṟi allatu koḷkai. vēḷaiyaik kuṟikkum camaiyam eṉṉum col, matattaik kuṟittaṟku aikāram akaramāyiṟṟu. oru col poruḷ vēṟupaṭutaṟku eḻuttu māṟuvatu, oru colliyal nūl neṟimuṟaiyākum.
kā : paḻaimai (toṉmai) - paḻamai (purāṇam); mutaliyār - mutaliyōr.
noṭippaḻamai, paḻamai pēcutal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.  (ta.ma.)
makaṭpāṟ kāñci
oru maṟakkuṭip peṇṇai maṇakka virumpiya vēntaṉ, akkuṭiyār ataṟkicaiyā viṭattu, perumpaṭai yoṭu ceṉṟu avaroṭu pōr purivatuṇṭu. ammaṟavar tam kuṭiyiṉ māṉattaik kāttaṟku añcātu etirppar. itu makaṭpāṟ kāñci eṉappaṭum  (ta.ti.11).
makaḷ
makaḷ - mahilā
maḻa - maḻavu - makavu. maḻa - maka - makaṉ, makaḷ.
makaḷ = 1. putalvi. nalkūrntār celva makaḷ (kalit. 56)
2. peṇ. āya makaṇīyāyiṉ (kalit. 107).
3. maṉaivi. maṉakkiṉi yāṟku nīmaka ḷāyatūum (maṇi 21:30).
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mah eṉṉum mūlam makiḻ, makiḻvi eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.  (va.va: 212).
makaṉ
makaṉ iḷamaik karuttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa col.
ul - mul - mel - meṉmai. in. mulāyam = melliya. piṟappu allatu tōṟṟak karuttiṉiṉṟu, muṉvaral, muṉmai, iḷamai, ciṟumai, meṉmai, aḻaku mutaliya karuttukkaḷ kiḷaikkum.
mel - E. mellon, soft, L. mollis, soft, E. mollify, to make soft.
mellutal = kaṭiṉamāṉa vuṇavaip pallāl araittu melitākkutal.
o.nō : L. mol. to grind, to make soft by grinding L. mola, E mill, building with machinery for grinding corn.
E. molar, f. L. molaris, mammal’s back teeth serving to grind.
E. muller, tool used for grinding powders on slab, upper part of the millstone, f. ME. mulour f. mul, to grind. L. mola, millstone.
mel - meli - melivu.
mel - melaku. melkutal = melitātal.
mel - melu - melukku = meṉmai.
mel - metu - va. mrutu, E. smooth, OE. smoth, smethe, smeeth, ka.metu.
metu - mettu. metteṉal = metuvāyiruttal.
metteṉavu = meṉmaiyāṉa col, kuṇam, ūṟu, poruḷ. veṭṭeṉavu metteṉavai vellāvām (mūturai, 33).
mettu - mettai = metuvāṉa irukkai, paṭukkai, talaiyaṇai.
melukku - metukku. metukkiṭutal = meṉmaiyātal meṉmaiyā yiruttal.
metu - metuvu - metuku. metu - metuppu.
mel - mella = metuvāka. mella - meḷḷa = paiya.
mel - mellam - meḷḷam.
mul - muṉ - muṉṉi = kāṭṭil iyaṟkaiyāka viḷaiyum ciṟu payaṟṟuvakai.
muṉ - muṉi = yāṉaikkaṉṟu. muṉiyuṭaik kavaḷam pōla (kalit. 360).
muṉ - miṉ - L. min, small.
iv vērccolliṉiṉṟu piṉvarumāṟu pala coṟkaḷ tirintuḷḷaṉa.
mince (to cut small), miniature, minicab, minify, minim, minimal, minimalise, minimum, minion, minish (diminish), minister (servant, one who is small), ministerial, ministration, ministrant, ministrative, ministry, minimize, minor, monority, minus, minuscule, minute, minutia.
muṉ - muṉaku - muṉaṅku. muṉakutal (muṉaṅkutal) = metuvāka vāykkuṭ collutal, muṇu muṇuttal.
o.nō : ānu., nu. aṭiyan, vaṭi ayamana ṭaṭiṟa aracaaraca.
mul - muḷ - muḷu.
muḷutal = arici ventu muṇṭi varutal. cōṟu muḷuntu varukiṟatu.
muḷ - muḷai. muḷaital = muḷaittal. vitai iṉṉum muḷaiya villai (u.va.)
muḷaittal = 1. tōṉṟutal. oṉṟāy muḷaitteḻuntu (tiruvāca. 10:8). 2. eḻutal. kālai ñāyiṟu katir virittu muḷaippa (maṇi. 8:18).
ma. muḷai. ka. moḷe. te. molatcu.
muḷaiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ. muḷaiyāṉ = ciṟu kuḻantai, inta muḷaiyāṉ pēccai yār kēṭkiṟatu? (w).
muḷai = 1. vittiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭuvatu. vittiya veṇ muḷai (aiṅkuṟu. 29). 2. iḷamai. muḷaiyamai tiṅkaḷ (kamparā. kumpaka. 16). 3. marakkaṉṟu. ataṉṟāḷ vaḻiyē muḷaiyōṅ kupu (cīvaka. 223). 4. muḷaiyāṉ. 5. makaṉ (piṅ.). 6. mūṅkil muḷai. 7. puṇmuḷai. 8. mūlamuḷai. 9. tirikai muḷai. 10. katavukkuṭumi. 11. kuṟuntaṟi.
ma. muḷa, tu. muḷe, ka. moḷe, te. molakka.
muḷ - mur - muru - muruku = 1. iḷamai (tivā). 2. aḻaku (piṅ.). 3. eḻucci (tivā.). 4. murukaṉ. aruṅkaṭi vēlaṉ murukoṭu vaḷaii (maturaik - 611). 5. teyvam. muruku meyppaṭṭa pulaitti pōla (puṟanā. 259). 6. vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭṭu. murukayarntu vanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ (kuṟun. 362). 7. tiruviḻā. murukayar pāṇiyum (cūḷā. nāṭ. 7). 8. tirumurukāṟṟup paṭai. muruku porunāṟu (taṉippā.).
muruku - murukaṉ. ma. murukaṉ, ka. muruka (g).

muḷ - muṇṭu. muṇṭutal = vittiṉ muḷai muḷuttal allatu muṭṭutal.
muḷ - muṭṭu = iḷam piñcu. muṭṭukkāy = muṟṟāta iḷanīr.
muṭṭuk kurumpai = ciṟu kurumpai.
muṭṭu - moṭṭu = 1. arumpu. moṭṭaṟā malar (tiruvāca 29:8). 2. tēriṉ kūmpu. māmoṭ ṭoṭintu ... māṉṟēr citaiya (pārata. nāṉkā nāḷ. 24).
moṭṭu - moṭṭai. moṭṭaip payal = maṇamākāta iḷaiñaṉ, iḷavaṭṭam. (potuvāka iḻivuk kuṟippu).
muḷ - muṇ - muṇa. muṇamuṇattal = metuvāka vāykkuṭ pēcutal.
muṇ - muṇu. muṇu muṇuttal = metuvāka vāykkuṭ pēcutal.
muṇu - muṇukku. muṇukku muṇukkeṉal = ciṟu kuḻavi ciṟitu ciṟitākat tāyppāluṇṇutal.
muṇamuṇa - moṇa moṇa. moṇa moṇa veṉal = vāykkuṭ collutal. cuṟṟivantu moṇa moṇeṉṟu collu mantiram ētaṭā (civavāk. pā.)
muṇukku - muṭukku = 1. ciṟṟaḷavāṉa kuṭinīr. 2. ciṟu teruc cantu.

muṭukku - viṭukku = ciṟṟaḷavāṉa kuṭinīr.
muṇ - maṇ - maṇi = ciṟiyatu. maṇikkayiṟu, maṇik kākkai, maṇikkāṭai. maṇikkuṭal, maṇikkai, maṇippuṟā mutaliya kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷai nōkkuka. maṇ - maṇṇai = iḷamai (piṅ.).
maṇi - māṇi = 1. ciṟiyatu. 2. māṇavaṉ, paḷḷippiḷḷai. 3. maṇamākātavaṉ. 4. kuṟaḷaṉ. karumāṇiyā yiranta kaḷvaṉē (tiv. iyaṟ. 2:61), māṇik kuṟaḷuru vāya māyaṉ (tiv. periyāḻ. 5:2:5).
māṇi - māṇavaṉ - ta. māṇava.

muku - mukuḷ - mukuḷam = 1. arumpu. paṅkaya mukuḷan taṉṉaik koṅkaiyāp paṭaitta (tiruvālavā. 4 : 14). 2. aintu viralun tammil talaikuvintu uyarntu niṟkum iṇaiyā viṇaikkai vakai. (cilap. 3 : 18, urai). 3. (ōkam) irukālum okkavaittu maṇṭalamāka irukkum irukkai vakai. (tattuvap. 109, urai). 4. mūḷaiyiṉ piṉ pakutiyāṉa muḷḷan taṇṭuk koṭiyiṉ kuvaṭu (cikaram), (iṅ. vai. pak. 27).
mukuḷam - va. mukula.

mukam - va. muka (mukha).
mukañceytal = (ce. ku. vi.). 1. tōṉṟutal. mukañ ceykārikai (peruṅ. uñcaik. 35 : 49). 2. muṉṉātal. tōṟṟiṉāṉ mukañcey kōlam (cīvaka. 6275) - (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) muṉṉiṉāṉ vaṭaticai mukañceytu (cīvaka. 1408).
mukam eṉpatu, nilaic collākavum varuñ collākavum ettuṇaiyō kūṭṭuc coṟkaḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e.ṭu. mukavurai, mukakkaḷai, mukaccāṭai, mukatalai, mukanaṭpu, mukappaḻakkam, mukamaṇṭapam, mukavāyk kaṭṭai.
aṟimukam, kaḻimukam, curimukam, tirumukam, tuṟaimukam, nūṉmukam, pōrmukam, maṟaimukam, muṉaimukam.
mukam eṉṉuñ colliṟkut tōṟṟam allatu muṉ pakkam eṉpatē aṭippaṭaipporuḷ. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ataṟku vāy eṉpatai aṭippaṭaip poruḷākak koṇṭu, ataṟkēṟpap poruntāp poyttalāka oru karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
mukam - mukaṭu = 1. ucci. mukaṭutumit taṭukkiya paḻampalluṇaviṉ (perumpāṇ. 246). 2. uyarvu. muṉimai mukaṭāya mūvā mutalvaṉ (cīvaka. 1609). 3. talai. mukaṭūr mayirkaṭinta ceykai yārum (tēvā. 936 : 10). 4. mutukuppuṟam. collat takumukaṭ ṭoṭṭakam (kaṉā. 15). 5. vīṭṭiṉ mēṟkūrai. makaṉ - māṉ - maṉ (āṇ pālīṟukaḷ).
e.ṭu. paṇimakaṉ, perumāṉ, ceṟumaṉ.
makaḷ - māḷ (peṇpā līṟukaḷ).
e.ṭu. paṇimakaḷ, vēṇmāḷ.
maka + kaḷ = makkaḷ (marūup puṇarcci).
makkaḷ - (irupāṟ potup palarpāṟ peyar) = 1. ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷ. e.ṭu. āṇmakkaḷ, peṇmakkaḷ, irupāl makkaḷ. 2. vaḷarcciyuṟṟa māntar. e.ṭu. āṇmakkaḷ, (āṭavar), peṇmakkaḷ (peṇṭir), irupāl makkaḷ (irupāṉ māntar).
makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē  (tol. mara. 33).
uyartiṇai yeṉmaṉār makkaṭcuṭṭē  (tol. kiḷavi. 1)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, ilakkaṇa nūlilum aṟa nūlilum paṇpaṭṭa māntarē makkaḷeṉṟum, paṇpaṭā māntarellām mākkaḷeṉṟum, kuṟikkappaṭuvareṉpatu pōtarum.
e.ṭu.
vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉaiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉai yavar.  (kuṟaḷ. 410)
ceviyiṟ cuvaiyuṇarā vāyuṇarviṉ mākkaḷ
aviyiṉum vāḻiṉu meṉ.  (. 420)
makaṉ eṉṉum teṉcol, kōtiya (gothic) moḻiyil maku (magus) eṉṉum, kēliya (Gaelic) moḻiyil mak (mac) eṉṟum, tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
“Mac (mak) A Gaelic word signifying son, and prefixed to many surnames, as Mac Donald, Mac Grigor, &c. It is synonymous with Son in names of Tentonic origin ... It is allied to Goth magus, a son, fem. magaths (G. magd, a maid)”. I.D.E.L. Vol. III. p. 91.
Gadhelic (Gaelic), a. Of or pertaining to that branch of the Celtic race which comprises the Erse of Ireland, the Gaels of Scotland and the Manx of the Isle of Man.
- The Imperial Dictionary of the English Language, Vol. II, p. 352.
Goth, n. One of an ancient Teutonic race of people, first heard of as inhabiting the shores of the Baltic. - Do. p. 413.
Mac eṉṉum kēliyac col Mc eṉṟu kuṟukiyum vaḻaṅkum.
e.ṭu. : Mc Adam. 
makaṉ - māṉ - maṉ = māntaṉ.
maṉpatu = makkaṭ ṭokuti. maṉpatu maṟukkat tuṉpaṅ kaḷai vōṉ (paripā. 15:52).
maṉpatu - maṉpatai = 1. makkaḷiṉam. maṉpatai kākkuniṉ puraimai (puṟanā. 210). 2. paṭaimakkaṭ ṭokuti. maṉpatai peyara (patiṟṟup. 77:3).
maṉ - Teut. man, Skt, manu.
“MAN, a human being. (E). ME. man, Chaucer, C.T.I. 43. A.S. mann, also mon; Grein, ii. 105 + Du man; Ice. madr (for manur); also man; Dan mand (with excrescent d); Goth, manna; G mann; (the G. mensch = mannich, i.e. mannish human). Allied to Skt. manu, Vedic manus -, a man. B. connected by some with Skt. man, to think. See Mind. But it is unlikely that the orig Sense could have been thinker.
Skeat’s Etymological Dictionary of the English Language, p. 358. 
maṉ - va. manu.
manu, thinking creature, man, Manu.
manuṣa, a man.
manuṣī, a woman
manuṣya, human, manly; human being, man.
manuṣvat, as a man.
manvantara (nt), age of a Manu.
mānava, descended from or belonging to Man or Manu.
mānavaya, to act like man.
mānavīya, descended or derived from Manu.
mānuṣya, human nature or condition.
- Monier William’s Sanskrit - English Dictionary : Man eṉṉum āṅkilac collaiyum manu eṉṉum camaṟkirutac collaiyum muṟaiyē mind. mana eṉṉum coṟkaḷōṭu toṭarpu paṭutti thinking animal, thinking creature eṉṟu mēlaiyar cilar poruḷ kūṟuvar. atu tavaṟu.
iṭaic cērppu
muku - mukku - iruteru kōṇampaṭak kūṭum mūkkup pōṉṟa mūlai. mukkuvēṟu; muṭukku (cantu) vēṟu.
mukku - mukkai = āṟu tirumpum allatu īrāṟu kūṭum iṭattu mukkup pōṉṟa mūlai.
mukku - mūkku = 1. mukattiṉ muṉ nīṇṭuḷḷa uṟuppu. 2. paṟavai yalaku. 3. yāṉait tutikkai. 4. kalaṅkaḷiṉ vāyil mūkkuppōl nīṇṭuḷḷa uṟuppu. 5. ilai nuṉi. 6. koṇṭaik kaṭalaip payaṟṟiṉ muṉai, 7. vittiṉ muḷai. 8. vaṇṭippāriṉ mūkku vaṭivāṉa muṉaippakuti.
ma. bj., tu. mūkku, ka. mūku (g).
mūkku - mukkaṉ = 1. eṭuppāṉa mūkkuḷḷavaṉ. 2. mīṉkotti.
makuṭam
makuṭam - makuṭa ( t|) mukuṭa ( t|)
mukam - mukaṭu = mūkkuppōṉṟa kūraiyucci.
mukaṭu = 1. ucci. mukaṭu tumittaṭukkiya paḻampal luṇaviṉ (perumpāṇ. 246).
2. vīṭṭiṉ ucci.
3. vāṉamukaṭu. vāṉeṭu mukaṭṭai yuṟṟaṉaṉ (kamparā. maruttu. 30).
4. talai. mukaṭūr mayirkaṭinta ceykaiyārum (tēvā. 936 : 10).
5. uyarvu. muṉimai mukaṭāya mūvā mutalvaṉ (cīvaka. 1609).
mukaṭu - (mukaṭam) - makuṭam = uccimayir muṭi, maṇimuṭi, tēr muṭi, oruporuṭ pala pāṭṭup potu muṭivu.
iṉi, mukiḻ - mukiḻam - mukuḷam - mukuṭam - makuṭam = moṭṭup pōṟ kūmpiya maṇimuṭi eṉṟumām.
māmoṭṭoṭintu ... māṉṟēr citaiya  (pārata. nāṉkā nāḷ. 24).
pērā. parō. iccollait teṉcolleṉṟē kūṟutal kāṇka. (The Sanskrit Language, p. 381).  (va.va.)
mukuṭam piṟai vaṭiviṉateṉṟum, kirīṭam kuvintateṉṟum, mauli mummuṉaiyateṉṟum mā. vi. a. kūṟum.  (va.va: 212-213).
maṅkalam
maṅkalam - maṅkala
maṅku - maṅkal - mañcal = maṅkalāṉa niṟam. anniṟak kiḻaṅku (mañcaḷ). mañcal - mañcaḷ.
o.nō : poṅku - poñcu, iṅkē - iñcē (koccai).
maṅkal - maṅkalam = 1. mañcaḷāl allatu mañcaḷ nīrāl kuṟikkappeṟum naṉṉilaimai.
maṅkala makaḷiroṭu mālai cūṭṭi  (puṟam. 332)
2. tirumaṇam. maṅkala vāḻttup pāṭal (cilap.).
3. tirumaṇattāli.
maṟṟainal laṇikaḷ kāṇuṉ maṅkalaṅ kātta maṉṉō  (kamparā. urukkāṭṭu. 35).

maṅkalam - maṅkaḷam. vaṭavar - kāṭṭum mūlam. maṅk = cel, iyaṅku. pakuttaṟivuṭaiyār kaṇṭu teḷika.  (ti.ma. 751).
maṅkala vaḻakku
oruvar tuṉpuṟum pōtu, avarai āṟṟit tēṟṟuvatum avaroṭu tāmum cērntu tuṉpuṟuvatum, uyarntōr iyalpu. kaṇṇaṉṉa kēḷirai iḻappatum, eri pōṉṟa vaṟumaiyāl vāṭuvatum peruntuṉpamām. attuṉpaṅkaḷai avaṟṟiṟkuriya collāṟ kuṟippiṉum avaṟṟāl tuṉpuṟuvōrkkut tuṉpam perukumeṉṟu karuti, iṉpamum naṉmaiyum kuṟikkum coṟkaḷāl avaṟṟaik kuṟittu vantaṉar paṇṭai mēlōr. itaṉai maṅkala vaḻak keṉpar ilakkaṇavāciriyar.
cettāṉait tuñciṉāṉ eṉṟum, cāvaip perum piṟitu eṉṟum, cuṭukāṭṭai naṉkāṭu eṉṟum, tāliyaṟutalait tāli perukiṟṟu eṉṟum vaṟumaiyai nirappu eṉṟum, vaṟumaip pāṭṭai nal kūrtal eṉṟum kūṟuvatu maṅkala vaḻakkām.
tuñcutal tūṅkutal. nirappu niṟaivu, nal kūrtal naṉmai mikutal. iṉi, cāvuk kētuvāṉa naccuyirikaḷaiyum maṅkalac collāṟ kuṟippatu vaḻakkam. e.ṭu. nalla pāmpu.  (col. 112.)
maci
maci - maṣi
mattu - matti. mattittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭaital, kaṭaintu kaḷi allatu kūḻākkutal.
matti - mati. matittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭaivatupōṟ kaiviralāṟ kuḻaittal.
kuḻantaikkuc cōṟṟai matittu ūṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
mati - maci. macittal = kuḻaittal. macital = kuḻaital.
maci - macaku = vaikkōṟ kariyōṭu viḷakkeṇṇey kalantu macitta (kuḻaitta) vaṇṭi mai.
eḻutu maiyum iṅṅaṉamē vēṟu poruḷai macittu amaikkap peṟum.
macittu maiyai viḷḷa veḻuti  (patiṉo. tiruvālaṅ. mūt. 2)
maci = eḻutumai. macikalan teḻutappaṭṭa (cūḷā. tūtu. 83).
macikkūṭu, macikkuppi eṉpaṉa ulaka vaḻakku.
maci - mayi - mai.
karuniṟattaik kuṟikkum mai eṉṉum colvēṟu. māl - mā - mai.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum mūlam maṣ = citai, cētappaṭuttu. mā. vi. a.
ic colliṉ kīḻ, “(Prob. invented to serve as the source of the words below)” eṉṟu kuṟittiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
maṣi eṉpatu mēlaiyāriya vaḻiyāy vaṭamoḻi peṟṟa teṉ col tiripukaḷuḷ oṉṟākum.

o.nō : ai - ayir, tai - tayir, pai - payir, vai - vayir.
mā. vi. a. “(of unknown derivation, but of. sÝman)”.
“sÝman, n. the body, Nir.; the mouth, L. (both meanings prob. invented to explain sÝmasÝa#na and sÝmasÝru)” eṉṟu kuṟittiruttal kāṇka.  (va.va.214).
mañcu viraṭṭu
ēṟu taḻuval eṉṉum paṇṭai vaḻakkamē iṉṟu kaḷḷar maṟavariṭaic callikkaṭṭu eṉṟum mañcu viraṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ti.5)
maṭam
maṭuttal = uṇṇutal. maṭu - maṭai = uṇavu. maṭaittoḻil = camaiyal toḻil. maṭaiyaṉ = camaippōṉ.
maṭaippaḷḷi = camaiyalaṟai allatu camaiyal maṉai. maṭai nūl = camaiyaṟkalai nūl.
maṭu - maṭam = aṟavuṇavuccālai, tuṟaviyarum irappōrum uṇṭu taṅkum iṭam, camayac cārpāṉa tuṟaviyar viṭuti, tuṟaviyar kallūri.
tamiḻakattiṟ pērūrtoṟum maṭamuṇṭu. atil paṇṭāram paratēci eṉṉum āṇṭikaḷum irappōrum ayalūr eḷiyārum taṅkuvar. āṇṭikaḷ kūṭi maṭaṅkaṭṭiṉāṟpōl. eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. āṇṭimaṭam eṉpatu peruvaḻakku.
celvap peṟṟōr, tamakkaṭaṅkātu tammaiviṭṭup pirintu ceṉṟa makkaḷ pallāṇṭu kaḻittut tammūr maṭattiṟku vantu taṅkuvārāyiṉ, tam uṟavaik kāṭṭikkoḷḷātē avarkku iravil nalluṇavaḷippatu vaḻakkam. ataṟku irāmaṭam ūṭṭutal eṉṟu peyar.
vaṭamoḻiyil maṭam eṉṉum teṉcol maṭa (mat%|ha) eṉṟu tiriyum. ataṟku,
“a hut, cottage, (esp.) the retired hut (or cell) of an ascetic (or student), ..... a cloister, college (esp. for young Brahmans), temple .....” eṉṟu mā. vi. a. poruḷ kūṟum. itaṟku maṭ (mat|h) eṉpatai mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar. atu mūlamaṉṟu  eṉpatai,
“(prob. invented for the word below) eṉṟu mā. vi. a. kūṟuvatiṉiṉṟu terintu koḷka. vaṭavar kūṟum mūlapporuḷ varumāṟu :-
tātu pāṭam - to dwell or to be intoxicated. 
vōpatēvar - to grind.  (va.va.)
maṭalēṟṟam
mutupaḻakkālattil, oru kaṭuṅkātalaṉ allatu kātaṟpittaṉ avaṉ kātaliyai maṇattaṟku avaḷ peṟṟōr icaiyāviṭiṉ, avaḷaip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟṟam eṉṉum uyirccētattiṟ kiṭa māṉa oru vaṉmuṟaiyaik kaiyāḷvatuṇṭu. atu ikkālattuc cattiyākkirakameṉṉum pāṭu kiṭappup pōṉṟatu.
maṭalēṟat tuṇinta kātalaṉ, nīrccēlai oṉṟēyuṭuttu uṭam pelāñ cāmpaṟ pūci erukkamālai yaṇintu, taṉ kātaliyiṉ ūr naṭuvē tavanilaiyilamarntu, avaḷ uruvai varainta oru tuṇiyaik kaiyilēnti, atai uṟṟu nōkkiya vaṇṇamāy vāḷāviruppaṉ. ataṉaik kaṇṭa avvūrār nī āyvu (cōtaṉai) tarukiṉṟāyā? eṉakkēṭpar. avaṉ tarukiṉṟēṉ eṉiṉ, paṉaṅkarukku maṭṭaiyāṟ ceyta oru poyk kutirai yiṉ mēl avaṉaiyēṟṟit teru neṭuka iḻuttuc celvar. aṅṅaṉam iḻukkum pōtu, avaṉ uṭampiṟ karukkaṟuttuk kāyam paṭṭaviṭamellām veḷuttut tōṉṟiṉ avaṉukku avaṉ kātaliyai maṇamuṭittu vaippar; allākkāl vaiyār.  (ta.ti. 8,9)
maṇṭalam
maṇṭalam - maṇṭala
mul - muṉ - muṉi = vil. mul - vil. o.nō : muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
mul - mur - muri. murital = vaḷaital. muri - mūri = vaḷaivu.
mur - muru - muruku = piṟaipōl vaḷainta kātaṇi.
muru - muṟu = muṟuku. muṟukutal = vaḷaital, tirukutal.
muṟuku - muṟukku = tirukal, tirukiya tiṉpaṇṭam. muṟukku - muṟukkam.
muṟu - muṟṟu - muṟṟukai = cūḻkai.
muṟu - muṟai = vaḷaivu, taṭavai (turn). muṟai - miṟai = vaḷaivu.
muṟu - muṟi. muṟital = vaḷaital. muṟi - maṟi. maṟital = maṭaṅkutal.
mul - (muḷ) - muṇṭu = uruṭci, tiraṭci, muṇṭu - muṇṭai = uruṇṭai, muṭṭai. muṇṭai viḷaipaḻam (patiṟ. 60:6).
muḷ - muṭṭu - muṭṭai. muṭṭu - muṭṭāṉ - tirunīṟṟuruṇṭai.  (va.va.)
muḷ - (muṇ) - muṇam - muṇaṅku. muṇaṅkutal = uḷvaḷaital.
muṇam - muṭam = vaḷaivu, kāl vaḷaivu. muṭam - muṭavu - muṭavaṉ.
muṭam - muṭaṅku, muṭaṅkutal = vaḷaital. muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku. muṭakku - maṭakku. muṭakkam - maṭakkam.
muṭam - muṭantai = vaḷaintatu. muṭam - (muṭal) - muṭalai = kuṟaṭu, uruṇṭai.
muṭi - muṭiccu = vaḷaittuk kaṭṭiyatu, marat tirukal. muṇṭum muṭiccum eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
muṇṭu - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = vaḷaital. maṇṭu - maṇṭi. maṇṭi yiṭal = muḻaṅkālai maṭakkutal.
maṇṭu - maṇṭalam = vaṭṭam, nāṭṭuppakuti, kālappakuti, nūṟpakuti, vaṭṭavaṭivam, vaṭṭamāyc cuṟṟivarukai, vaṭṭamāṉa poruḷ.  (va.va.)
koṅku maṇṭalam, toṇṭai maṇṭalam eṉpaṉa toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakku.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭali. maṇṭalittal = vaṭṭamātal, vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟivarutal, īṟu toṭaṅkiyil (antātiyil) īṟum mutalum oṉṟittu varutal.
maṇṭali = vaṭṭamāṉa poṟikaḷuḷḷa pāmpu.
maṇṭalam - maṇṭilam = 1. vaṭṭam. 2. vaṭṭamāṉa iṭam.
parippuc cūḻnta maṇṭilamum  (puṟam. 30)
3.	vaṭṭamāṉa katiravaṉ. pakalcey maṇṭilam  (perumpāṇ. 442).

maṇ - maṇi = nāḻikai vaṭṭil, maṇiyaṭikkum vaṭṭamāṉa veṇkalat taṭṭu, maṇiyeṉṉuṅ kāla aḷavu, uruṇṭai, uruṇṭaiyāṉa vitai.

maṇa nāḷaṉṟē maṇamakkaḷaik kūṭṭutal
cila peṟṟōr, maṇattiṉ nōkkattaiyē uṇarātu nīṇṭa kāla māka iṉpantuyttu veṟutta taṅkaḷaip pōṉṟē iḷa maṇa makkaḷaiyuṅ karutik koḷkiṉṟaṉar. kaḷa voḻukka iṉpam vāykkāta maṇamakkaṭkellām maṇanāḷiṉpamē ciṟantatu eṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum. mēlum maṇanāḷiṟ kūṭāmaiyāl, oḻukkakkēṭu, aiyuṟavu, matiyāmai, aṉpiṉmai, nīṭukūṭi vāḻāmai mutaliyaṉa irucārum ēṟpaṭutaṟkiṭamām.  (ta.ti.59).
maṇamakkaḷ
kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyumāka oruvarukkoruvar vāḻkkait tuṇaivarāy illaṟam toṭaṅkum iruvarum, tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. avviruvaraiyum pirittuk kūṟuṅkāl, maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷ eṉṟum, maṇavāḷaṉ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṟum, peṇ māppiḷḷai (maṇavāḷappiḷḷai) eṉṟum, peṇ piḷḷai eṉṟum, kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. maṇamakaṉaip piḷḷai eṉpatu vaṭārkkāṭu vaḻakku.  (ta.ti.muṉ. vi).
kulaveṟi mella mellac cuṟṟaveṟiyai uṇṭu paṇṇiṟṟu oru muṟai kārap peṇṇukku allatu āṇukku, eṅkē vēṟiṭattil maṇam muṭintuviṭukiṉṟatō eṉṉum accattiṉāl, piḷḷaip paruvattilēyē avarkku maṇam ceytu vaippatu vaḻakkamāy viṭṭatu. cilar toṭṭiṟ kuḻantaikkum maṇam ceytu vaittatuṇṭu. atu toṭṭil maṇam eṉappaṭṭatu. orutti kaṉṉiyāy iṟantāl tīkkatiyaṭaivāḷ eṉṉum āriyak koḷkaiyum, iḷamai maṇattiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.  (ta.ti.37).
maṇāṭṭuppeṇ
maṇāṭṭup peṇ eṉpatu muṟaiyē māṭṭuppeṇ nāṭṭup peṇ eṉa maruvi, marumakaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil, tañcai vaṭṭārattil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.  (ta.ti. muṉ. vi).

mataṅkam
mataṅkam - mrutaṅka
mataṅkam eṉpatu mattaḷattiṉ ciṟumai. iṭakkaṇṇaiyum valakkaṇṇai yum toṭarntu taṭṭuvatāl ēṟpaṭum oliyiṉiṉṟu ippeyar peṟṟatu.
mataṅkamoṭu tuntupi ... muḻaṅkavē  (tiruvāta. pu. kaṭavuḷ. 1).
mrutaṅka eṉṉum col piṉvarumāṟu iruvakaiyuḷ oṉṟil tōṉṟiyirukkalāmeṉṟu mā. vi. a. kūṟum.
(1) mrutam + ka = aṭikkumpōtu celvatu (“going about while being beaten”)
ipporuḷil ka (ga) eṉpatu kam (gam) eṉpataṉ kaṭaik kuṟai.
(2) martala - mrutaṅka.
āriyar varumuṉpē tamiḻ muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉpatum mataṅkam āṭṭiṟkum pāṭṭiṟkum uriya icaittamiḻk karuvi yeṉpatum, aṟika.  (va.va.217).
mataccīrtiruttam
1. tirukkōvil tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu
kiṟittavam ki.pi. 3 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu urōma nakarattiṉiṉṟu pirit tāṉukkuṭ pukuttappaṭṭatu. ataip pukuttiyavar maṟaimoḻi ilattīṉamā yiruntataṉāl, kōvil vaḻipāṭu am moḻiyilē yē naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu. āyiṉ, 16 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, mārṭṭiṉ luttar eṉṉum cerumāṉiyar, pōppāṇṭavariṉ atikārattai yetirttup peruṅ kiḷarcci ceytu cīrtirut tattai yēṟpaṭuttiya piṉ, ellā nāṭukaḷilu muḷḷa cīrtiruttak kiṟittavak kōvilkaḷil, avvan nāṭṭut tāymoḻiyilēyē vaḻipāṭu naṭantu varukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam, airōppiyarum āṅki larum, tam urimai yuṇarcciyāl, ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ayaṉ moḻi vāyilāka vantamatattaiyum, tam tāymoḻi matamāka māṟṟik koṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻarō, tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻarāl tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭa mata vaḻipāṭṭai, orupōtum, vaḻakkil illāta, oruvarkkum teriyāta ayal nāṭṭu ilakkiya moḻiyil naṭaipeṟuvittu, ceviṭar pōl niṉṟu mīḷkiṉṟaṉar.
civaṉiyamum māliyamum tūya tamiḻa mataṅkaḷā yiruntum, aṉpāṉa tantaiyuṭaṉ avaṉ arumai makkaḷ neñcu kalantu nēraṭiyāyp pēci iṉpuṟa muṭiyāvāṟu, iṭaiyil ōr ayalāṉ niṉṟu taṭuttu, avarkaḷ koṇṭu vanta tiṉpaṇṭaṅ kaḷaiyum tāṉē vāṅkik koṭuttu, avarkaḷ tantaiyaip pōṟṟip pukaḻvatu pōlum, tantai avarkaḷai vāḻttuvatu pōlum, tāṉē maṉappāṭañ ceytu koṇṭaṉavum avar kaṭkup poruḷ teriyātaṉavumāṉa cila toṭarkaḷaic colli, ataṉpiṉ avarkaḷē vilakik koḷḷumāṟu ceyvatupōl, kōvililuḷḷa paramat tirut tantaiyum aruṭ kaṭalumāṉa iṟaivaṉuruviṟku, tamiḻar tāmē tam aṉpārnta neñcu kaṉinta vaṇakkattait tam tāymoḻiyil terivittu vaḻipaṭṭup paṭaittu, pēriṉpap peru makiḻcci yaip peṟa muṭiyāvāṟu, vaṭanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu vanta pirāmaṇaṉ iṭainiṉṟu, paṭaipput tēṅkāyai vāṅkit tāṉē yuṭaittu, tamiḻarkkut teriyāta, taṉakkun teḷivāka viḷaṅkāta, araic ceyaṟkai ilakkiya naṭaimoḻiyākiya camaṟkirut til tāṉ uruppōṭṭataic colli, añcalakattilum vaippakattilum (Bank) variyakattilum paṇañ celuttiyavar tirumpuvatu pōl vaḻipaṭṭavarait tirumpac ceyvatu, ettuṇai ēmāṟṟum tuṇiccalumāṉa tīc ceyalām! āṅkilap paṭṭak kalviyarum aṟiviyal ārāycciyāḷarum itaṟku iṇaṅki yiruppatu, ettuṇai iḻi takavāṉa aṭimait taṉamām!
tamiḻar iruvakaic caṭaṅkukaḷaiyum kōvil vaḻi pāṭṭaiyum tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟac ceytal vēṇṭum.
potuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷil, avait talaivar ‘ammaiyappaṉ tiruvaṭipōṟṟi! eṉṟē olikka. avaiyiṉar ‘arakara (aravara) mātēvā! eṉṟu ārpparikka.
2. pal vakuppuk kōviṟ pūcakar
potuk kalvi, ciṟappuk kalvi, tamiḻp paṟṟu, teyva nampikkai, pulāṉ maṟuttal, tuppuravu, oḻukkam ākiya eḻuvakait takutiyum uḷḷavarai, ellā vakuppi ṉiṉṟum pūcakarākat terinteṭuttup paṇi yāṟṟuvittal vēṇṭum. itaṟku māṟā yiruppavarai aracu taṇṭittalum vēṇṭum.
3. vaḻipāṭṭil vīṇ celavu niṟuttam
kōvilkaḷiṟ paṭaikkappaṭum uṇavup poruṭkaḷ, vaḻipaṭu vārkkum pūcakarkkum irappōrkkum ēḻaikaṭkum payaṉpaṭum. āviṉ, pāl muḻukku, neyyāṭṭup pōṉṟavai oruvarkkum payaṉpaṭā. iṟaivaṉukkum ēṟkātu. makkaṭ ṭokaip perukka mum vēlai yillāt tiṇṭāṭṭamum poruḷ vilai yuyarvum vaṟumaiyum vaṟaṭciyum mikka ikkālattil, ēḻai makkaḷ kuṭikkak kūḻumiṉṟip paciyum paṭṭiṉiyumāyk kiṭantu varuntu kaiyilum, taṟkolai ceytu varukaiyilum, uyiraṟṟa ōruruvat tiṉ mēṟ kuṭaṅ kuṭamāyp pālaik koṭṭupavaṉai, iṟaivaṉ aṭiyāṉ eṉṉātu koṭiyāṉ eṉṟē karutuvaṉ. iṟai vaṉukku eṇṇillā ellā vulakum eṉṟum contam. avaṉukku oru poruḷum tēvai yillai. avaṉē ellārkkum ellāvaṟṟaiyum aḷippavaṉ. avaṉ virumpuvatu maṉam oṉṟē. atai muḻumaiyum aḷikka muṭiyātavar, pūcakarkkum ēḻai kaṭkum palvakaip paṇi ceyyum potu makkaṭkum payaṉ paṭumāṟu, tat tamakku iyaṉṟavaḷavu palvakaip poruḷkaḷaik kāṇikkai yākap paṭaikkalām.
4. uruvilā vaḻipāṭu
tuṟavaṟattārkku maṭṭumaṉṟi, aṟivu viḷakkam peṟṟa illaṟat tārkkum uruvilā vaḻipāṭē ukantatām. uruva vaḻipāṭṭi ṉāl, iṟaivaṉiṉ eṅkum niṟainta taṉmai uṇarappaṭātu pōm. atōṭu, avaṉatu aḷaviṭappaṭāta perumaikkum atu iḻukkākum.
ulakiluḷḷa uyiriṉaṅkaḷuḷ uyarntatu māntaṉiṉam. ātalāl, talaiciṟanta vakaiyilum, māntaṉ vaṭivil tāṉ iṟaivaṉaik kāṭṭa muṭiyum. atuvē avaṉ māṭcimaikku iḻukkā yirukka, pakuttaṟi villā yāṉai vaṭiviṟ paṭivamamaittu vaṇaṅkuvatu ettuṇaip paḻippākum! oruvar paṭattait tavaṟāka varaiyiṉ, avarukku ettuṇaic ciṉam eḻukiṉṟatu! māntaṉukkē aṅṅaṉa māyiṉ iṟaivaṉukku eṅṅaṉamirukkum!
kaciṉi makamatu kaṟ paṭimaikaḷai yuṭaittup poṟ paṭimaikaḷai urukkuvāṉ; caṇpakak kaṇ nampi ceppup paṭimaiyait tiruṭik kāṭu mēṭāy iḻuttuc ceṉṟu viṟpāṉ, paṭimai oṉṟuñ ceyyātu.
viḷḷuvamō cīrācai vīṭuviṭṭuk kāṭutaṉil
naḷḷiruḷiṟ ceṇpakakkaṇ nampiyāṉ - meḷḷavē
āṭeṭukkuṅ kaḷvaraippō lañcā temaikkaricaṟ
kāṭutoṟu mēyiḻuttak kāl.
oruvaṉatu teyvap paṭimaiyē avaṉ vīṭṭup paṭikkallāka vantu amaiyiṉum amaiyalām.
illaṟa makkaṭku uruva vaḻipāṭu iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉpar cilar. kiṟittavarum makamatiyarum uruvilā vaḻipāṭṭiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kāṇka.
tuṟavaṟam uyarnta aṟivu neṟi yātalāl paṭṭiṉattārum tāyumāṉavarum pōṉṟa uṇmait tuṟaviya rellām, uruva vaḻipāṭṭai aṟiyāmaic ceyalākavē karutiṉar.
iṟaivaṉukku neñcak kōyilē iṉiya teṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum tirumūlarum kaṇṭaṉar.
malarmicai yēkiṉāṉ māṇaṭi cērntār
nilamicai nīṭuvāḻ vār  (kuṟaḷ. 3)
uṭampiṉai muṉṉam iḻukkeṉ ṟiruntēṉ
uṭampiṉuk kuḷḷē yuṟuporuḷ kaṇṭēṉ
uṭampuḷē yuttamaṉ kōyilkoṇ ṭāṉeṉ
ṟuṭampiṉai yāṉirun tōmpukiṉ ṟēṉē.  (tiruman. 705)
paṭamāṭak kōyil pakavaṟkoṉ ṟīyil
naṭamāṭak kōyil namparkkaṅ kākā
naṭamāṭak kōyil namparkkoṉ ṟīyil
paṭamāṭak kōyil pakavaṟka tāmē  ( 1821)
matattai yoḻittu viṭṭāṟ pirāmaṇiyam aṭiyōṭoḻiyu meṉṟu karuti, kaṭavuḷillai yeṉṟu cilar colli varukiṉṟaṉar. uruva vaṇakkattai oḻittu viṭṭālē pirāmaṇiyam oḻintu pōmeṉṟu uṟutiyāyc collalām.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil uruvilā vaḻipāṭu patiṉeṇ cittar kālattil tōṉṟiya teṉṟum, kiṟittuviṟkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, tavaṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ulakil mutaṉ mutal kaṭavuḷai uṇarntavar tamiḻaṟivarē. kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum col, kumari nāṭṭil talaik kaḻakak kālattilēyē tōṉṟiya tākum. muntu nūl kaṇṭu muṟaippaṭa eṇṇip pulan tokukkappaṭṭa tolkāppiyattil, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉuñ col āḷappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
āriyar vantapiṉ, kaṭavuṭ camayam maṟaikkap paṭṭatu; kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum colliṉ poruḷum kuṟaikkap paṭṭatu.
5.  intu matam eṉṉātu teṉmatam eṉal
1.	intu eṉpatu vaṭanāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟiṉ peyarāṉa cintu eṉpataṉ tiripu.
	cintu = ōr āṟu, atu pāyum nāṭu. cintu eṉṉum peyar pāracīkattil hintu eṉat tirintatu. piṟkālattil atiṉiṉṟu intiyā eṉṉum peyar kirēkkattil tōṉṟiṟṟu.
	vēta āriyar mutaṉ mutal cintu veḷiyil taṅki yiruntataṉāl, hintu eṉpatu nāḷaṭaivil nāvalan tēyam muḻumaikkum peyarāyiṟṟu.
	āriyar tam vēḷvi matattoṭu tamiḻa mataṅkaḷaiyum iṇaittu, ak kalavaiyait tamateṉṟu kāṭṭi, intiyā muḻutum paravi intiyar ellārum ak kalavai matattait taḻuvumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭataṉāl, hintu eṉṉum tēcap peyar, at tēca makkaḷaiyum am makkaḷ matattaiyum kuṟikkalāyiṟṟu. atu tamiḻil intu eṉat tirintatu.
2. 	intu matam eṉpatu, oru taṉi matamaṉṟi āriyamum tamiḻamuñ cērnta kalavai matam.
3. 	intu matat teyvaṅkaḷāṉa muttiru mēṉikaḷuḷ, paṭaipput toḻilaṉāṉa nāṉ mukaṉ (piramaṉ) eṉṉum āriyat teyvattai, tamiḻar toṭakkan toṭṭu oppuk koḷḷa villai. avaṉ civaṉaiyē ēmāṟṟiṉa paṭumōcap poyyaṉ eṉṟum, ōṅkārap poruḷ teriyātu murukaṉāṟ kuṭṭup paṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, kataikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
4.	civaṉ eṉṟum tirumāl eṉṟum iruvēṟu peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum orē iṟaivaṉ, kāpput teyva meṉṟum aḻipput teyva meṉṟum iru vēṟu teyvaṅkaḷākak kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷāṉ.
5.	mut toḻil iṟaivaṉāṉa civaṉ allatu tirumāl, orē toḻil talaivaṉākak kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷāṉ.
6.	orē iṟaivaṉai iru vēṟu teyvamākak kāṭṭiyatāl, civaṉ periyavaṉā, tirumāl periyavaṉā eṉṉum matap pōr mūṇṭuḷḷatu.
7.	poyyum puraṭṭumāṉa pala kataikaḷum koḷkaikaḷum kūṟṟukkaḷum, āriyat toṉmaṅkaḷilum koṇ muṭipiyali lum meyp poruḷiyalilum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
8.	āriyaṉ nilat tēvaṉ eṉṟu uyarttap paṭṭum, tamiḻaṉ cūttiraṉ eṉṟu tāḻttap paṭṭum, uḷḷaṉar.
9. 	intu matattāl tamiḻap paṇpāṭu aḻikkap paṭukiṉṟatu.
10. intu matattāl tamiḻ vaḻipāṭu etirkkap paṭukiṉṟatu.
ātalāl, iṉimēl, civa neṟiyāṉ taṉṉaic civaṉiyaṉ eṉṟum, tirumāl neṟiyāṉ taṉṉai māliyaṉ eṉṟumē, kuṟittal vēṇṭum. iru neṟikkum potumai kuṟikka virumpiṉ, teṉ matattāṉ allatu tamiḻa matattāṉ eṉṟu kuṟittal vēṇṭum.
intu uyarnilaip paḷḷiyeṉṟum, intuk kallūri yeṉṟum, tamiḻ nāṭeṅkum irukkum kalvi nilaiyap peyarkaḷait teṉṉavar uyarnilaip paḷḷi, teṉṉavar kallūri yeṉṟu māṟṟal vēṇṭum. mūtta talai muṟaiyaic cērnta periyōr  perumpālum aṭimaiyarāyum pēṭiyarāyum iruttalāl, iḷantalai muṟaiyaic cērnta māṇavarē peruṅ kiḷarcci ceytu im māṟṟattaic ceytal vēṇṭum. intu eṉṉuñ col āriya aṭimait taṉattaik kuṟittalāl, urimait tamiḻar accollāl tammaik kuṟittal peru māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalākum.
mutaṟkaṇ, centamiḻp pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉ talai nakarākiya tirunelvēliyil uḷḷa, tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar intuk kallūriyiṉ peyarai, tiraviyam tāyumāṉavar teṉṉavar kallūriyeṉṟu nellait tamiḻa māṇavar māṟṟi, ēṉaik kalvi nilaiya māṇavarkkum nalvaḻi kāṭṭuvārāka.
6. ellā matamum taṉittamiḻ pōṟṟal
kaṭavuḷ puṟak kaṇṇāṟ kāṇap paṭāmaiyālum, matam maṟumaiyai nōkkiya tātalālum, makkaḷ maṉappāṉmai palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭiruppataṉālum, uḷnāṭṭu mata meṉṟum veḷi nāṭṭu matameṉṟum vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭātu karuttu vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku iṭan tantu, taṉit tamiḻaip pōṟṟuvataiyē ellā matattārum kaṭaippiṭittal vēṇṭum.
matattai aḻikka muṭiyumā?
kaṭavuḷ nampikkai yillāta taṟperumai vēntarum, neṟi tappiya aṟiviya lārāycciyāḷarum, potuvuṭaimaik koḷkaiyiṉarum, kaṭavuṭ koḷkaiyai oḻikkat tammāl iyaṉṟavarai muyaṉṟu vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. mataviyal muṟṟum maṉattaip poṟutta tātaliṉ, atai evarum aḻikka viyalātu cākkiya nāyaṉār pakaivar pōṉṟu civap paṭimaiyaik kallālaṭittē, civaṉaṭiyā rāyiṉār eṉiṉ, vēṟu eṉṉa collavirukkiṉṟatu! 
mēlum, potuvuṭaimai iṟaivaṉuk kēṟṟatē. makka ḷellārkkum uṇa veṉiṉ, tantai makiḻattāṉē ceyvāṉ! aṅṅaṉamē paramat tantaiyum.
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka vulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 1062)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. kiṟittavar etir nōkkum āyiravāṇṭu aracāṭciyum potuvuṭaimai vakaiyil tāṉ iyalum.
kaṉavu kāṇātu uṟaṅkum nēram tavira, maṟṟellā nērattilum - iruppiṉum, naṭappiṉum, vēlai ceyyiṉum uraiyāṭiṉum uṇṇiṉum - iṟaivaṉai niṉaikkavum vaḻuttavum vēṇṭavum iyalu mātaliṉ, maṉam uḷḷavarai matattai oruvarālum aḻikka muṭiyā teṉa aṟika.  (ta.ma.)
matam eṉṉum col varalāṟu
āṟu, neṟi, koḷkai, matam, camayam eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟuṭ piṉṉiraṇṭum vaṭamoḻi yeṉṉum camaṟkirutattilum vaḻaṅkuvatālum, ammoḻi piṟamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koḷḷāta tēvamoḻi yeṉṉum nampikkai iṭaikkālat tamiḻaruḷḷattiṟ patikkap paṭṭiruntataṉālum, avviru collum vaṭacollē yeṉṟu iṉṟum karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
muttutal = 1. poruntutal allatu cērtal. tiru muttu āram = tirumakaḷ cēru māram; kaḻai pōy viṇ muttum eṉṟāṟpōla. (cīvaka. 504, nac. urai) 2. meṉmaiyākat toṭutal. 3. muttamiṭutal. putalvar pūṅkaṇ mutti (puṟam. 41).
muttu - muṭṭu. muṭṭutal = 1. vaṉmaiyākat toṭutal, mōtutal. tuḷḷit tūṇ muṭṭumāṅ kīḻ (nālaṭi. 94). 2. muṭṭit tāṅkutal. 3. muṭital. muṭṭaṭi yiṉṟik kuṟaivu cīrttākiyum (tol. po. 435). 1. muṭṭu.
takaram meṉmaiyaiyum ṭakaram vaṉmaiyaiyum uṇarttum. o.nō: ottu - oṭṭu, kuttu-kuṭṭu. muṭṭu = tākku, poruttu, muṭivu.
muṭṭu - maṭṭu - muṭivu, aḷavu. muttu - mattu = aḷavu. mattu - matti - mati.
matittal = 1. aḷaviṭutal. maṇviḻaintu vāḻnāṇ matiyāmai (tirikaṭu. 26). 2. karututal. āṭalai matittāṉ (kantapu. tiruviḷai. 1). 3. poruṭ paṭuttutal. maṇṇāḷvāṉ matittumirēṉ (tiruvāca. 5:12).
mati = aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam. e.ṭu. ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati.
mati - matippu = 1. aḷaviṭukai. 2. poruṭ paṭuttukai, kaṇṇiyap paṭuttukai. umaiyaic cāla matippoḻinta vallamarar māṇṭār (tēvā. 1014 : 7). 3. karuttu. maṇṇula kāḷu matippai yoḻittē (piramōt. 8 : 99). tōrāyam. 5. kaṇṇiyam. te. matimpu.
mati - matam = 1. peritāka matippatu. 2. karuttu. 3. koḷkai. neṟimuṟai. 5. camayam. tatta mataṅkaḷē yamaivatāka. (tiruvāca. 4. 52). va. mata. (mata).
mata eṉṉum camaṟkirutac collai maṉ eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirippar. maṉ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu, māṉ, mā, māṉam mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟakkumē yaṉṟi mati allatu matam eṉṉum col tōṉṟātu.
kaṭaitalaik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum viṉaic collum, mattu eṉṉum, vaṭiviṉiṉṟē tirintiruttalai nōkkuka. muttu - mottu - mattu = mottaiyāka virukkum kaṭaikaruvi.
mattu - matti, mattittal = mattiṉāṟ kaṭaital. matti - mati. matittal = 1. kaṭaital. mantaraṅ koṭu matitta nāḷ cētupu. caṅkara. 20). 2. kaṭaital pōl viralā laḻutti maciyac ceytal .. mati - maci. macital = kaṭaintatu pōṟ kaḷiyātal. kuḻantaikkuc cōṟṟai matittu ūṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
mattu eṉṉum peyarai manta (mantha) eṉṟum, mattittal eṉṉum viṉaiyai mat (math) eṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil tirittirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṟku vēr tamiḻilēyē yaṉṟi vaṭamoḻiyi lillai.
aḷattaṟ poruḷ kuṟitta mete eṉṉum āṅkila viṉaic col kavaṉikkat takkatu. E mete (to measure), OE, OS metan ON meta, Goth. mitan, OHG mezzan.
E. meter = person or thing that measures. Metron (measure) eṉṉum kirēkkac col māttirai eṉpataṉ tiripu. mā - māttiram - māttirai - va. mātrā. māttal eṉṉum viṉai tamiḻil vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
eḻuvakai matamē uṭaṉpaṭal maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷaivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukōḷ orutalai tuṇivē
piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭal ēṉaip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ taṉmataṅ koḷalē
eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉiṉṟu (11), oru poruḷ allatu neṟimuṟai (principle) iṉṉavā ṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu matittuk koḷvatē matam eṉṟum, camayam matameṉṉum peyar peṟṟatum ivvakaiyilēyē eṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
teyvam uṇmai yiṉmai, teyvattiṉ taṉmai, teyvattāl ēṟpaṭum naṉmai, teyvattiṟkum māntaṉukkum uḷḷa uṟavu, maṟumai yuṇmaiyiṉmai, maṟumaiyil irukkum vāḻvunilai ākiyavaṟṟaip paṟṟi iṉṉavāṟirukku meṉṟu oruvar matittuk koḷvatē, potuvāka matameṉṟu collap paṭuvatākum.
teyvamum maṟumaiyum puṟakkaṇṇāṟ kāṇappaṭāta poruḷkaḷātalālum, makkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmai vevvēṟu vakaippaṭṭiruppataṉālum, matamāṉatu, ovvoruvarum tattam aṟiviṟkum cūḻnilaikkum ēṟṟavāṟu matittuk koḷvatākavē yuḷḷatu.
teyvam uṇṭeṉṟu koḷvatu pōṉṟē, illaiyeṉṟu koḷvatum, matamākum. ākavē, nampu matam nampā matam eṉa matam iruvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala tiṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, nampu matap pirivukaḷē mikap paṟpala tiṟattaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa.  (ta.ma. : va.va.)
matam tōṉṟiya vakai
1. accam
kāykaṉi kiḻaṅku mutaliya iyaṟkai viḷaiporuḷaiyum vēṭṭaiyāṭiya vilaṅku paṟavai yiṟaicciyaiyum uṇṭu vāḻntu, anākarika nilaiyilirunta muntiyal māntar; tīyum iṭiyum pōṉṟa pūta iyaṟkaikkum, pāmpu pōlum naccuyirikkum, iṟantō rāvikaṭkum avaṟṟuḷ oru cāraṉavāṉa pēy kaṭkum, añciya accamē teyva vaṇakkattai allatu ciṟuteyva matattai mutaṟkaṇ tōṟṟuvittatu. kollun taṉmaiyuḷḷa ellāvaṟṟaiyum teyvamākak koṇṭu, avai tammaik kollāvāṟu iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyumākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷaip paṭaittum, irutiṇai yuyirikaḷaiyum kāvu koṭuttum, vantaṉar.
nākarikam mutirntuḷḷa ikkālattum, immaiyilum maṟumai yilum nērakkūṭiya tuṉpaṅkaṭku añcuvatē, palvēṟu mata voḻukkaṅkaṭkum perumpālum aṭippaṭaik karaṇiyamā yirukkiṉṟatu.
accattiṉāṟ paṭaippatellām tīmaivilakkalaik kuṟik kōḷākak koṇṭatu.
2. muṟkāppu
māntaṉ vāḻkkai palvakait tuṉpam niṟaintataṉāl, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟkut toṉṟu toṭṭup pātukāpput tēṭikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. uṭamaikaḷaiyum uyiraiyum kāppataṟku muṉpu kāvaṟkāraṉum piṉpu aracaṉum ēṟpaṭṭaṉar. āyiṉ, makkaḷāl taṭukka muṭiyāta koḷḷai nōy, pañcam, kaṭuṅkāṟṟu, peruveḷḷam mutaliya tuṉpaṅkaḷait taṭuttaṟkum nīkkaṟkum, māntariṉattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa cila maṟaivāṉa teyvaṅkaḷē āṟṟaluḷḷavai yeṉṟu karuti, aṉaivarum avaṟṟai vaṇaṅkavum vaḻipaṭavum talaippaṭṭaṉar. attuṉpaṅkaḷ at teyvaṅkaḷiṉ cīṟṟattāl nērvaṉa veṉṟum nampi, avaṟṟiṟku añciṉar. ātalāl, muṟkāppu accattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatākum. 
tīyum pāmpum pēyum eṉṟumuḷḷaṉa. koḷḷainōy, pañcam mutaliyaṉa orōvoru kālattu nikaḻvaṉa.
3. naṉṟiyaṟivu
palvēṟu tīṅkukaṭkum accaṅkaṭkum peritum iṭantarum kāriruḷaip pōkki, uyirvāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavu tēṭavum iṉattāruṭaṉ uṟavāṭavum, pēraḷavāka utavum katiravaṉaiyum ciṟṟaḷavāka utavum tiṅkaḷaiyum; uṇṇak kāykaṉiyum taṅkat taṇṇiḻalum kuṭiyirukka uṟaiyuḷum utavum palvakaip paḻumaraṅkaḷaiyum; iḷamai mutal mutumai varai ellārkkum iṉṉuyirt tīmpāl utavum āvaiyum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum; teyvamākap pōṟṟiyatum vaṇaṅkiyatum naṉṟiyaṟivu paṟṟiyatākum. avvakai vaṇakkattaik kuṟitta kālantoṟum toṭarntu ceyvatu, mēṉmēlum naṉmai peṟalaik kuṟikkōḷākak koṇṭatākum. vaṇakka mellām paṭaippoṭu kūṭiyatē.
4. pārāṭṭu
iṉattaik kākkap pakaivaruṭaṉ pulipōṟ porutupaṭṭa, uyirīki (pirāṇattiyāki) yākiya taṟukaṇ maṟavaṉukkum, maḻai varuvittum, paḻukkak kāycciya poṉṉaik kaiyilēntiyum, tīyōraic cāvittum, ciṉattāl ūrai yerittum, uṭaṉ kaṭṭai yēṟiyum, kaṭuṅkaṟpaik kātta pattiṉip peṇṭiṟkum; kal naṭṭi viḻā veṭuttatu pārāṭṭup paṟṟiya tākum.
teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ
peyyeṉap peyyu maḻai.  (kuṟaḷ. 55)
eṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu, orucila nikaḻccikaḷaiyēṉum cāṉṟākak koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṭṭavaṉ kallum pattiṉik kallum iṉattār allatu payaṉ peṟṟavar naṭṭuc ciṟappuc ceyvatu, naṉṟiyaṟivaiyum orumaruṅku taḻuviyatē.
tīyāṉatu, kolluntaṉmaiyāl accattiṟkum, iruḷ pōkkiyum uṇavucamaikka vutaviyum koṭuvilaṅkukaḷai veruṭṭiyum kuḷirakaṟṟiyum naṉmai ceyvatāl naṉṟiyaṟiviṟkum, uriyatāyiṟṟu.
5. aṉpu
irutiṇaip pakaiyaiyum aḻittum, uṇaviṟku vaḻivakuttum, naṭunilaiyāka āṭci ceytum, kuṭikaḷai aravaṇaittuk kātta aracaṉ iṟanta piṉ, avaṉukkup paṭimaiyamaittup paṭaittu vaṇaṅkiyatu aṉpu paṟṟiyatākum. itiṉiṉṟē, viṇṇulaka vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) vaṇakkam tōṉṟiṟṟu.
6. karutukōḷ
mutaṟkālattil kuṟiñci nilattilēyē vāḻnta māntar, piṉṉar ēṉai nilaṅkaḷilum paraviyapiṉ, avvannilattiṟ kēṟpa ovvoru teyvan tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ, ovvoru perunilattiṟkum pērāṟṟiṟkum perun toḻilukkum perunaṉmaip pēṟṟiṟkum, kātaṟpaṇpiṟkum, cātaltīṅkiṟkum ovvoru teyvam iruppatākak karutappaṭṭatu.
kaṇṇāṟkāṇum iyaṟkaik kūṟukaḷum nikaḻccikaḷum tōṟṟaṅkaḷum āvikaḷum viṉaikaḷumaṉṟi, maṉattālēyē paṭaittuk koḷḷum teyvaṅ kaḷellām karutukōḷiṉ viḷaivēyākum.
nīrūṭṭiyum nīrāṭuvittum uṇavu viḷaittum palvakai yutavum āṟṟai naṉṟiyaṟivu paṟṟi vaṇaṅkuvatu vēṟu; ataṟkut taṉit teyvamuṇṭeṉṟu karutuvatu vēṟu.
7. aṟivuvaḷarcci
māntaṉ nākarikamaṭaintu aṟivu vaḷarntu paṇpāṭuṟṟa piṉ, maṟumaiyum kaṭavuḷuṇmaiyum kaṇṭu, palvēṟu mataṅkaḷaiyum camayaṅkaḷaiyum vakuttatu aṟivu vaḷarcciyākum.
makkaḷ maṉappāṅkum aṟivunilaiyum palvēṟu vakaip paṭṭiruppatāl, mata camayaṅkaḷum palvēṟāyiṉa.
aṟivuvaḷarcci yeṉṉum peyarukku muraṇāka, palvēṟu mūṭap paḻakkaṅkaḷum koḷkaikaḷum mataṅkaḷiṟ kalantirup patuṇmaiyē. avai perumpālum paḻaṅkālat taṉmaiyālum taṉṉalakkārariṉ cūḻcciyālum ēṟpaṭṭavai. ikkāla aṟivu nilaikkēṟpa, avaṟṟai iyaṉṟavarai nīkkik koḷḷal vēṇṭum.
matamum camayamum perumpālum nampikaiyaip poṟuttaṉa. (ta.ma.)
mati(1)
mati - mati
mati = 1. aḷavu, aḷavīṭu.
2. aḷantaṟiyum aṟivuk karaṇam.
matinuṭpam nūlō ṭuṭaiyārkku  (kuṟaḷ. 636)
3. pakuttaṟivu. matiyili maṭaneñcē (tiruvāca. 5: 33).
4. aṟivu. matimai cālā maruṭkai nāṉkē (tol. po. 255).
vaṭavar maṉ (karutu) eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu matam, mati eṉṉum coṟkaḷait tirippar. maṉ eṉpatu vēṟuvaḻic collāyiruppa tōṭu tūyatamiḻccol eṉpatu,  (va.va.218)
mati (2)
mati : muttutal mella muṭṭutal. muṭṭu  maṭṭu = aḷavu. muttu  mattu = aḷavu. mattu  matti  mati. matittal = aḷaviṭutal. aḷantaṟital. mati = aḷantaṟiyum akakkaraṇam, aḷaviṭap paṭum poruḷ, pakuttaṟivu, aṟivu. mati - mati, miti (va.).

kumpu = kūṭṭam.

kumar - kumaraṉ, kumari.
kuṟiñcinilat teyvamāṉa murukaṉum pālainilat teyvamāṉa kāḷiyum, eṉṟum iḷamaiyar eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē muṟaiyē kumaraṉ, kumari eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kāḷiyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭut teṉkōṭi malaiyum vaṭakōṭi yāṟum kumariyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. kumari malaiyiṉ peyarālēyē, mūḻkip pōṉa teṉkaṇṭattaik kumarikkaṇṭam eṉkiṉṟōm. kumarimalai yirunta kālam, āriyar eṉṟa iṉamē tōṉṟāta toṉmutu paṇṭaik kālamākum.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tamiḻaṉ toṉmaiyai muṟṟum maṟaittut tamiḻai vaṭamoḻiyiṉ vaḻiyatākak kāṭṭal vēṇṭi, piṉvarumāṟu kumaraṉ, kumariyeṉṉum iru coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭivaiyum poruḷaiyum tirittum mikuttum uḷḷaṉar.
kumaraṉ - kumāra = kuḻantai, paiyaṉ, iḷaiñaṉ, makaṉ (irukku vētam).
kumari = kumārī = ciṟumi, pattiliruntu paṉṉīrakavaip paṭṭavaḷ, iḷaiñai, makaḷ (atarvavētam).
iv viru coṟkaḷaiyum āriyac collākak kāṭṭumāṟu mūlattaiyum kīḻvarumāṟu tirittuḷḷaṉar.
ku + māra = eḷitāka iṟappatu.

kumaraṉ kumari eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭacolleṉṟu nīkkiviṭiṉ, vaṭa moḻiyāḷar kūṟṟai ottukkoṇṭatākavum, kumarimalai mūḻkiyatu āriya varukaikkup piṟpaṭṭatākavumē muṭital kāṇka.
