mati viḷakkam
colliyal (Etymology) moḻinūluḷ (Philology) aṭaṅku mēṉum, pala coṟkaḷ pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyirutta lāṉum, oru moḻic col vevvēṟu vakaiyil piṟa moḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, moḻiyārāycci ceyyātār collārāycciyil iṟaṅkutal takātu. moḻiyārāyccikku moḻi nūlilakkiyak kalvi maṭṭumaṉṟi palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa aṟivum coṟṟokuti yaṟivum vēṇṭum.
poruṭpāṭunōkkic coṟkaḷai.
(1) oru poruṭ pala col (Synonyms).
(2) pala poruḷoru col (Homonyms).
eṉa iru vakaiyākap pakuttaṉar paṇṭait tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar. ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē, oru poruṭcol, pala poruṭ col eṉak kuṟukkiyum vaḻaṅkalām. oru poruṭ col eṉpatu orēyoru poruḷuḷḷa col eṉṟum poruḷpaṭiṉum oru poruṭ palacol eṉpatu orumaikku ēṟkāmaiyāṉum. iraṭṭuṟaṟ coṟkaḷ pala iṭamnōkkip poruḷ vēṟu pāṭuṇarttalāṉum, oru poruṭcol eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku iraṭṭuṟaṉmai iḻukkākāteṉka.
iṉi, pala poruṭ collum.
(1) oru vērp pala poruṭcol (Homonymy).
(2) pala vērp pala poruṭcol (Polysemy).
eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. kaṭu eṉṉum col. mikuti, vaṉmai, vali, viraivu, kāram, koṭumai mutaliya pala poruḷai orē vērp poruḷaṭiyil uṇarttutalāl oru vērp pala poruṭ collum; māṇṭāṉ eṉṉum col, māṇ, māḷ eṉṉum iru vēṟu viṉaippakutikaḷiṉ paṭarkkai āṇpāl iṟanta kāla muṟṟātaliṉ pala vērp pala poruṭ collum; ākum.
oru poruṭ collum iṅṅaṉamē oru vēruṭaimaiyum pala vēruṭaimaiyumpaṟṟi iru tiṟappaṭum.
mati eṉṉum col, paruttal, kaṭaital, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, mayakkam, kaḷ, yāṉai, nilā, iṉimai, karuvāppaṭṭai, aḷavu, matippu, aṟivu, acaiccol mutaliya poruḷkaḷil oru vērp pala poruṭ collāṉa teṉ collum, peṇpāl aṭai moḻiyīṟāka variṉ, māṉ eṉṉum tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirinta vaṭa collum ākum. iv viraṇṭiṟkum evvakait toṭarpumillai. ivaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu :
muttu - mattu - matti - mati. u - a (aḷḷait tiripu).
muttutal = cērtal. (cīvaka. 504). mukattoṭu mukam cērtal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu kūṟukaḷ oṉṟucēriṉ tiraṭci yuṇṭākumātalāl, cērtaṟ karuttil tiraṭcik karuttut tōṉṟum.
cērē tiraṭci  (tol. col. 363)
muttu - muttai = tiraṭci (cūṭā), cōṟṟuruṇṭai.
muttai - mottai = parumaṉ, uruṇṭai.
mottai - mōttai = parutta veḷḷāṭṭukkaṭā.
muttu - mottu - mottam = tiraṭci, muḻumai.
muttu = tiraṇṭa vitai, maṇi, kiḷiñcilil viḷaiyuṅ kal, koppuḷam.
muttu - muttam = periya muttu, ic col vaṭamoḻiyil mukta veṉṟu tiriyum. am perumaip poruḷ īṟu.
muttu - mattu = tiraṇṭa tayir mutaliyaṉa kaṭaikaruvi. mattu - mattam āymakaḷ mattam piṇitta kayiṟu (kalit. 110).
mattu - matti, mattittal = kaṭaital.
matti - mati. matittal = kaṭaital, viralāl allatu kaiyāl nacukkip pacaipōlākkutal.
mati - maci. macital = pacaipōlātal (ta.vi.)
macittal = pacai pōlākkutal (pi.vi.)
mattu, mattam eṉpaṉa manta (mantha) eṉṟum; matti, mati eṉpaṉa mat (math) eṉṟum, maci eṉpatu maṣ eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
mattam = erumaikkaṭā, iccol vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum. potuvāka, ōr iyaṅkutiṇai uyiriyiṉ uṭampu paruttalāl koḻuttalum mataṅkoḷḷutalum uṇṭākum.
matittal = 	1. paruttal, upputal.
			2. koḻuttal cerukkutal
matittetir tevvā (iraku. mīṭci 37)
			3. mataṅkoḷḷutal.
matitta kaḷiṟṟiṉiṉ  (kamparā. pañcacē. 56)
mati = matayāṉai. kōṇ matittiṭar kiṭantaṉa, (kamparā. nākapāca. 136)
mati - matam = mikuti. perumai, valimai, vaḷam, cerukku, matanīr.
matam - mataṉ = mikuti, valimai, cerukku.
mattam = matam, mattamā = yāṉai, mattam - matam eṉṟumām.
mattaṉ = koḻuttavaṉ. matta ṉirāvaṇaṉ kotittāṉ (irāmanā. uyut. 44).
mattam, mattaṉ eṉpaṉa matta eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. 
matam - matar. matarttal = mikutal, ceḻittal, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, cerukkutal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu vaḻikaḷ allatu karuttukkaḷ kalattalāl, kalakkamum mayakkamum uṇṭākum.
mattittal = kalattal, maruntu kalattal.
mattam = mayakkam. mattam - matam - mataṉ = kalakkam.
o.nō : kula - kulavu - kalavu, kalavutal = kalattal kulavutal = kūṭutal. kula - kala - kalaṅku - kalakku - kalakkam. muya - muyaṅku - muyakkam. muyaṅkutal = taḻuvutal, kalattal. muya - maya - mayaṅku - mayakku - mayakkam = kūṭṭam, kalakkam. mayattal = mayaṅkutal. mayantuḷēṉulaka vāḻkkaiyai (aruṭpā. apayattiṟaṉ. 14).
mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu, uṇarviṉmai (mūrccai) veṟi, kōṭṭi
(paittiyam), kāmam ākiya karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum.
mattam = mayakkam, kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi, ūmattai.
mattam - mattaṉ = matimayaṅkiyavaṉ, kōṭṭi piṭittavaṉ.
mattam - matam = kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi.
mattu = ūmattai, matattal = mayaṅkutal.
mattu, mattam, mattaṉ eṉṉum im muccollum vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum; mata eṉpatu mada eṉat tiriyum.
cerukku. yāṉai matam ākiya iru karuttum mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākavuṅ koḷḷalām. o.nō: kaḷi-kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal = kaḷḷuṇṭu veṟittal, mayaṅkutal.
nilavoḷiyāl kōṭṭi yuṇṭākumeṉṟu paṇṭaik kālattil kīḻ nāṭṭilum mēlnāṭṭilum oru karuttiruntatāl, tiṅkaḷukku mayakkan taruvatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
mattu - (matti) - mati: allatu matam - mati = tiṅkaḷ.
Luna (Latin) = tiṅkaḷ, Lunacy (Eng.) = kōṭṭi.
mati = mātam (matiyāl aḷakkappaṭum kāla aḷavu).
mati - mātam - māsa (va.).
matip peyariṉiṉṟē mātap peyar uṇṭākum. tiṅkaḷ = mati, mātam, (Moon-month). mati eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil tiṅkaṭ poruḷ illai. iyalpākat takaram cakaramākat tiriyumēyaṉṟic cakaram takaramākat tiriyātu. ātalāl, mātam eṉpatu teṉ collātal teḷivu. āyiṉum, āriya mayakkāl tamiḻar pētuṟuvatu avar pētaimaiyē. ēṟkeṉavē tiṅkaṭ col matiyaiyum oru kiḻamaiyaiyum kuṟittalāl, atai mēlumoru poruḷil vaḻaṅki mayakkutal vaḻuvākum.
mayakkan tarum kaḷ allatu tēṉ iṉittalāl, mayakkak karuttil matuk karuttum tōṉṟum.
mattu - maṭṭu = tēṉ, kaḷ.
mattu - matu - matur - maturi - maturam.
matu - matam = tēṉ. mataṅkamaḻ kōtai (cīvaka. 2584).
matu = tēṉ, kaḷ, iṉimai, atimaturam.
matu - mati = atimaturam.
maṭṭu eṉpatu madya eṉṟum, matu eṉpatu madhu eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
iṉi, muttu eṉpataṉ valvaṭivāṉa muṭṭu eṉṉum col, oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷai muṭṭutalāl aṟiyappaṭum iyakka muṭivai allatu vaḻi ellaiyai uṇarttutalāl, cērtal allatu poruntutaṟ karuttil ellai allatu aḷavukkaruttum tōṉṟum. mukattāṟ poruntutal muttutalum talaiyāṟ poruntutal muṭṭutalum ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ, meṉmai vaṉmai kuṟikka, muṟaiyē takara ṭakaram vantaṉaveṉa aṟika. iṅṅaṉamē muṭṭiyāl mutukil aṟaital kuttutalum maṇṭaiyil aṟaital kuṭṭutalum ātal kāṇka. āyiṉum, mutaṟkālattil takarac collē ṭakaraccoṟ poruḷaiyuṅ kuṟittatu.
muṭṭutal = muṭital.
muṭṭu - maṭṭu = muṭivu, ellai, aḷavu, maṭṭu - maṭṭam.
(mattu) - (matti) - mati.
matittal = aḷaviṭutal, uḷḷattāl aḷaviṭutal, uyarvākak karututal, karuttākak koḷḷutal.
matippu = ēṟattāḻak kaṇippu (kaṇicam), uyarvu.
mati = matippu, aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam (ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati), aḷantaṟiyum pakuttaṟivu, aṟivuppulaṉ, mati - matam = koḷkai camayam, nūṟkoḷkai.
mati eṉṉum col muṉṉilai paṭarkkai yacainilaiyāy varuṅkāl, aḷavu eṉṉuṅ karuttil atu pōtum eṉṟēṉum, aṟivu eṉṉum karuttil atu aṟivākum eṉṟēṉum poruḷpaṭum. paḻañcēra nāṭākiya kēraḷattil, mati eṉpatu pōtum eṉṉum poruḷil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
matam (camayam) eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil mata eṉa vaḻaṅkum. aḷavīṭu eṉṉum karuttoḻittu, itukāṟuṅ kūṟiya ellāp poruḷkaḷilum, mati eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṭaiya mata, matam, mataṉ, matar mutaliya coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattoṭu pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa paṇṭaiyilakkiyattoṭu pala ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, maṟaintupōṉamaiyāl, pala coṟkōvaikaḷiṉ, iṭaiyilirunta aṇṭukaḷai iṉṟu kāṭṭamuṭiyavillai.
makaṉ eṉṉum col varumoḻiyākavum īṟākavum varuṅkāl māṉ eṉṟu maruvum.
e.ṭu. : marumakaṉ - marumāṉ, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē tirumakaṉ eṉpatu tirumāṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
tiru eṉpatai vaṭamoḻiyār srī eṉṟu tirittuk koṇṭatāl tirumāṉ eṉpatu srīmāṉ eṉat tirintatu. itaṉaic cīmāṉ eṉa eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta col lākkiṉar tamiḻar. tiruvaraṅkam - srīraṅkam - cīraṅkam eṉṉum tirivaiyum nōkkuka.
cīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl cīmāṭṭi yātalāl, srīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmāṭṭi yum tirumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl tirumāṭṭiyum ātal vēṇṭum. tiruvāṭṭi eṉpatu tiruvāḷaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumakaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumāṭṭi eṉṟum perumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl perumāṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. kalai marumāṭṭi (tiruviḷai, tirumaṇa 62). perumāṭṭi (tivā) perumāṉ - pirāṉ perumāṭṭi - pirāṭṭi ivai marūuc citaivu.
vāṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟu vat, vatī eṉṟu muṟaiyē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum tirittuk koṇṭatu pōṉṟē, māṉ eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟum mat, matī eṉṟu tirittuk koṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ātalāl, peṇpāṟ peyarkkut tirumati eṉṟu aṭaikoṭukkātu tirumāṭṭi eṉṟu koṭuttal vēṇṭum. srīmāṉ srīmatī eṉpaṉa vaṭa col vaṭivukaḷ. srīmatī eṉpatu eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākum pōtu tirumati eṉṟākum. iv vaṭivai vilakka vēṇṭum.
maturai
maturai - maturā (dh). mati - matirai. o. nō kuti - kutirai.
mati - matirai - maturai = matikkulattavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ mutal talainakar (teṉmaturai), kaṇṇaṉ āṇṭa vaṭamaturai, ap peyar peṟṟa kaṭaikkaḻakap pāṇṭiyar talainakar (vaikai maturai).
civaperumāṉ tam caṭaimuṭiyiluḷḷa tiṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu matuvaip poḻinta iṭam (vaikai) maturai eṉappeṟṟatu eṉṟum, matu eṉṉum aracaṉ āṇṭatiṉāl matupura eṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭapeyar matrā (vaṭa maturai) eṉat tirintateṉṟum, kāraṇaṅkāṭṭuvar.  (va.va.230).
maturait tamiḻk kaḻakam
tamiḻmoḻiyum ilakkiyamum nākarikamum paṇpāṭum varalāṟum pōṟṟutal paṟṟi, varukiṉṟa puttāṇṭu vāḻttup pōl 1981 caṉuvarit toṭakkattil, pāṭalmikka kūṭaluṟṟa māṭa maturaiyil paṉimalai yeḻucciyum kumarimalai muḻukkum kaṇṇetiril nikaḻntāṟpōl kāṭṭavirukkum tiraippaṭap piṭippum.
ciṟaivāṉ puṉaṟṟillaic ciṟṟam palattumeṉ cintaiyuḷḷum
uṟaivā ṉuyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇ ṭīntamiḻ
eṉṟu māṇikkavācakar pāṭiya tamiḻ vaḷarkkuṅ kaḻakam oṉṟu niṟuvum tiṭṭamum, ulakam pārāṭṭumaḷavu talaiciṟantaṉavākum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṉavupōl tōṉṟi maṟaiyum tiraippaṭattiṉum, naṉavāka nilaittu niṉṟu nāṭṭiṟku nalam payakkum kaḻaka niṟuvaṉam cālac ciṟanta teṉpataic colla vēṇṭuvatillai.
orē kaḻakam
paṇṭait tamiḻakam cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum mūvēntaka mākap pirintu, kumarimalaimutaṟ paṉimalai varai paraviyirunta tēṉum, pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum orē nāṭṭil, ovvoru kālat tilum orē kaḻakam iruntu vantatu. tamiḻulakeṅkaṇumuḷḷa talaimaip pulavarellām atiṟ kalantiruntaṉar.
talaiyiṭai kaṭaiyeṉa mukkaḻakam iruntatākac collap paṭiṉum, pāṇṭināṭṭilēyē pāṇṭiyarē kaḻakam niṟuvi vantatāl, uṇmaiyil am mūṉṟum oṉṟē. kaṭalkōḷāṟ pāṇṭiyarāṭci iṭaiyīṭupaṭṭatiṉālēyē, mūvēṟiṭattil mūvēṟu kālattil kaḻakam niṟuva nērntatu. iru kaṭalkōḷum nikaḻntirāviṭiṉ, talaikkaḻakam eṉṉum oṉṟē iṟutivarai toṭarntiruntirukkum.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭākiya kumarināṭu, tamiḻ tōṉṟiya nilamāta lālum, ayaṉmoḻi vāṭai immiyum vīcāta centamiḻ nāṭātalālum, pāṇṭiyarkkut tāymoḻip paṟṟut tatumpi vaḻintataṉālum, tamiḻmakaḷē kalaimakaḷumāyiruntata ṉālum, aṟivai vaḷarttal aracaṉ kaṭamaiyātalālum, pāṇṭiyarkkut tamiḻai vaḷarttup pōṟṟik kākkum kaḻakam niṟuvātiruttal iyalavillai.
17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta taḷavāy irakunāta cētupati yeṉṉum ciṟṟaracar paṉṉiru pulavaraip pōṟṟik kākkavum, in nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattil pālavanattam vēḷ pāṇṭit turaittēvar pulavar iruvaraip pōṟṟik kākkavum nūṟṟu varkku mēṟpaṭṭavarai āṭṭai viḻāviṟ kūṭṭavum āṟṟalarā yiruntirukkavum, pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉ intiyā aḷavu nilapparappuḷḷa kumarināṭṭai oru kuṭaikkīḻ āṇṭa pāṇṭi yaṉukkut tamiḻppaṟṟum, pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉmaraip pōṟṟik kākkum celvac ciṟappum iruntirāteṉṟu koḷvatu, ettuṇai iraṅkattakkatākum!
talaikkaḻakattil akattiyarum tolkāppiyarum iruntatillai. talaikkaḻakak kālam ki.mu. tōrā 10,000 - 5,000. akattiyar tamiḻakam vanta kālam ki.mu. tōrā. 1200. tolkāppiyar kālam ki.mu. 7 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻiṉ mutupaḻan toṉmaiyālum, varalāṟṟaṟivum kālavārāycciyum iṉmaiyiṉālum, mukkaḻaka varalāṟṟil muṉṉavaraiyum piṉṉavaraiyum orukālattavarāka mayakkiviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, kaḻakamē yiruntatillai yeṉpatu kāṭṭik koṭukkun taṉmaiyaittāṉ kāṭṭum.
nakara aṭippaṭaiyilum, māvaṭṭa aṭippaṭaiyilum, mānila aṭippaṭaiyilum, nāṭṭu aṭippaṭaiyilum, palvēṟu tamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷait tōṟṟiyiruppatai aṟavē nīkkiviṭṭu, paḻam pāṇṭināṭṭut tamiḻk kaḻakam pōṉṟu orē ulakap potu māperun tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuva vēṇṭum. ataṉ kiḷaikaḷ palvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil irukkalām.
nila varampiṉmai
tamiḻar pala nāṭukaḷilum tīvukaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruppatāl, tamiḻulakam aṉaittaiyum taḻuviya tamiḻk kaḻakamāyiruttal vēṇṭum.
tokai varampiṉmai
talaikkaḻakap pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar; iṭaik kaḻakap pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar, kaṭaikkaḻakap pulavar nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar. nilapparappiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu pulavar tokai vēṟupaṭṭiruntatu. maṟṟappaṭi, civaṉiyap pulavar eḻuvar, tirumāliyap pulavar eḻuvar, camaṇap pulavar eḻuvar, puttap pulavar eḻuvar, āriya matap pulavar eḻuvar, ulakiyal matap pulavar eḻuvar, matamaṟṟa pulavar eḻuvar eṉṟō, vēṟu vakaiyākavō pulavar tokai amaiyavillai. takutiyuṭaiyavar aṉaivarum iṭampeṟal vēṇṭum.
pulavar takuti
oru tamiḻppulavar kallūriyil māṇavarākap payiṉṟō taṉiyāka oru perum pulavariṭam kaṟṟō, palkalaikkaḻakaṅkaḷ naṭatti varum tērveḻutit tēṟip pulavar paṭṭam peṟṟavarkkuḷ, nuvalāciriyarākavō (pōtakāciriyarākavō), nūlāciriyarāka vō, uraiyāciriyarākavō, itaḻāciriyarākavō, ārāycciyā ḷarākavō pallāṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟi avaravar tuṟaiyil atikāri yeṉa aṟiñarāl oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭavarē, tamiḻppulavar kaḻaka vuṟuppiṉarākat takkār.
ilakkaṇa aṟivum pulavar paṭṭamum iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṭip paṭait takutiyākum. āṅkilattiṟ paṭṭam peṟṟavaraiyē oppuk koḷḷum muṟaiyait tamiḻilum kaṭaippiṭittal vēṇṭum. mēṭaip pēccāḷarākavō itaḻāciriyarākavō iruntāl maṭṭum pōtātu. ilakkaṇa aṟiviṟkup palkalaik kaḻakap pulavar paṭṭamē cāṉṟātal vēṇṭum. tērveḻutātu peṟum kaṇṇiyap (Honorary) paṭṭamum cāṉṟākātu.
varicaiyaṟital
pulamaic cāṉṟāṉa palvēṟu tiṟaṅkaḷuḷ, kaṭaikkaḻakac ceyyuṭkum paṉuvaṟkum urai varaiyum āṟṟal cālac ciṟantatu. nāvalar vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭārukkup piṉ aṭuttapaṭiyāka at tiṟamaiyuṭaiyār. mutuperum pulavar uraivēntar auvai cu. turaicāmippiḷḷaiyē. avarait talaivarākak koṇṭu pulavarait tērnteṭuppatu talaiciṟanta vakaiyākum.
pulamik kavaraip pulamai terital
pulamik kavarkkē pulaṉām - nalamikka
pūmpuṉa lūra potumakkaṭ kākātē
pāmpaṟi yumpāmpiṉ kāl.  (pā. 7)
avarukku aṭuttavar mutuperum pulavar vēṇukōpālap piḷḷaiyē.
tamiḻppulavaruḷ, vaiyāpuri (Heretic School) vaḻiyiṉar eṉṟum, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ vaḻiyiṉar (Orthodox School) eṉṟum, iru muraṇpaṭṭa kūṭṭattār uḷar. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ pōl muṟṟum taṉittamiḻē vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatai evarum maṟukkalām. āyiṉ, uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟṟai oruvarum maṟukkavō maṟaikkavō māṟṟavō oṇṇātu. ātalāl, camaṟkirutattiliruntu tamiḻ vantateṉṟum, aṭippaṭait tamiḻccoṟkaḷellām camaṟkirutameṉṟum, pāṇiṉīyattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭatu tolkāppiyam eṉṟum, cintuveḷi nākarikam āriyara teṉṟum, irukku vētattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatu tamiḻ eṉṟum, tamiḻar vaṭamoḻip parata cāttirattiṉiṉṟu naṭamum nāṭakamuṅ kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum, koccait tamiḻaiyum centamiḻpōṟ koḷḷavēṇṭu meṉṟum, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkut tōṉṟiṟ ṟeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻi yeṉṟum, pirāmaṇar nilattēvar eṉṟum, tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭu vaṭamoḻiyilēyē naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, intiyai etirttal kūṭāteṉṟum, tamiḻar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavar eṉṟum, piṟavāṟun tamiḻukku māṟākavum kēṭākavum col vārum eḻutuvārum ellām tamiḻkkaḻakattil iṭampeṟat takkārallar.
tamiḻai muḻuttūymaiyākap pēcāviṭiṉum, iyaṉṟavarai tūymaiyākap pēca muyala vēṇṭum: tamiḻppeyarun tāṅka vēṇṭum.
ulakap potumai
iṉa kula mata iṭa kaṭci vēṟupāṭiṉṟit takutiyoṉṟē paṟṟi, ellāruñ cērtaṟkēṟṟa kaḻakamā yiruttal vēṇṭum.
iruppiṭam maturai.
naṭaimuṟai
paltuṟaippaṭṭa tamiḻārāycciyē tamiḻkkaḻakattiṉ talaimaip paṇiyāyiruttal vēṇṭum. paṇṭait tamiḻkkaḻakaṅkaḷum ceyta paṇi ituvē. avar tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷap paṭṭa maturai yeṉpa. avar tamiḻārāyntatu kapāṭapuram eṉpa. avar tamiḻārāyntatu uttara maturaiyeṉpa, eṉṟē mukkaḻakap paṇiyumpaṟṟi mukkaḻaka varalāṟu kūṟutal kāṇka. ciṟaivāṉ ... uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāynta voṇṭīntamiḻ eṉṟē māṇikkavācakarum pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka.
ārāyccit takuti, matinuṭpam, paranta kalvi, naṭunilaimai, añcāmai, taṉṉalamiṉmai, meyyaṟi yavā eṉṉum āṟām. ivvāṟum illār pōliyārāycciyār, poruḷīṭṭavē vallār.
ulakat tamiḻārāycci niṟuvaṉamum ik kaḻakamum oṉṟākavē yirukkalām.
uraivēntar auvai cu. turaicāmip piḷḷai maturaiyilēyē yiruppatāl, aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu kaḻaka ārāycciyai mēṟpārttu varalām.
nāṭāṭcit talaivar (mutalamaiccar) pāṇṭiyaṉpōl eṉṟum niṟuvaṉat talaivarāyiruppār.
āṇṭutōṟum āṭṭaiviḻāvum araṅkēṟṟamum nikaḻalām. araṅkēṟṟamum paricaḷippum paṇṭai nāṭpōṉṟē naṭantēṟal vēṇṭum. kaḻakattil araṅkēṟiya nūlē nāṭṭil ulavutalum nilavutalum vēṇṭum.
celavu poruḷ varuvāykaḷ
tamiḻnāṭṭaracu otukkīṭu. naṭuvaṇaracu otukkīṭu, tiraviṭanāṭukaḷiṉ otukkīṭu, peruñcelvar naṉkoṭai, tamiḻnāṭṭut tirumaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ tiruttāṉam, tiruppati vēṅkaṭavar tiruvīkai, palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ paṇavutavi, oṉṟiya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṉ kalviyaṟiviyaṟ paṇpāṭṭuk kaḻaka (UNESCO) oppuravuttokai, palkalaikkaḻaka nalkaik kuḻu (U.G.C.) nalkai mutaliyaṉa kaḻakac celaviṟkup poruḷtēṭum vaḻivakaikaḷām.
kaḻakap peyar
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷum kaḻakam, kūṭal, tokai, avai, maṉṟam mutaliya coṟkaḷālēyē kuṟikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa. ak kālattil āriyam eṉṉum pērum tōṉṟavillai. caṅkam eṉṉum vaṭacol ki.pi. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē camaṇaroṭu tamiḻakam pukuntiruttal vēṇṭum.
patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa parañcōti muṉivar, tam tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattil, kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ kaḻakamō ṭamarntu eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatāl, iṉṟum kaḻakam eṉṉum collaiyē āḷalām.
vaṭamoḻiyiṟ caṅkam eṉṉum col samka (samga) eṉṉum vaṭivupeṟum. atai samgha eṉṟum tirittu samhaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripākak kūṟuvar. samka eṉṉuñ coṟku uṭaṉcellutal allatu oṉṟucērtal, kūṭutal eṉpatē poruḷ.
kummutal = kūṭutal, kuvital, kum - kummal (kuviyal) - L. cumulus, heap. um - kum - kumpu - kumpal.
kummaliṭu - L. cumulate.
kum - L. cum - E. cum, com.
L. cum (kum), Gk. sum (sum), Skt. sam (sam) = uṭaṉ, kūṭa. E. sym.
ē(ēku) - yā - i. jā. fh(ga#); E. go, Skt. gam, to go.
Skt. gam - ga, samga = to go together, to unite.
Samgha = combination, collection, assembly, association, society.
itaṉāl, caṅkam (samka) eṉṉum vaṭacollum teṉcolliṉ tiripē eṉṟu terintukoḷka. ākavē, kaḻakam eṉṉum collē irumaṭip poruttamākum.  (ce.ce.)
mantiram
mantiram1 - mantra (i.vē.).
muṉṉutal = karututal. muṉ - maṉ. maṉ + tiram (tiṟam) = mantiram. eṇṇattiṉ tiṇmaiyiṉāl. eṇṇiyavāṟu niṟaivēṟum eṇṇam allatu kūṟṟu allatu pāṭṭu mantiramām.
eṇṇiya eṇṇiyāṅ keytupa eṇṇiyār
tiṇṇiya rākap peṟiṉ.  (kuṟaḷ. 666)
mantiram = 1. eṇṇam (piṅ.).
2. cūḻviṉai 3. cūḻviṉaiñar (mantirimār) avai.
maṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉakku nāyēṉ mantirat tuḷḷēṉ  (kamparā. urukkāṭ. 31)
3. koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) mantira nūl.
pōkamiku mantiramā maṟaiyoṉṟu  (tirumuṟai kaṇ. 26)
4. cāvippuk kūṟṟu.  (va.va.)
mantiram - mantiri = aracaṉukkuc cūḻviṉait tuṇaiyā yiruppavaṉ.
mantirakkāraṉ = cāvippu mantiram paṇṇupavaṉ (mantiravāti).
mantiram - mantiri. mantirittal = (ce. kuṉṟāviṉai) 1. mantirattāl aṭakkutal.
3. mantirattāl ūḻkutal (tiyāṉittal).
mantirippār maṉattuḷāṉai  (tēvā. 590 : 4)
4. kūṭiccūḻtal.
makaṭpēci mantirittu  (tiv. nāyc. 6,3).
5. mantira maṉṟāṭṭuc ceytal.
cirattiṉañ cuṟṟapiṉṉai mīṇṭiṭa mantirippār  (pirapōta, 5,7)  (va.va.)
mantirap poruḷvayiṉ āakunavum  (tol. 932)
niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa.  (. 1434)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyamum,
niṟaimoḻi māntar perumai nilattu
maṟaimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum  (kuṟaḷ. 28)
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷuṅ kūṟuvatāl, tirumantiram pōṉṟa maṟai nūlkaḷ akkālat tiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varaippiṉ
nāṟpeya rellai yakattavar vaḻaṅkum 
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum (1336) atai valiyuṟuttum, vāymoḻi yeṉṟatu mantirattai. eṇṇattiṉ tiṇmaiyē allatu maṉṉum tiṟamē mantiramātalāl, maṉ eṉṉuñ collum mantirattaik kuṟikkum (piṅ.).  (va.va: 221-222: ti.ma. 751).
mayir
mayir : mai = karumai. mai - (may) - mayir = kariya muṭi. o. nō: ai - ayir, pai - payir, uy - uyir, cey - ceyir, ceyirttal = civattal, ciṉattal. mayir - macir - cmacru (va.).  (ti.ma.751).
mayil
mayil - mayūra
mai = karuniṟam. il = iṭam, kaṇ. mai - mayi + il = mayil = kariya allatu nīlap pīlik kaṇkaḷai yuṭaiyatu.
mayil tamiḻakak kuṟiñci nilap paṟavai; kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākiya murukaṉūrti.
vaṭavar aiyuṟṟuk kāṭṭum mūlam mā2 = oli (to sound).  (va.va. 222, 223).
marakkala vakai
puṇai - nīril mitakkum kaṭṭai.
teppam - palamitappuk kaṭṭaikaḷiṉ cērkkai.
kaṭṭumaram - irukaṭaiyum vaḷainta marakkaṭṭu.
tōṇi - tōṇṭappaṭṭa maram pōlvatu.
ōṭam - vēkamākac cellum taṭṭaiyāṉa tōṇi.
timil - tiraṇṭa mīṉ paṭaku (tiṇṭimilvaṉ paratavar puṟam. 24).
paḵṟi - paṉṟi pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa tōṇi.
parical - vaṭṭamāṉa piramput tōṇi.
ampi - vilaṅku mukam allatu paṟavai mukam pōṉṟa mukappaiyuṭaiya marattōṇi.
paṭaku - pāykaṭṭiya tōṇi.
nāvāy - nīraik koḻittuc cellum pōrk kalam.
kappal - palapāy kaṭṭi vaṇikac carakkēṟṟic cellum peruṅkalam.  (col. 44)
marakkuraṅku viḷaiyāṭṭu
(komparacaṉ kuḻaiyaracaṉ)
āṭṭiṉ peyar : ciṟuvar marattiṟ kuraṅkupōl ēṟi viḷaiyāṭum āṭṭu marak kuraṅku eṉpatām. itu pāṇṭi nāṭṭil komparacaṉ kuḻaiyaracaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkum.
āṭuvār tokai : iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭa palar itai āṭuvar.
āṭumuṟai : āṭuvārellārum oru marattarukē oru vaṭṭak kōṭṭukkuḷ niṉṟukoṇṭu ovvoruvaṉāy iṭak kālai maṭakkit tūkki, ataṉ kavaṭṭūṭu oru kallaiyāvatu kuccai yāvatu eṟivar. kuṟainta tolaivu pōkkiyavaṉ, piṟar maratti lēṟip piṭikkac coṉṉa piṉ avaraip piṭittal vēṇṭum. piṭikkiṟavaṉ marattilēṟumpōtu, cilar marattiṉiṉṟum kutittu vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ pōy niṉṟu koḷvar. cilar kiḷaikkuk kiḷaitāvi āṭṭaṅ kāṭṭuvar. vaṭṭattiṟkuṭ pōy niṟkumuṉ yārēṉum toṭappaṭṭuviṭṭāl, avaṉ aṭutta āṭṭaiyil piṟarai muṉ coṉṉavāṟu piṭittal vēṇṭum. marakkiḷaikaḷi lirukkumpōtu komparacaṉ kuḻaiyaracaṉ eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟuvar tammaik kūṟikkoḷvar.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : kuraṅkukaḷiṉ ceyaliṉiṉṟu iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : maramēṟap payilutal iv vāṭṭiṉ payaṉām.
marappaṭṭai vakai
paṭṭai puḷi vēmpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paṭṭai; ciṟāmpu teṉṉai paṉai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ paṭṭai; maṭṭai allatu taṭai vāḻai yiṉ paṭṭai. (paṭṭai eṉṉum peyar vāḻait taṭaikkum vaḻaṅkum).  (col. 71).
marapiyal
1. vaḻakkiyal vakai
coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivaruvatu vaḻakku.
atu, iyalpu vaḻakku, takuti vaḻakku eṉa irupāṟpaṭum.
iyalpu vaḻakku
1. ilakkaṇamuṭaiyatu,
2. ilakkaṇappōli,
3. marūu,
eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
ilakkaṇantavaṟāta collum coṟṟoṭarum ilakkaṇa muṭaiya (ilakkaṇa) vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. yāṉai, yāṉai malaiyil vāḻum.
ilakkaṇam ciṟitu tavaṟiyum ilakkaṇamuṭaiyatu pōla vaḻaṅkum collum coṟṟoṭarum,ilakkaṇap pōli vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. viciṟi - civiṟi (eḻuttumuṟai māṟṟu)
kōvil - kōyil (uṭampaṭumeym māṟṟu)
nakarppuṟam - puṟanakar (coṉmuṟai māṟṟu)
coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarum maruvi vaḻaṅkuvatu marūu vaḻakkām. maruvutalāvatu citaital allatu kuṟukutal.
e.ṭu. vāḻnaṉ - vāṇaṉ.
		tiruccirāppaḷḷi - tirucci.
takuti vaḻakku
1. iṭakkaraṭakkal, 2. maṅkalam, 3. kuḻūukkuṟi eṉa mūvakaippaṭum
avaiyil collattakāta iṭakkarāṉa poruḷai allatu karuttai aṭakki (aḵtāvatu maṟaittu)c colvatu, iṭak karaṭakkal vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. paṉṟiyoṭu cērnta kaṉṟum pavvī tiṉṉum.
amaṅkalamāṉa karuttai maṅkalac collālum coṟṟoṭa rālum kuṟippatu, maṅkala vaḻakkām.
e.ṭu. tuñciṉāṉ
		iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭi niḻalaṭaintāṉ
		iyaṟkai yeytiṉāṉ			   (cettāṉ)
		tirunāṭalaṅkarittāṉ
		perumpiṟitu (cāvu) uṟṟāṉ
piṟarkkup pulaṉākātavāṟu, oru kuḻuvār maṭṭum tamakkuḷ maṟaiporuḷavāka vaḻaṅkum kuṟikaḷ allatu coṟkaḷ, kuḻūuk kuṟiyām.
e.ṭu. karuntalai (1/4)
iru kuraṅkukkai (mucumucukkai)
kuṟippu : oru nāṭṭil oru pakutikkē ciṟappāyuriya ticai vaḻakkaiyum, uyarntōrallātār vaḻaṅkum iḻi vaḻakkaiyum, ayaṉmoḻi taḻuviya ayalvaḻakkaiyum, kaṭṭuraikaḷilum nūlkaḷilum vaḻaṅkutal kūṭātu.

paccaikkuṟavaṉ = perumpācāṅkuk kāraṉ.
paccaikkollaṉ = vēlaittiṟamaiyaṟṟa kollaṉ.
paccaippatam = kūlattiṉ muṟṟāpparuvam, naṉṟāy vēkāta nilai.
paccaippuṇ, pacumpuṇ = āṟiyirāta puṇ, putuppuṇ.
paccaippuḷukaṉ = perumpoyyaṉ.
paccaippū = pālkuṭikkiṟa kuḻantai.
paccaippoy = muḻuppoy.
paccaip pacumpoy = peru muḻuppoy.
paccaimaṇ = iḷaṅkuḻantai
paccaiveṭṭu = pakkuvam ceyyappaṭātanilai, paḻukkātanilai.
paccai veṭṭukkal = kūṭāta ceṅkal.
pacuṅkuṭi = yōkkiyamāṉa kuṭi, uḻavaṉ.
pacuntamiḻ, paintamiḻ = iṉiya tamiḻ.
pacumpai = vaṇikar tōḷil māṭṭik koḷḷum nīṇṭa pai.
paiṅkūḻ = iḷampayir.
paiñcēṟu = cāṇam.
poṉmai = polivu, aḻaku, viḷakkam.
poṟpa = polivupeṟa.
veṇmai = veṟumai, veḷippaṭai, aṟiviṉmai, kaḷḷa miṉmai, uḷḷīṭiṉmai.
veṇkaṭaṉ, veṇṇilaikkaṭaṉ = īṭukāṭṭātu vāṅkuṅ kaṭaṉ.
veṇkāval = veṟuṅkāval (Simple imprisonment).
veṇkai = toḻilceytu paḻakāta kai.
veṇcōṟu = veṟuñcōṟu.
veṇṭēr = kāṉal (Mirage).
veṇṇilam = veṟuntarai, maṇaltarai.
veṇṇōvu, veṇṇōkkāṭu = piḷḷaippēṟṟiṟkumuṉ uṇṭākum veṟu vētaṉai.
veṇpatam = iḷañcūṭṭuppatam.
veṇpāṭṭam = muṉpaṇamiṉṟi viṭuṅ kuttakai.
veṇpuḻukkal, veṇpuḻukku, veṇpuḻuṅkal = iḷampuḻukkal, iḷampuḻukkalarici.
veṇmaṭṭak karuttu = mēleḻunta vārittīrmāṉam (Superflcial judgement).
veṇmaṭṭam = mēleḻuntavāri, nuṇukkamiṉmai.
veṇmaṭṭavēlai = nuṇukkamillāta vēlai, mēleḻunta vāriyākac ceyyum vēlai.
veḷvīccu = āṭamparappēccu.
veḷveṭi = kuṇṭu illāta tōṭṭā.
veḷḷanti = kaḷḷaṅkavaṭaṟṟavaṉ.
veḷḷaṭi = cātāraṇam.
veḷḷaṭi veruṭṭu = peṭṭaimiraṭṭu.
veḷḷaveḷi = paraveḷi.
veḷḷaṟivu = avivēkam.
veḷḷiṭi = kōṭaiyil maḻaipeyyātu iṭikkum iṭi etir pārātu tiṭumeṉavarum tuṉpam.
veḷḷiṭai, veḷḷiṭaimalai = mikatteḷivāyiruppatu.
veḷḷitu = veḷippaṭaiyāṉatu.
veḷḷilai = veṟṟilai.
veḷḷuyir = tūya āṉmā.
veḷḷuruṭṭu = veṟṟaccuṟuttam.
veḷḷuḻavu = ciṟitu īramuḷḷa nilattil uḻum uḻavu.
veḷḷeḻuttu = eḻuttu viḷakkamāyt teriyāmaikkē tuvāṉa pārvaikkuṟai (Long sight).
veḷḷeṉa = atikālaiyil, iruṭṭumuṉ, kuṟitta kālattiṟku muṉpē.
veḷḷēṭu = veṟṟēṭu.
veḷḷēṟaṉ = paḻutāṉa irumpu (Scrap iron).
veḷḷai = kaḷḷamaṟṟavaṉ.
veḷḷaikkavi = poruṭceṟivillāta pāṭṭu.
veḷḷaikkōṭṭi = payaṉilpēccu, payaṉilapēcum aṟivilār kūṭṭam.
veḷḷaiccol = eḷiyacol, tiruntāccol.
veḷḷaittamiḻ = eḷiya naṭaittamiḻ.
veḷḷaittaṉam = kaḷḷamiṉmai.
veḷḷainōkku = kaḷḷamaṟṟa pārvai.
veḷḷaippuṇ = ūṉveḷuttu eḷitilāṟāta puṇ.
veḷḷaippūccu = tavaṟṟai maṟaikkai.
veḷḷaippēccu = veḷippaṭaiyāṉa pēccu, kaḷḷamaṟṟa pēccu.
veḷḷaipūcutal = tavaṟṟai maṟaittal.
veḷḷaimakaṉ = mūṭaṉ.
veḷḷaimati = aṟiyāmai, aṟivukkuṟaivu.
veḷḷaimaḻai = ciṟu maḻai.
veḷḷaimaṉam = kavaṭaṟṟa maṉam.
veḷḷokkal = kuṟṟamaṟṟa iṉam, celvamuḷḷa kiḷaiñar.
veḷḷōkkāḷam = umiḻnīrai maṭṭum veḷiyēṟṟum kumaṭṭal.
veḷḷōṭṭam = pututtērai mutaṉmutalāka ōṭṭip pārttal payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku muntiya nōṭṭam (parīṭcai).
veḷḷōlai = eḻutappaṭāta ōlai.
